Report 3 v1.2 update. Automatic offers: a visit with a preferred pet sitter is offered to that person first; otherwise one pet sitter at a time by rank, and pet sitters with the same base score at that place are offered together (GBR-12, JOB-02). An amount approved for a complaint is taken off an unpaid bill, also when the booking is Overdue, and is a refund after payment (GBR-30). Reasons of a returned order and of the carrier; start of the payment and pick-up deadlines of product orders (GBR-36). After two independent reviews: each service at home has a length of a visit, which sets the time window of each visit, and visit 2 of a day starts at or after the end of visit 1 (GBR-09, MSG148); a change of time is checked against the booking rules when the customer sends it, the rules that can change are checked again when Staff approve it, and a visit can be in only one waiting request (GBR-22, MSG149 to MSG151); a change request expires 6 hours before the old time or the new time, whichever comes first (GBR-19); a complaint with an amount stays open until the Admin decides (UC-53, MSG147); a booking ends when its last visit is finished or cancelled, and a customer can complain about the whole booking within 7 days after it ends, and about a visit closed by Staff from Booking Details (GBR-17, GBR-26); before the bill is made, the amounts paid back are limited by the expected bill (GBR-30); JOB-03 works out the bill again when the results of a booking on hold are final; the Sale Staff record the new goods of a faulty product exchange (UC-93); a counter sale takes the goods off the stock when it is completed (GBR-38); smaller corrections in Sections I to III.

Report 3 v1.2 update. A pet sitter application has five states: Pending, Under review, Interview, Approved and Rejected; the Admin approves or rejects it only at Interview, and one phone number, email or ID card number has at most one open application (GBR-25, UC-18). Definition and Acronyms 
Acronym
Definition
PWC
Paw World Care – the product name and project code
Paw World
The business that runs the pet shop and the home care service, employs the pet sitters and assigns every visit
SRS
Software Requirement Specification (this document)
UC-xx
Identifier of a use case (Section I.4.2)
BF-xx
Identifier of a main business flow (Section I.2)
FE-xx
Identifier of a major feature in Report 1
ERD
Entity Relationship Diagram
GBR-xx
Identifier of a business rule (Section V.1)
MSGxx
Identifier of a system message (Section V.2)
JOB-xx, API-xx
Identifiers of a background job and an external API (Background Job Inventory and External API Inventory at the end of Section III)
API
Application Programming Interface: the way PWC and an outside system call each other over the internet
SMS
Short Message Service: a text message sent to a mobile phone
UML
Unified Modeling Language, used for the business flow and use case diagrams
VietQR
The Vietnamese standard QR code for bank transfers, scanned with a banking app
OTP
One-time password: a short code sent by email or SMS that users type to sign in or to confirm a new email address or phone number
payOS
Payment gateway used by PWC; customers pay by scanning a VietQR code with their banking app
Guest
Visitor who is not signed in
Customer
Registered pet owner who books care for his or her dogs or cats, books grooming at the shop and buys products
Pet Sitter
Caregiver approved by Paw World who works under a full-time or part-time contract and does home visits, dog walks and, with grooming skills, bathing and grooming at the shop; use case names use the short form “Sitter”
Staff
Paw World coordinator who assigns by hand the visits nobody accepted, replaces pet sitters, approves booking changes and days off, checks pets in and out for grooming at the shop and handles cancellations, incidents and complaints
Sale Staff
Shop employee who prepares and ships online orders, hands over orders picked up at the shop, sells at the counter, records the goods suppliers deliver and sees the stock
Admin
Paw World manager who manages accounts, pet sitters and their contracts and shifts, services, prices, policies, products, suppliers and stock, closes pay statements and approves every amount paid back; can also do everything a Staff member and a Sale Staff member do
Booking
A customer's order for one service, covering one or more visits
Bill
What the customer pays for a booking after it ends: the visits done plus any late cancellation fees, minus any amount an Admin approved to take off for an incident or a complaint, paid online within 48 hours. It is not a tax invoice: Paw World's accounting makes tax invoices outside PWC
Refund
Money paid back to a customer: for an incident or a complaint, always approved by an Admin and at most the bill of the booking (taken off the bill if it is not paid yet); or for a prepaid product order that is cancelled or returned. Paw World's accounting pays refunds outside PWC
Earnings
What a pet sitter has earned in a pay period: the pay in his or her contract, a bonus for each finished visit or groomed pet that depends on its difficulty level, and the tips paid through PWC; Paw World pays it outside PWC
Pay Statement
The statement PWC makes for each pet sitter at the end of a pay period (a week for part-time, a month for full-time), which the pet sitter accepts or complains about within 3 days
Customer Tier
Member, Silver or Gold: a level PWC gives each customer from what he or she paid in the last 12 months; each tier has a discount
Difficulty Level
How hard a pet is to care for, worked out from the customer's answers about its behaviour and health; the pet sitter's bonus depends on it
Needs action
The list on Staff's visit board of visits the system could not handle alone, for example a visit nobody accepted
Offer
An invitation the system sends to one pet sitter to take a visit, with a time limit for the answer
Visit
One care session inside a booking, with its own time window, pet sitter, status and care report
Product Order
An order for products from the shop, placed online (delivered or picked up at the shop) or at the counter
Stock
For each product: the stock on hand, the quantity held by unfinished orders, and the quantity available (stock on hand minus the quantity held)
Goods Receipt
The record of one delivery from a supplier: for each product the quantity received, accepted and rejected with a reason
Grooming Appointment
A customer's booking for bathing or grooming one or more pets at the shop, paid when the pets are handed back
COD
Cash on delivery: the carrier collects the price of the order when it delivers the parcel
Care Instructions
Pet-specific instructions (feeding, behaviour, health notes) that the customer confirms for a visit; the pet sitter sees them with the home access notes of the address
Care Report
What the pet sitter records at a visit: check-in and check-out times, checklist, photos and notes
Handover
Transfer of a visit and its confirmed instructions to a replacement pet sitter
Incident
A problem that happens during a visit, for example a sick pet or a pet sitter who cannot get into the home
Complaint
A customer's complaint about the result of a booking or a visit, sent after the visit
Pending (application)
Status of a pet sitter application from the moment it is sent until an Admin approves or rejects it; the person has no account yet
Service Area
A group of wards or communes that an Admin sets up, where Paw World sends pet sitters and delivers orders; the travel surcharge and the shipping fee depend on it
Visit Board
Staff's page that shows the visits of the day, the visits waiting for a pet sitter and the Needs action list
Planning board
The part of the Visit Board where Staff give a visit to a pet sitter by hand, with the free pet sitters ranked
I. Overall Requirements
1. Context Diagram
Paw World Care (PWC) is a web application for one pet care business, Paw World, that sends its own approved pet sitters to look after dogs and cats in customers' homes. Customers book online and pay the bill after the booking ends; the system offers each visit to free pet sitters in ranked order, Staff assign by hand the visits nobody accepted and replace pet sitters when needed, pet sitters carry out the visits and report what they did, and Admins manage accounts, pet sitter applications, services, prices and every amount paid back to customers. PWC exchanges data with three outside systems: the payOS payment gateway (payment links, payment status and payment results), an email service (sign-in codes, application and account emails, bills and payment reminders) and an SMS gateway (sign-in codes). The system also runs scheduled jobs (Section III, Background Job Inventory) that offer visits to pet sitters, pass an unanswered offer to the next pet sitter, send visit and payment reminders and mark overdue payments. The context diagram below shows these actors and systems and the main information they exchange with PWC: a solid arrow is data sent to PWC, a dashed arrow is data PWC sends back.

2. Main Business Flow
PWC has seven main business flows: three for the care services and four for the shop. Each flow is shown on a page of its own as a swimlane diagram, with a lane for each actor and one for the system. A box is a step and a diamond is a question; the words on an arrow give the answer that leads that way.
2.1 Pet Sitter Onboarding (BF-01)
Trigger: A person fills in the application on “Become a pet sitter”, without an account.
End condition: An active pet sitter account is created and the pet sitter adds a profile and service areas, with weekly working hours (part-time) or the shifts the Admin entered (full-time); or the application is rejected and the reason is sent by email.

2.2 Booking, Visits and Payment (BF-02)
Trigger: Services, prices and service areas are set up by an Admin, and a customer wants pet care at home.
End condition: Every visit of the booking is done or cancelled and the bill is paid (or is 0 and the booking is closed), or an unpaid bill is handed to Paw World to collect outside PWC.

2.3 Incidents, Complaints and Refunds (BF-03)
Trigger: A pet sitter or a customer reports an incident at a visit, or a customer submits a complaint about a booking or a visit.
End condition: The result of the case is final (the customer agreed, did not answer within 72 hours, or the Admin made the final decision), any money the Admin approved (at most the bill) is taken off the bill or refunded, and the pet sitter is suspended if the Admin decided so.

2.4 Online Product Order (BF-04)
Trigger: A customer places an order for food or accessories on PWC, for delivery or for pick-up at the shop.
End condition: The order is delivered or handed over at the shop, or it is cancelled or returned: its hold has ended, the stock on hand has gone down only for goods that left the shop and up only by returned goods that can be sold again, and any refund is recorded for Paw World's accounting. A faulty or wrong product reported in time is exchanged, refunded or rejected.

2.5 Counter Sale (BF-05)
Trigger: A customer wants to buy products at the shop counter.
End condition: The goods are paid for and handed over and the stock on hand has gone down once, or the order is cancelled and its hold has ended, or the customer leaves without an order.

2.6 Goods Receipt (BF-06)
Trigger: A supplier delivers goods to the shop.
End condition: A goods receipt is saved, the stock on hand has gone up by the accepted quantity, and any rejected goods are reported to the Admin and returned to the supplier.

2.7 Shop Grooming Appointment (BF-07)
Trigger: A customer wants pets bathed or groomed at the shop.
End condition: The appointment is closed: each pet is ready and paid for, cancelled at no charge, or stopped; or the customer did not come; or a very risky pet is referred to the shop by phone.

3. Conceptual Data Model
3.1 Entity Relationship Diagram
The ERD uses the Crow's Foot notation; relationship labels are business verbs.

3.2 Entity Descriptions
One block per entity with its purpose and key business attributes only.
Entity: User
Field
Value
Purpose
Every person with an account: Customer, Pet Sitter, Staff, Sale Staff or Admin.
Key business attributes
Full name, email (confirmed by email), phone number (confirmed by SMS), role; no password. Customer only: one emergency contact (name, phone number, relationship) and the tier (Member, Silver or Gold), which JOB-05 updates every night (GBR-42).
Business identity
Email address and phone number, each unique
Status / lifecycle
Active ⇄ Locked (an Admin locks or unlocks a Customer, Staff or Sale Staff account, UC-10). A pet sitter account is created Active when an Admin approves an application (GBR-28); a pet sitter is suspended, not locked (GBR-04). A customer account exists once its email or its phone number is confirmed with a code (GBR-27).
Entity: Pet Sitter Application
Field
Value
Purpose
A request from a person who wants to become a pet sitter.
Key business attributes
Personal details, phone number and email, ID card number, service areas, work type (full-time or part-time), experience, documents; for the review: the four checks (valid ID card, at least 18 years old, portrait photo, experience with pets), call time, interview result (Passed, Not passed or Could not be reached), note, reason for rejection (UC-18). The applicant has no account yet (UC-16).
Business identity
Application code; one phone number, email or ID card number has at most one open application (Pending, Under review or Interview, GBR-25)
Status / lifecycle
Pending → Under review → Interview → Approved / Rejected (an Admin moves the application one step at a time and approves or rejects it only at Interview, UC-18)
Entity: Pet Sitter Profile
Field
Value
Purpose
Working information of an approved pet sitter.
Key business attributes
Photo, introduction, experience, service areas, contract type (the contract itself is in Contract), weekly working hours for a part-time pet sitter or shifts for a full-time one (see Shift), days off (see Day Off Request), average rating
Business identity
The pet sitter's user account
Status / lifecycle
Active / Suspended
Entity: Pet
Field
Value
Purpose
A dog or cat owned by a customer.
Key business attributes
Name, species, breed, weight, temperament, habits, feeding and care needs, vaccination status (last date; complete, not complete or unknown; optional photo of the vaccination book), photos
Business identity
Pet code (two pets of one owner may share a name)
Status / lifecycle
Active / Removed

Entity: Booking Policy
Field
Value
Purpose
The booking rules an Admin sets. A booking keeps a copy of the policy in force when it was made.

The shop settings on the same screen (refusal limit for cash on delivery, deadlines of orders, time to report a faulty product) are not part of the booking policy.
Business identity
Date it takes effect (one policy is in force at a time)

In force → Replaced

Status / lifecycle

Entity: Booking
Field
Value
Purpose
A customer's order for one service, with one or more visits.
Key business attributes
Customer, service, pets, copy of the address, dates and one or two visit times a day, copy of the length of a visit (services at home), total price, discount, late cancellation fees, bill amount, payment due date (48 hours after the bill is final), preferred pet sitter, policy snapshot
Business identity
Booking code
Status / lifecycle
Confirmed → Awaiting payment (bill final) → Paid; Closed when the bill is 0, for example when every visit was cancelled for free; On hold, only from Awaiting payment, while a complaint or an incident about it is open and until its result is final (GBR-26); Overdue when not paid in 48 hours, then Paid when the customer pays. A booking already Overdue stays Overdue while a complaint about it is open, and its due time does not move; an amount approved for that complaint is taken off the bill (GBR-30)
Entity: Visit
Field
Value
Purpose
One care session inside a booking.
Key business attributes
Booking, time window (from the start time for the length of a visit kept in the booking), price of the visit, assigned pet sitter, confirmed version of the care instructions, who cancelled it and the late cancellation fee if any
Business identity
Booking code and visit number
Status / lifecycle
Unassigned → Offered → Assigned → Arrived (check-in) → In progress (first save of the checklist) → Finished (check-out) → Report ready (care report sent); Cancelled (by the customer, or by Staff at no charge); Closed by Staff with a reason if not checked out 2 hours after its scheduled end: counts as done only with a check-in photo; otherwise Cancelled at no charge, with no earnings for that visit (GBR-24, GBR-31)
Entity: Booking Change Request
Field
Value
Purpose
A customer's request to move a booking or a visit to another time.
Key business attributes
Booking or visit, old time, new time, reason, Staff member who decided, expiry time (6 hours before the old time or the new time, whichever comes first)
Business identity
Request code
Status / lifecycle
Pending → Approved / Rejected / Expired / Closed (every visit of the request was cancelled)
Entity: Visit Offer
Field
Value

An invitation the system sends to one pet sitter to take a visit. Staff do not send offers: they give a visit to a pet sitter directly (UC-31).

Business identity

Status / lifecycle

3.3 Data Business Rules
Rules about data that are not obvious from the ERD.
Rule
Description
Enforced On
Unique email and phone number
No two accounts may share the same email address or phone number, whatever their role. A customer who also wants to work as a pet sitter applies with another email and phone number (GBR-05).
Sign-up and account creation
Account creation
Customers sign up themselves. A pet sitter applicant sends an application without an account; the pet sitter account is created when an Admin approves it, from the phone number and email in it (GBR-28). Staff and Sale Staff accounts are created by an Admin (UC-10). Admin accounts are created by the development team at deployment, with no screen to create them.
Creating any account
One pet sitter per visit
A visit has at most one assigned pet sitter at a time. If a pet sitter accepts an offer at the same moment as Staff assign the visit by hand, or two Staff members assign the same visit at the same moment, the first one saved keeps the visit; the later save is refused and that person is told it is taken.
Accepting an offer, assigning or reassigning a pet sitter
No overlapping visits
A pet sitter cannot hold two visits whose times, plus the travel time between them, overlap.
Assigning and accepting visits
Address copy
A booking keeps a copy of the service address it was made for; editing the saved address later does not change it.
Booking creation, editing an address
Served area
An address whose ward or commune is in no service area can be saved but is marked Not served and cannot be chosen for a booking (GBR-10).
Saving an address, booking creation
Price snapshot
A booking keeps the price, discount and policy that applied when it was made.
Booking creation and later price or policy changes
Confirmed instructions
A visit keeps the version of the care instructions the customer confirmed; a new version needs a new confirmation.
Editing instructions, check-in
Private access notes
Home access notes are shown only to the pet sitter assigned to the visit, from 1 hour before the visit until check-out; a replacement pet sitter sees them only after confirming the handover.
Viewing visit details
Sign-in with a code
There are no passwords. A sign-in code has 6 digits, is valid for 5 minutes and can be sent again after 60 seconds, at most 5 times an hour; 5 wrong codes block sign-in for 15 minutes. A session lasts 30 days for Customers and Pet Sitters and 12 hours for Staff, Sale Staff and Admins (GBR-08).
Sign-up, sign-in, changing the email or phone number
Pay after the visits
A booking is confirmed when it is placed; nothing is paid in advance.
Booking creation
Automatic offers
A visit is offered to one pet sitter at a time, in ranked order, or at once to all the pet sitters with the same score (GBR-12), with an answer time of 2 hours. When nobody is left, the visit goes to Staff's Needs action list. A visit that starts within 24 hours and still has no pet sitter also goes to Needs action, but the offers go on with an answer time of 30 minutes; the pet sitter or the Staff member saved first keeps the visit.
New or returned visits, answering an offer
Late cancellation fee
A visit cancelled less than 24 hours before it starts adds 30% of its price to the bill; an earlier cancellation is free.
Cancelling a booking or visit
Booking bill
When a booking ends, the bill is the visits done plus late cancellation fees minus any amount an Admin approved for an incident or a complaint; it is due 48 hours after it is final. While a complaint or an incident about the booking is open, and until its result is final (GBR-26), the bill is on hold and cannot become overdue; a booking already Overdue stays Overdue and keeps its due time (GBR-30). A bill of 0 closes the booking.
End of a booking, complaints
Change request expiry
A change request that Staff have not handled 6 hours before the old time or the new time, whichever comes first, expires, and the visits keep their old time.
Approving booking changes
No booking while overdue
A customer whose payment is overdue cannot create a new booking.
Booking creation
Booking length
A booking covers at most 30 days.
Booking creation
Payment recorded once
A payment is marked Paid only once, even if payOS sends the result again.
payOS payment webhook
One rating per visit
A visit can be rated once, within 7 days after it ends.
Rating a visit
Protected records
Pets, addresses and customer accounts used by bookings that are not finished cannot be removed or locked.
Removing a pet or address, locking an account
4. User Requirements
4.1 Actors
PWC has six human actors and three external systems. Work that runs on a schedule is done by background jobs (Section III, Background Job Inventory), not by an actor. The use case diagrams also use Registered User, an abstract actor for the use cases that Customer, Pet Sitter, Staff, Sale Staff and Admin share.
#
Actor
Description
1
Guest
Visitor who is not signed in. Views services, prices, service areas and policies and the products of the shop, registers as a customer and applies to become a pet sitter.
2
Customer
Pet owner with an account. Manages his or her profile, addresses and pets, books home care and grooming at the shop, confirms care instructions, sees the visits, the care reports and the dog's location during a walk, cancels, pays the bill after the visits, reports incidents, complains, and buys products online.
3
Pet Sitter
Caregiver approved by Paw World; the account is created when an Admin approves the application. Sets working hours, accepts or declines visits, reads the visit details and home access notes, confirms handovers, checks in and out, completes the checklist, reports incidents and sees his or her earnings.
4
Staff
Paw World coordinator. Assigns by hand the visits that the automatic offers could not fill, replaces pet sitters, follows all bookings and today's visits, cancels visits when no pet sitter is available or the pet sitter has not arrived 30 minutes after the start, follows unpaid bookings, handles incidents and complaints, proposes the amounts to pay back, and checks pets in and out for grooming at the shop and takes the payment.
5
Sale Staff
Shop employee. Prepares and ships online orders, records their delivery status, confirms returned orders, hands over orders picked up at the shop, sells at the counter, records the goods suppliers deliver and sees the stock.
6
Admin
Paw World manager. Manages user accounts (creates Staff and Sale Staff accounts, locks or unlocks Customer, Staff and Sale Staff accounts), interviews applicants by phone and approves or rejects their applications, suspends or reinstates pet sitters, manages pet sitter contracts, sets services, prices and booking policies, manages products and suppliers, approves every amount paid back to customers, and views pet sitter earnings, the stock and the dashboard. An Admin can also do everything a Staff member and a Sale Staff member do.
7
payOS Gateway
External payment system. Creates payment links with a VietQR code for booking bills, online product orders and payments at the shop, answers payment status queries and sends the payment result back to PWC.
8
Email Service
External email system. Sends sign-in codes and codes to confirm an email address, application receipt and result emails, account emails, and payment reminders to customers who have an email.
9
SMS Gateway
External SMS system. Sends sign-in codes and codes to confirm a phone number.
4.2 Use Cases (UC)
PWC has 95 use cases. Each use case belongs to one of the features FE-01 to FE-25 of Report 1. This list is the scope the team commits to in Report 3; the functions planned for later (Report 1, LI-09) have no use case here.
ID
Use Case
Feature
Use Case Description
UC-01
Register Account
FE-01 Registration and Authentication
A guest creates a customer account with a full name and either an email or a phone number, without a password, and confirms it with one one-time code (includes Verify OTP). After registering, the customer is signed in and lands on the Customer Dashboard; the other email or phone number can be added later with Change Email or Phone Number.
UC-02
Log In
FE-01 Registration and Authentication
A registered user enters a confirmed email or phone number and the code sent to it (includes Verify OTP) and reaches the dashboard of his or her role. The user stays signed in for 30 days (Customer, Pet Sitter) or 12 hours (Staff, Sale Staff, Admin). A locked Customer, Staff or Sale Staff account cannot sign in; a suspended pet sitter can still sign in but gets no new visits.
UC-03
Log Out
FE-01 Registration and Authentication
A signed-in user ends the session from the header and returns to the home page.
UC-04
Change Email or Phone Number
FE-01 Registration and Authentication
A signed-in user adds the email or phone number that the account does not have yet, or changes one, and confirms the new value with a code (includes Verify OTP). A user who has lost the only confirmed one contacts Paw World; PWC cannot restore it in this version.
UC-05
Verify OTP
FE-01 Registration and Authentication
The system sends a 6-digit code to the email or phone number and checks the code the user types. Included by Register Account, Log In and Change Email or Phone Number. A code is valid for 5 minutes and can be sent again after 60 seconds, at most 5 codes an hour; five wrong codes block that email or phone number for 15 minutes.
UC-06
View Home Page
FE-02 Public Service Information
A guest or customer sees the introduction of the Paw World shop (food, accessories, bathing and grooming) and its home care services, with links to the products, the services and booking.
UC-07
View Service List
FE-02 Public Service Information
A guest or customer sees the shop's bathing and grooming services and the home care services (home visits, dog walking and vet accompaniment) with their starting prices.
UC-08
View Service Details
FE-02 Public Service Information
A guest or customer sees what a service includes, its limits, the price table and surcharges.
UC-09
View Service Areas and Policies
FE-02 Public Service Information
A guest or customer sees the service areas (groups of wards and communes), the cancellation and refund policies and the responsibility policy: what Paw World covers, what it does not cover and what the customer must do.
UC-10
Manage User Accounts
FE-03 User Accounts and Customer Profiles
The Admin searches and filters the accounts of every role, opens an account to see its details, creates accounts for new Staff and Sale Staff members (name, email and phone number; they sign in with a one-time code), corrects the full name of a pet sitter, a Staff or a Sale Staff member, who cannot change it themselves, and locks or unlocks Customer, Staff and Sale Staff accounts with a reason. A customer account with bookings or orders that are not finished cannot be locked. Roles cannot be changed: a role comes from the way the account was created.
UC-11
Update Customer Profile
FE-03 User Accounts and Customer Profiles
The customer updates his or her full name and one emergency contact (name, phone number and relationship), and sees his or her tier (Member, Silver or Gold) and its discount. The email and the phone number are added or changed with Change Email or Phone Number.
UC-12
Manage Service Addresses
FE-03 User Accounts and Customer Profiles
The customer adds, edits and removes service addresses with home access notes. The customer picks the ward or commune, so the system knows the service area. Among pet sitters, only the pet sitter of a visit sees the access notes (View Home Access Notes); Staff see them only while handling that visit. An address outside every service area can be saved but is marked Not served and cannot be chosen for a booking. A booking keeps a copy of its address, so later edits do not change it. An address used by a booking that is not finished cannot be removed.
UC-13
View Pet List
FE-04 Pet Profiles
The customer sees his or her dogs and cats and removes a pet that is no longer cared for. A pet in a booking that is not finished cannot be removed.
UC-14
Add Pet Profile
FE-04 Pet Profiles
The customer adds a dog or a cat with its name (required), breed, weight, temperament, habits, feeding and care needs, vaccination status (last date; complete, not complete or unknown; an optional photo of the vaccination book) and photos. Pet sitters see the profile with each offer and visit; the vaccination status does not block a booking.
UC-15
Update Pet Profile
FE-04 Pet Profiles
The customer changes the details, care needs or photos of a pet. Visits that already have a pet sitter keep the confirmed instructions until the customer confirms the new version for them (see Confirm Care Instructions).
UC-16
Submit Sitter Application
FE-05 Pet Sitter Recruitment and Management
A guest who wants to become a pet sitter fills in the application without an account: full name, date of birth, phone number and email (both required), ID card number, service areas, work type (full-time or part-time), experience, ID card photos, a portrait photo and certificates if any. The system checks that the person is at least 18 years old and has no open application (Pending, Under review or Interview) with the same phone number, email or ID card, saves the application as Pending and emails a receipt with the application code.
UC-17
View Sitter Applications
FE-05 Pet Sitter Recruitment and Management
The Admin lists the applications by status (Pending, Under review, Interview, Approved or Rejected) and can filter them by service area and work type.
UC-18
Approve or Reject Sitter Application
FE-05 Pet Sitter Recruitment and Management
The Admin checks the application against a checklist (valid ID card, at least 18 years old, portrait photo, experience with pets) and calls the applicant for an interview. The application moves from Pending to Under review and then Interview; the Admin approves or rejects it only at Interview. Approval creates an active pet sitter account from the phone number and email of the application and emails a welcome message. Rejection needs a reason from a list (for example Could not be reached); the email gives the reason and the date from which the person can apply again (30 days later).
UC-19
Suspend or Reinstate Pet Sitter
FE-05 Pet Sitter Recruitment and Management
The Admin suspends an approved pet sitter with a reason, so the pet sitter gets no new visits and pending offers are withdrawn. A pet sitter in the middle of a visit finishes it first. Visits the pet sitter has already accepted are offered automatically to other pet sitters and flagged for handover. The Admin can reinstate the pet sitter later, with a note.
UC-20
View Sitter Earnings
FE-05 Pet Sitter Recruitment and Management
The pet sitter sees, for each booking or groomed pet, the work he or she finished with the visit pay (part-time), the difficulty bonus and the tips paid through PWC (shared equally among the pet sitters who did the work); the Admin sees the same for every pet sitter. The pay of each period is on the pay statement (Review Pay Statement). Paw World pays pet sitters outside the system.
UC-21
Manage Pet Sitter Contracts
FE-05 Pet Sitter Recruitment and Management
The Admin records the contract of each pet sitter: full-time (monthly pay, fixed shifts) or part-time (pay for each visit), with start and end dates. Paw World pays outside the system.
UC-22
Manage Services
FE-06 Service and Policy Management
The Admin creates, updates and deactivates home care services and shop grooming services, including the checklist of tasks and, for home care services, the length of a visit, which sets the time window of each visit (for a vet trip, the planned length). A deactivated service cannot be booked any more, but bookings already made keep their price and terms.
UC-23
Configure Service Prices
FE-06 Service and Policy Management
The Admin sets base prices, surcharges for pet size and extra pets, travel surcharges by service area, discounts for bookings with many visits (for example 5% from 7 visits, 10% from 20 visits) and the shipping fee of product orders by service area. A booking or an order keeps the prices that applied when it was made.
UC-24
Configure Booking Policies
FE-06 Service and Policy Management
The Admin sets the service areas (groups of wards and communes), the responsibility policy shown to customers, the minimum booking notice (24 hours), the longest booking (30 days), the free cancellation window (24 hours), the late cancellation fee (30% of the visit price), the payment deadline (48 hours), the refund rules, the travel time needed between two visits, the answer time for offers and how late a pet sitter may withdraw from a visit, and for the shop the refusal limit for cash on delivery, the payment deadline of prepaid orders, the pick-up deadline and the time to report a faulty product.
UC-25
Create Booking
FE-07 Service Booking
The customer chooses a service, the pets it is for, the address, the dates and one or two visit times a day, and the care needs, answers short questions about each pet's behaviour and health, sees the final price, including any discount for many visits and the tier discount, checks the care instructions filled in from the pet profiles, accepts the responsibility policy and confirms. The customer can ask for a preferred pet sitter who has cared for the pet before, who is offered the visits first; a pet sitter cannot be chosen by name. A booking must be made at least 24 hours ahead and can last at most 30 days; the second visit of a day starts at or after the end of the first. It is confirmed when it is placed and paid after the visits; its visits are offered to pet sitters automatically. A customer with an overdue payment cannot book; a customer with no paid bill yet can book at most 7 days and have only one unpaid booking; two bookings of one pet cannot overlap. Each visit time on each date becomes a separate visit.
UC-26
View My Bookings
FE-07 Service Booking
The customer lists his or her bookings by status.
UC-27
View Booking Details
FE-07 Service Booking
The customer opens a booking to see its terms, assigned pet sitters, payments and reports.
UC-28
View Visit Schedule
FE-08 Multiple-Visit Bookings
The customer sees all visits of a booking, each with its own time window, pet sitter and status.
UC-29
Set Working Hours
FE-09 Pet Sitter Availability
A part-time pet sitter keeps his or her weekly working hours; a full-time pet sitter sees the shifts of the contract (Manage Shift Schedule) and does not set hours. Every pet sitter chooses his or her service areas; an Admin can change the service areas of a pet sitter. If a change affects visits the pet sitter has already accepted, those visits are flagged to Staff.
UC-30
View Unassigned Visits
FE-10 Assignment and Conflict Checking
The Staff member sees each visit waiting for a pet sitter with the state of its automatic offers, ordered by start time, and a Needs action list: visits the automatic offers could not fill (nobody accepted), visits still without a pet sitter 24 hours before they start, visits whose pet sitter has not checked in 30 minutes after the start, withdrawals less than 12 hours before a visit, visits affected by a pet sitter's change of working hours, visits not checked out 2 hours after their end, handovers not confirmed 1 hour before the visit, booking change requests waiting for Staff, and the bookings of a customer with an overdue bill, so the Staff member can call the customer. A visit closed by the Staff member without check-out counts as done only if it has a check-in photo; otherwise it is cancelled at no charge and the pet sitter is not paid.
UC-31
Assign Sitter to Visit
FE-10 Assignment and Conflict Checking
For a visit in the Needs action list, the Staff member opens the planning board, sees the free pet sitters ranked with reasons (the same ranking as the automatic offers) and gives the visit to one by hand, after calling him or her if needed. A pet sitter with an overlapping visit cannot be chosen; outside working hours or outside the pet sitter's service areas the system shows a warning and the Staff member confirms. If a pet sitter accepts an automatic offer for the same visit, or another Staff member assigns it, at the same moment, the first one saved keeps the visit and the Staff member who saved later is told.
UC-32
View All Bookings
FE-10 Assignment and Conflict Checking
The Staff member searches all bookings by customer, date and status and opens a booking to see its visits, pet sitters and payments.
UC-33
Monitor Today's Visits
FE-10 Assignment and Conflict Checking
The Staff member sees all of today's visits with their live status: waiting for a pet sitter, assigned, arrived, in progress, finished. Visits where the pet sitter has not checked in 15 minutes after the start are shown in red and the Staff member calls the pet sitter; at 30 minutes the visit goes to Needs action, and the Staff member sends another pet sitter or cancels the visit at no charge. The customer's live card says that the pet sitter is late.
UC-34
View My Assignments
FE-11 Acceptance and Reassignment
The pet sitter sees the visits offered to him or her and the visits already accepted, with today's visits in the planned order (JOB-06).
UC-35
Accept or Decline Visit Offer
FE-11 Acceptance and Reassignment
The pet sitter accepts or declines an offered visit within the answer time (2 hours, or 30 minutes for a visit that starts within 24 hours); declining needs a reason. When an offer is declined or not answered and no other offer of the visit is still open, the visit is passed automatically to the next pet sitter on the list. A full-time pet sitter is offered only visits inside his or her shifts. If Staff assign the visit by hand at the same moment, or the visit is no longer free, the first one saved keeps it and the pet sitter is told the visit is taken.
UC-36
View Visit Details
FE-11 Acceptance and Reassignment
The pet sitter opens an offered or accepted visit to see the pet profile, the confirmed care instructions and the address. The home access notes are shown in the same screen under the rules of View Home Access Notes.
UC-37
View Home Access Notes
FE-11 Acceptance and Reassignment
The pet sitter of a visit sees the home access notes of its address (gate code, where the key is) from 1 hour before the visit until check-out. A replacement pet sitter sees them only after confirming the handover. Extends View Visit Details.
UC-38
Reassign Sitter
FE-11 Acceptance and Reassignment
The Staff member replaces the pet sitter of a visit. The customer and both pet sitters are notified, and the new pet sitter must confirm the handover.
UC-39
Cancel Booking or Visit
FE-12 Booking Changes and Cancellations
The customer cancels a booking or a single visit. A visit cancelled 24 hours or more before it starts costs nothing; a later cancellation adds a late cancellation fee (30% of that visit's price) to the bill. The Staff member cancels, without any charge, a visit that still has no pet sitter 12 hours before it or whose pet sitter has not arrived 30 minutes after the start.
UC-40
Confirm Care Instructions
FE-13 Care Instructions and Handover
The customer confirms the care instructions for each visit, and the visit keeps the confirmed version. Instructions can be changed until 2 hours before a visit; later changes apply from the next visit. If the customer changes the instructions after a pet sitter is assigned, the pet sitter is notified and must confirm reading the new version before checking in.
UC-41
Confirm Handover
FE-13 Care Instructions and Handover
A replacement pet sitter reads the care instructions and the visit history and confirms them before starting the visit.
UC-42
Check In
FE-14 Care Execution and Reporting
The pet sitter records arriving at the customer's home with a photo taken on the phone, from 30 minutes before the visit starts. Only the pet sitter assigned to the visit can check in, and a replacement only after confirming the handover. The customer sees that the pet sitter has arrived.
UC-43
Check Out
FE-14 Care Execution and Reporting
The pet sitter records leaving with a photo, after every required checklist item is done or skipped with a reason. The visit becomes Finished and the care report is sent to the customer. A visit not checked out 2 hours after its end goes to Staff, who close it with a reason; it counts as done only if it has a check-in photo.
UC-44
Complete Visit Checklist
FE-14 Care Execution and Reporting
The pet sitter ticks the care checklist (food, water, litter, play) and adds notes and at least one photo. If no photo can be uploaded, the pet sitter gives a reason instead and Staff see that the visit has no photo. The pet sitter cannot check out until every required item is ticked or has a reason for being skipped.
UC-45
View Care Report
FE-14 Care Execution and Reporting
The customer or Staff reads the report of a visit with the checklist and photos.
UC-46
View Dog Walk Location
FE-14 Care Execution and Reporting
During a dog walk, from check-in to check-out, the pet sitter's phone sends its location and the customer sees where the dog is on a map, live only: the route and past locations are not kept after check-out. Only for dog walks; home visits and vet trips show no location.
UC-47
Report Incident
FE-15 Incident Handling
A pet sitter or a customer reports a problem that happens during a visit, for example a sick pet or a pet sitter who cannot get into the home, with a description and photos. A customer who is not happy with the result of a visit afterwards submits a complaint instead (Submit Complaint).
UC-48
Resolve Incident
FE-15 Incident Handling
The Staff member records the follow-up actions and the result. Money to be paid back (for example vet costs, or the price of a visit that should not be charged because the pet sitter could not get in) is proposed by the Staff member with evidence, at most the bill of the booking, and approved by the Admin; it is taken off the bill if it is not paid yet, or refunded if it is. While an incident is open the bill of the booking waits.
UC-49
Pay for Booking
FE-16 Payment Processing
When a booking ends, the customer sees the bill (visits done, with the discount for many visits worked out again on the visits done and the tier discount, plus late cancellation fees, minus any amount the Admin approved for an incident or a complaint), may add a tip, and pays it online through payOS within 48 hours by scanning a VietQR code. A tip for a booking done by several pet sitters is shared equally among those who did the work. The bill is shown in the app and also emailed when the customer has an email. The system checks the payOS webhook and records the payment only once. While a complaint or an incident about the booking is open, and until its result is final (the customer has 72 hours to disagree), the bill waits. If a payment fails or payOS cannot create the payment link, the bill stays unpaid and the customer can try again.
UC-50
View Unpaid Bookings
FE-16 Payment Processing
The Staff member lists bookings whose payment is waiting or overdue, with the amount and the due date, to contact the customers.
UC-51
Process Refund
FE-17 Refund Processing
The Admin approves, lowers or rejects (with a reason) every amount proposed by Staff for a complaint or an incident, at most the bill of the booking. If the bill is not paid yet the amount is taken off it; if it is paid, the refund is recorded and Paw World's accounting pays it back outside the system. The tab Order refunds lists the refunds of product orders already paid: prepaid orders that were cancelled or returned, and faulty product claims refunded, cash on delivery included; Paw World's accounting pays them outside the system.
UC-52
Submit Complaint
FE-18 Ratings and Complaints
The customer files a complaint about a finished visit within 7 days after it ends (a visit closed by Staff from Booking Details, within 7 days after it was closed), or about a whole booking within 7 days after it ends (its last visit is finished or cancelled; when every visit was cancelled, only if its bill is above 0), and later sees his or her complaints with their status and result. The customer can disagree with the result once, within 3 days after receiving it, and the case then goes to the Admin. A problem during a visit is reported as an incident (Report Incident).
UC-53
Handle Complaint
FE-18 Ratings and Complaints
The Staff member follows a complaint until it is resolved and proposes any money to pay back, which the Admin approves (at most the bill); a complaint with an amount stays open until the Admin decides, and only then does the customer get the result. When the customer disagrees with the result, the Admin makes the final decision. While a complaint is open the bill of the booking waits.
UC-54
View Admin Dashboard
FE-20 Admin Dashboard
The Admin sees the numbers of today's visits and shop appointments, visits without a pet sitter, open incidents and complaints (those open more than 3 days shown apart), revenue and product sales.
UC-55
Browse Products
FE-21 Product Catalogue
A guest or customer browses food and accessories by category and pet type, searches them and sees the price and whether each product is in stock.
UC-56
View Product Details
FE-21 Product Catalogue
A guest or customer sees the photos, description, price (with the customer's tier discount) and stock state of one product and adds it to the cart.
UC-57
Manage Products
FE-21 Product Catalogue
The Admin creates and edits products (code, name, category, pet type, description, photos, selling price) and hides or shows them. A new product must be in the catalogue before its goods can be received.
UC-58
Book Grooming Appointment
FE-22 Shop Grooming Appointments
The customer books bathing or grooming at the shop: the pets, the services and a time slot, with short questions about each pet, and sees the final price for each pet before confirming. A very risky pet must be booked by calling the shop.
UC-59
Check In Pet at Shop
FE-22 Shop Grooming Appointments
The Staff member sees the day's shop appointments, records no-shows and checks each pet in with photos and its condition compared with the customer's answers. A listed surcharge needs the customer's consent, otherwise the pet is cancelled at no charge. The pet goes to a pet sitter with grooming skills on shift.
UC-60
Hand Back Pet and Take Payment
FE-22 Shop Grooming Appointments
The customer is told the pet is ready; the Staff member gives the pet back and takes the payment at the counter in cash or by VietQR, with a tip if the customer wants.
UC-61
Manage Shopping Cart
FE-23 Online Product Orders and Delivery
The customer adds, changes and removes products in the cart and sees the total with the tier discount.
UC-62
Place Product Order
FE-23 Online Product Orders and Delivery
The customer chooses delivery (shipping fee by service area) or pick-up at the shop, and pays in advance through payOS, in cash on delivery or at the shop on pick-up. Cash on delivery and payment at the shop are not offered to a customer over the refusal limit. Only the quantity available can be ordered, and placing the order holds it; a prepaid order not paid by the deadline is cancelled (JOB-04).
UC-63
View My Orders
FE-23 Online Product Orders and Delivery
The customer lists his or her product orders and opens one to see the items, the payment, the status and the carrier's tracking code.
UC-64
Prepare and Ship Order
FE-23 Online Product Orders and Delivery
The Sale Staff member prepares a delivery order, books the delivery on the carrier's site and enters the tracking code; when the parcel is handed to the carrier the hold ends and the stock on hand goes down.
UC-65
Update Delivery Status
FE-23 Online Product Orders and Delivery
The Sale Staff member checks the carrier's site and records the carrier's status: handed to the carrier, delivered (cash on delivery collected), or being returned with the reason. The stock does not change here; returned goods are counted only when the parcel is back at the shop (Confirm Returned Order).
UC-66
Confirm Returned Order
FE-23 Online Product Orders and Delivery
When a returned parcel is back at the shop, the Sale Staff member checks the goods and records the reason and the quantity that can be sold again; only that quantity goes back to stock. Each order can be confirmed returned only once, so the stock is never added twice. Goods that cannot be sold again are not added back. A refusal counts against the customer only when the customer caused it; a prepaid order is marked for a refund.
UC-67
Hand Over Pick-up Order
FE-23 Online Product Orders and Delivery
The Sale Staff member marks a pick-up order ready (the customer is told) and hands it over at the shop, taking the payment if it was not paid in advance; the hold ends and the stock on hand goes down. An order not collected by the deadline is cancelled (JOB-04).
UC-68
Sell at Counter
FE-24 Counter Sales
The Sale Staff member looks up products and the quantity available, creates a counter order (the customer's phone number gives the tier discount and counts the sale toward the tier), which holds the stock, and takes cash or a VietQR payment; the customer can pay again or change the way to pay before the order is cancelled. When the order is paid, the Sale Staff member hands over the goods and completes the sale, which ends the hold and takes the goods off the stock on hand; a cancelled one only ends the hold.
UC-69
Manage Suppliers
FE-25 Suppliers and Stock
The Admin adds, edits and deactivates suppliers (name, contact, products supplied).
UC-70
Record Goods Receipt
FE-25 Suppliers and Stock
The Sale Staff member records each delivery from a supplier: for each product the quantity received, accepted and rejected with a reason. A receipt is always recorded, even when everything is rejected; the stock goes up by the accepted quantity; rejected goods are reported to the Admin and returned to the supplier outside the system; a make-up delivery is a new receipt.
UC-71
View Stock
FE-25 Suppliers and Stock
The Sale Staff member or the Admin sees for each product the stock on hand, the quantity held by unfinished orders and the quantity available, the low-stock products, and the history of receipts and adjustments.
UC-72
View Sitter Profile
FE-05 Pet Sitter Recruitment and Management
The customer opens the profile of the pet sitter assigned to a visit: photo, introduction, experience, visits done and rating. Staff see the full profile.
UC-73
Update Sitter Profile
FE-05 Pet Sitter Recruitment and Management
The pet sitter updates his or her photo, introduction and experience.
UC-74
View Sitter List
FE-05 Pet Sitter Recruitment and Management
The Staff member lists active pet sitters with their contract type, service areas, working hours or shifts, days off, rating, and the number of withdrawals and late check-ins in the last 30 days.
UC-75
Review Pay Statement
FE-05 Pet Sitter Recruitment and Management
At the end of each pay period (part-time weekly, full-time monthly) the system makes the pet sitter's statement: contract pay or visit pay, difficulty bonuses and tips through PWC. The pet sitter checks it within 3 days and accepts it or complains with a reason; with no complaint the statement is closed after 3 days.
UC-76
Close Pay Statement
FE-05 Pet Sitter Recruitment and Management
The Admin answers each complaint about a statement by correcting it with a reason or explaining it; the system keeps the history and tells the pet sitter, then the statement is closed; there is no second review. The Admin pays outside the system and marks the statement paid; a mistake found after payment is adjusted in the next period.
UC-77
View Customer Dashboard
FE-07 Service Booking
The customer opens the dashboard after signing in and sees the next visits and shop appointments, bookings still waiting for a pet sitter, bills to pay and product orders in progress, with links to book a service, to the shop and to the pet list.
UC-78
Track Visit Status
FE-08 Multiple-Visit Bookings
The customer follows a visit as it happens: assigned, arrived, in progress, finished, report ready.
UC-79
Request Day Off
FE-09 Pet Sitter Availability
The pet sitter asks for days off and a Staff member approves or rejects the request. When the request is approved, the pet sitter is taken off the accepted visits on those days; they are offered again automatically with a handover note, and Staff are told. A day off that touches a visit starting within 12 hours cannot be asked; the pet sitter withdraws from that visit instead (Withdraw from Visit), and a withdrawal less than 12 hours before the visit goes to Staff.
UC-80
Manage Shift Schedule
FE-09 Pet Sitter Availability
The Admin enters the fixed shifts of full-time pet sitters (home care and shop) in a calendar. Full-time pet sitters are offered visits only inside their shifts.
UC-81
View Staff Dashboard
FE-10 Assignment and Conflict Checking
The Staff member opens the dashboard after signing in and sees the numbers of today's visits and shop appointments, visits in Needs action, late visits, open incidents and complaints (those open more than 3 days shown apart), and unpaid bookings, each linking to its list.
UC-82
Withdraw from Visit
FE-11 Acceptance and Reassignment
The pet sitter withdraws from an accepted visit with a reason, at least 12 hours before it starts. The visit is offered automatically to the next pet sitter and flagged for handover. A later withdrawal goes to Staff's Needs action list.
UC-83
View Pet Sitter Dashboard
FE-11 Acceptance and Reassignment
The pet sitter opens the dashboard after signing in and sees the offers waiting for an answer with the time left, today's visits in the planned order (JOB-06) and shop shifts, and the next visits, with links to working hours, days off, earnings and pay statements.
UC-84
Request Booking Change
FE-12 Booking Changes and Cancellations
The customer asks to move a booking or a single visit to another time. Only the date and time can change, so the price stays the same. The new time must be at least 24 hours away, and the request must be sent at least 12 hours before the old time. Each visit can be moved only once. The new time must also follow the booking rules (booking notice and length, the two visits of a day, no other booking of the pet at that time); the system checks them when the request is sent, and checks the two visits of a day and the pet's other bookings again when Staff approve it. A visit already in a waiting request cannot be in another one.
UC-85
Approve Booking Change
FE-12 Booking Changes and Cancellations
The Staff member approves or rejects a change request; before the visits move, the system checks again the two visits of a day and the pet's other bookings, and the affected assignments are updated. A request not handled 6 hours before the old time or the new time, whichever comes first, expires and the visits keep their old time; the system expires such requests (JOB-02).
UC-86
View Incidents
FE-15 Incident Handling
The Staff member lists open and resolved incidents.
UC-87
Rate Visit
FE-18 Ratings and Complaints
The customer rates a finished visit and leaves a comment. Each visit can be rated once, within 7 days after it ends.
UC-88
View Notifications
FE-19 Notifications
A signed-in user sees updates about bookings, offers, payments, pet sitter changes, incidents, product orders and shop appointments; the Admin also sees goods receipts with rejected goods.
UC-89
View Operational Reports
FE-20 Admin Dashboard
The Admin filters bookings, visits, payments and refunds by period.
UC-90
View Audit Log
FE-20 Admin Dashboard
The Admin checks who opened the home access notes of a visit and when; the notes themselves are not shown.
UC-91
Update Grooming Status
FE-22 Shop Grooming Appointments
The pet sitter on a shop shift sees the pets given to him or her, marks each one in progress or ready, stops a pet that is too dangerous to go on, and notes in the pet profile anything harder than declared.
UC-92
Report Faulty Product
FE-23 Online Product Orders and Delivery
The customer reports a faulty or wrong product from an order with photos, within the time set by the Admin. Products are not taken back when the customer changes his or her mind.
UC-93
Handle Faulty Product Claim
FE-23 Online Product Orders and Delivery
The Sale Staff member checks the claim and the Admin approves an exchange for the same product (the Sale Staff member marks the new goods sent, which takes them off the stock) or a refund paid outside the system. Faulty goods are not added back to stock, so no stock adjustment is made for them.
UC-94
View Sale Staff Dashboard
FE-23 Online Product Orders and Delivery
The Sale Staff member opens the dashboard after signing in and sees the orders to prepare, orders ready for pick-up, returned parcels, faulty product claims to check and low-stock products, each linking to its list.
UC-95
Adjust Stock
FE-25 Suppliers and Stock
The Admin increases or decreases the stock of a product with a required reason (damaged, lost, internal use, wrong receipt). Old records are never edited and the stock cannot go below zero; a decrease cannot be more than the quantity available. This is not a stock count.
4.3 Use Case Diagrams
The four use cases shared by Customer, Pet Sitter, Staff, Sale Staff and Admin (log in, log out, change email or phone number and view notifications) are drawn once for the abstract actor Registered User, which the five roles inherit. Admin inherits Staff and Sale Staff, so the Admin diagram shows only the 20 use cases that belong to Admin; an Admin can also use the 17 use cases of Staff (including the final decision on a complaint or an incident result the customer disagrees with), the 9 use cases of Sale Staff and the four use cases of Registered User. Verify OTP has no actor of its own: it is included by Register Account, Log In and Change Email or Phone Number and uses Email Service and SMS Gateway. The carrier and the suppliers are not actors, because PWC has no link with their systems.

² Only for a visit that Staff are handling: a visit in Needs action, a late visit or a handover.
³ Only the pet sitter assigned to the visit, from 1 hour before the visit until check-out; a replacement pet sitter only after confirming the handover.
⁴ Only the pet sitters assigned to the customer's own visits (photo, introduction, experience, visits done, rating).
⁵ Only the customer's own bookings, visits, orders and appointments; cancellation charges follow the policy.
⁶ Only a visit that still has no pet sitter 12 hours before it, or whose pet sitter has not arrived 30 minutes after the start; the customer is not charged.
⁷ Only visits offered to the pet sitter, within the answer time; a decline needs a reason.
⁸ Only during a dog walk of the customer's own dog, from check-in to check-out; no past locations are kept (GBR-41).
⁹ One open application (Pending, Under review or Interview) per phone number, email and ID card; a rejected person can apply again after 30 days.
¹⁰ Only the pet sitter's own earnings and pay statements: contract pay or visit pay, difficulty bonuses and tips paid through PWC.
¹¹ Only the pet sitter's own service areas, and own weekly working hours for a part-time pet sitter.
¹² A customer who has refused goods more often than the limit set by the Admin can only pay in advance (GBR-36).
¹³ Only the pet sitter's own shifts.
¹⁴ Only the pet sitter's own accepted visits: alone until 12 hours before the visit, later through Staff (GBR-21).
¹⁵ Only the pet sitter's own days off; a day off that touches a visit starting within 12 hours cannot be asked, and the pet sitter withdraws from that visit instead (UC-82, GBR-21).
¹⁶ Only the pet sitter's own statement, within 3 days after it is sent (GBR-44).
¹⁷ Only the customer's own bookings, at least 12 hours before the old time, once per visit (GBR-22).
¹⁸ Only the customer's own finished visits, once each, within 7 days after the visit ends (GBR-48).
¹⁹ An assigned replacement pet sitter may also view the care reports of earlier visits in the same booking when reviewing the handover (UC-41). This access is available before handover confirmation and ends if the replacement assignment is removed.
²⁰ Only as a guest, after signing out: a signed-in Staff member or pet sitter who opens the shop pages is taken back to his or her own pages.

5. System Functionalities
PWC has 82 screens: public pages for guests, account pages for every signed-in user, and the pages of each role, reached from that role's dashboard after sign-in (screen flow below). It works with three outside systems through five external APIs (5.2), and seven background jobs do the timed work: visit reminders, automatic offers with the Needs action checks, payment reminders, expired product orders, customer tiers, the daily visit order and late visit alerts (5.3). The APIs are numbered API-01 to API-05 and the jobs JOB-01 to JOB-07 in the order of the tables; the last two parts of Section III describe each of them.
5.1 Screen Inventory
a. Screen Flow
Guest Screen Flow

Customer Screen Flow – Pets and Bookings

Customer Screen Flow – Shop and Account

Pet Sitter Screen Flow

Staff Screen Flow

Sale Staff Screen Flow

b. Screen List
#
Screen Name
Feature
Description
1
Customer Registration
FE-01 Registration and Authentication
Guest registers a Customer account with a full name and either an email or a phone number, without a password, and confirms it on OTP Verification. After registering, the customer is signed in and sees the Registration Success step with a button to the Customer Dashboard; the other email or phone number can be added later (Change Email or Phone Number).
2
Login / Logout
FE-01 Registration and Authentication
All users sign in without a password: they enter a confirmed email or phone number and type the code on OTP Verification. Users stay signed in for 30 days (Customer, Pet Sitter) or 12 hours (Staff, Sale Staff, Admin); logging out ends the session. A locked Customer, Staff or Sale Staff account cannot sign in.
3
Change Email or Phone Number
FE-01 Registration and Authentication
A signed-in user adds the email or phone number the account does not have yet, or changes one, and confirms the new value on OTP Verification. A user who has lost the only confirmed one contacts Paw World; PWC cannot restore it in this version.
4
OTP Verification
FE-01 Registration and Authentication
Shared screen where the user types the 6-digit code sent to the email or phone number, used by Customer Registration, Login and Change Email or Phone Number: the code is valid 5 minutes, can be sent again after 60 seconds, at most 5 codes an hour; 5 wrong codes block that email or phone number for 15 minutes.
5
Home Page
FE-02 Public Service Information
Landing page introducing the Paw World shop (food, accessories, bathing and grooming) and its home care services, with links to the products, the services and booking.
6
Service List
FE-02 Public Service Information
List of the shop's bathing and grooming services and the home care services (home visits, dog walking and vet accompaniment) with starting prices.
7
Service Details
FE-02 Public Service Information
Included activities, service boundary, price table and surcharges of one service.
8
Service Areas and Policies
FE-02 Public Service Information
Service areas (groups of wards and communes set by the Admin), the cancellation and refund policies, and the responsibility policy: what Paw World covers, what it does not cover and what the customer must do.
9
Account Settings
FE-03 User Accounts and Customer Profiles
Any signed-in user sees his or her full name and role, and which email and phone number are confirmed, with links to add the missing one or change one (Change Email or Phone Number). The name is read only: a customer changes it on Customer Profile, and the Admin corrects it for a pet sitter, a Staff or a Sale Staff member.
10
User List
FE-03 User Accounts and Customer Profiles
Admin searches and filters the accounts of every role (Customer, Pet Sitter, Staff, Sale Staff, Admin).
11
User Details
FE-03 User Accounts and Customer Profiles
Admin views an account, creates Staff and Sale Staff accounts (they sign in with a one-time code), corrects the full name of a pet sitter, a Staff or a Sale Staff member, who cannot change it themselves, and locks or unlocks Customer, Staff and Sale Staff accounts with a reason. Roles cannot be changed.
12
Customer Profile
FE-03 User Accounts and Customer Profiles
Customer changes his or her full name and manages contact details and one emergency contact (name, phone number and relationship) and sees his or her tier (Member, Silver or Gold) and its discount.
13
Service Addresses and Home Access
FE-03 User Accounts and Customer Profiles
Customer adds, edits and removes service addresses with home access notes (shown only to the pet sitter of the visit, from 1 hour before the visit until check-out). The customer picks the ward or commune, so the system knows the service area; an address outside every service area can be saved but is marked Not served and cannot be booked. A booking keeps a copy of its address, so later edits do not change it; an address used by a booking that is not finished cannot be removed.
14
Pet List
FE-04 Pet Profiles
Customer views and manages his/her dogs and cats.
15
Pet Details
FE-04 Pet Profiles
Add/edit a dog or a cat: name (required), breed, weight, temperament, behaviour, feeding and care requirements, vaccination status (last date; complete, not complete or unknown; optional photo of the vaccination book) and photos. Pet sitters see it with each offer and visit.
16
Pet Sitter Application Form
FE-05 Pet Sitter Recruitment and Management
A guest who wants to become a pet sitter fills in the application without an account: full name, date of birth, phone number and email (both required), ID card number, service areas, work type (full-time or part-time), experience, ID card photos, a portrait photo and certificates if any (JPG, PNG or PDF, at most 5 MB each; at most 3 submissions an hour). One open application (Pending, Under review or Interview) per phone number, email and ID card; a receipt with the application code is emailed.
17
Pet Sitter Application List
FE-05 Pet Sitter Recruitment and Management
Admin views the applications by status (Pending, Under review, Interview, Approved, Rejected) and filters them by service area and work type.
18
Pet Sitter Application Review
FE-05 Pet Sitter Recruitment and Management
Admin checks an application against a checklist (valid ID card, at least 18 years old, portrait photo, experience with pets), calls the applicant for an interview and records the result, then approves or rejects it; the application moves from Pending to Under review and Interview, and the decision is made only at Interview. Approval creates an active pet sitter account from the phone number and email of the application and emails a welcome message; rejection needs a reason from a list, and the email gives the reason and the date from which the person can apply again (30 days). Admin can also suspend an approved pet sitter and reinstate him or her later.
19
Pet Sitter Earnings
FE-05 Pet Sitter Recruitment and Management
Pet Sitter sees, for each booking, the visits he or she finished with the visit pay (part-time), the difficulty bonus and the tips paid through PWC (shared equally when several pet sitters did the work); Admin sees the same for every pet sitter. Pay for each period is on Pay Period Statement.
20
Pet Sitter Profile
FE-05 Pet Sitter Recruitment and Management
Pet Sitter updates photo, introduction and experience; Customer sees the profile of the pet sitter assigned to a visit (experience, visits done, rating); Staff and Admin see the full profile.
21
Pet Sitter List
FE-05 Pet Sitter Recruitment and Management
Staff lists active pet sitters with their contract type, service areas, working hours or shifts, days off, rating, and the number of withdrawals and late check-ins in the last 30 days.
22
Pet Sitter Contracts
FE-05 Pet Sitter Recruitment and Management
Admin records the contract of each pet sitter: full-time (monthly pay) or part-time (pay for each visit), with start and end dates. Paw World pays outside PWC.
23
Pay Period Statement
FE-05 Pet Sitter Recruitment and Management
At the end of each pay period (part-time weekly, full-time monthly) the system makes each pet sitter's statement: contract pay or visit pay, difficulty bonuses (fixed values in the system until the settings screen is built) and tips through PWC. The pet sitter checks it within 3 days and may complain; the Admin corrects it with a reason or explains, then the statement is closed. The Admin pays outside PWC and marks it paid; a mistake found after payment is adjusted in the next period.
24
Service Management
FE-06 Service and Policy Management
Admin creates, updates and deactivates home care and shop grooming services, with the checklist of tasks of each service and, for home care services, the length of a visit (for a vet trip, the planned length), which sets the time window of each visit.
25
Service Price Configuration
FE-06 Service and Policy Management
Admin configures base prices, pet-size/extra-pet surcharges, travel surcharges by service area, discounts for bookings with many visits (e.g., 7+ visits −5%, 20+ visits −10%) and the shipping fee of product orders by service area. A booking or an order keeps the prices that applied when it was made.
26
System Settings
FE-06 Service and Policy Management
Admin configures the service areas (groups of wards and communes), the responsibility policy shown to customers, the minimum booking notice (24 h), the late cancellation fee (30% of the visit price), the payment deadline (48 h), the longest booking (30 days), the free cancellation window (24 h), refund rules, the travel buffer between visits, the answer time for offers (2 h; 30 min for a visit within 24 h) and the time limits for withdrawals and changes. Shop settings: the refusal limit for cash on delivery (refused parcels and pick-up orders not collected), the payment deadline of prepaid orders, the pick-up deadline and the time to report a faulty product.
27
Create Booking
FE-07 Service Booking
Customer selects service, the pets it is for, address, dates with one or two visit times a day, and care requirements, answers short questions about each pet's behaviour and health, and may ask for a preferred pet sitter who cared for the pet before; the final price with any discount is shown before confirming and is paid after the visits, and the customer accepts the responsibility policy. The booking is confirmed when it is placed and its visits are offered to pet sitters automatically. Blocked for a customer with an overdue payment; a booking starts at least 24 hours ahead and lasts at most 30 days; the second visit of a day starts at or after the end of the first; a customer with no paid bill yet may book at most 7 days and have one unpaid booking; two bookings of one pet cannot overlap.
28
Booking List
FE-07 Service Booking
Customer lists his or her bookings by status; Staff list all bookings and search them by customer, date and status.
29
Booking Details
FE-07 Service Booking
Customer and Staff view booking terms, visits, assigned pet sitters, payments and reports.
30
Customer Dashboard
FE-07 Service Booking
Dashboard of a signed-in Customer: next visits and shop appointments, bookings waiting for a pet sitter, bills to pay, product orders in progress, and links to book a service, to the shop and to the pet list. Numbers and links only, no charts.
31
Visit Schedule
FE-08 Multiple-Visit Bookings
Generate and show the visits of a multi-day booking, each with its own time window and status.
32
Visit Status Tracker
FE-08 Multiple-Visit Bookings
Live visit card: customer follows each visit as it happens (Assigned, Arrived, In Progress, Finished, Report Ready).
33
Working Hours Calendar
FE-09 Pet Sitter Availability
Part-time pet sitters set their weekly working hours; full-time pet sitters see the shifts of their contract (Shift Schedule). Every pet sitter chooses his or her service areas, which the Admin can change.
34
Day Off Request
FE-09 Pet Sitter Availability
Pet Sitter asks for days off and Staff approve or reject them; when Staff approve, the pet sitter is taken off the accepted visits on those days, which are offered again automatically with a handover note, and Staff are told. A day off that touches a visit starting within 12 hours cannot be asked; the pet sitter withdraws from that visit instead, and a withdrawal less than 12 hours before the visit goes to Staff.
35
Shift Schedule
FE-09 Pet Sitter Availability
Admin enters the fixed shifts of full-time pet sitters (home care and shop) in a calendar; full-time pet sitters are offered visits only inside their shifts and do not set their own hours.
36
Visit Board
FE-10 Assignment and Conflict Checking
Staff sees each visit with the state of its automatic offers and a Needs action list (visits the automatic offers could not fill, visits still without a pet sitter 24 hours before they start, visits whose pet sitter has not checked in 30 minutes after the start, withdrawals less than 12 hours before a visit, visits hit by a change of working hours, visits not checked out 2 hours after their end, handovers not confirmed 1 hour before the visit, booking change requests waiting for Staff), and all of today's visits with their live status (late visits in red after 15 minutes). Staff can close a visit the pet sitter forgot to check out, with a reason. A visit closed without check-out counts as done only if it has a check-in photo; otherwise it is cancelled at no charge and the pet sitter is not paid. Bookings of a customer with an overdue bill also appear in Needs action, so Staff can call the customer.
37
Assign Pet Sitter
FE-10 Assignment and Conflict Checking
Planning board for the visits in Needs action: Staff gives a visit to a pet sitter by hand, using the same ranked list with reasons as the automatic offers. A pet sitter with an overlapping visit cannot be chosen; outside working hours or outside the pet sitter's service areas the system warns and Staff confirm after calling the pet sitter.
38
Staff Dashboard
FE-10 Assignment and Conflict Checking
Dashboard of a signed-in Staff member: numbers of today's visits, visits in Needs action, late visits, open incidents and complaints, and unpaid bookings, each linking to its list. Numbers only, no charts. Incidents and complaints open more than 3 days are shown apart.
39
My Assignments
FE-11 Acceptance and Reassignment
Pet Sitter views offered visits with their answer deadline and accepted visits, and accepts or declines an offer. Declining an offer needs a reason.
40
Reassign Pet Sitter
FE-11 Acceptance and Reassignment
Staff replaces the pet sitter of a visit; the customer and both pet sitters are notified.
41
Visit Details (Pet Sitter)
FE-11 Acceptance and Reassignment
Pet Sitter opens an offered or accepted visit: pet profile, confirmed care instructions and address; the home access notes are shown to the pet sitter of the visit from 1 hour before the visit until check-out, and to a replacement pet sitter only after the handover is confirmed.
42
Withdraw from Visit
FE-11 Acceptance and Reassignment
Pet Sitter withdraws from an accepted visit with a reason. Up to 12 hours before the visit, the visit is offered automatically to the next pet sitter and flagged for handover; later, the request goes to Staff, who replace the pet sitter by hand or cancel the visit at no charge.
43
Pet Sitter Dashboard
FE-11 Acceptance and Reassignment
Dashboard of a signed-in Pet Sitter: offers waiting for an answer with the time left, today's visits in the planned order and shop shifts, next visits, and links to working hours, days off and earnings. Numbers and links only, no charts.
44
Request Booking Change
FE-12 Booking Changes and Cancellations
Customer asks to move a booking or a visit to a new time (at least 24 hours away, sent at least 12 hours before the old time); the new time also follows the booking rules (booking notice and length, the two visits of a day, no other booking of the pet at that time), checked when the request is sent; the two visits of a day and the pet's other bookings are checked again when Staff approve it. A visit already in a waiting request cannot be in another one. Staff open the request from the Needs action list or from Booking Details and approve or reject it; affected assignments are updated.
45
Cancel Booking / Visit
FE-12 Booking Changes and Cancellations
Customer cancels a booking or single visit: free 24 hours or more before the visit, later a late cancellation fee (30% of that visit's price) is added to the bill. Staff cancels a visit at no charge when no pet sitter is found 12 hours before it or the pet sitter has not arrived 30 minutes after the start.
46
Visit Care Instructions
FE-13 Care Instructions and Handover
Customer confirms instructions per visit; each visit keeps its confirmed version.
47
Handover Confirmation
FE-13 Care Instructions and Handover
Replacement pet sitter reads the confirmed instructions, the handover notes and the reports of earlier visits and confirms them before the visit; the home access notes and check-in open after the confirmation.
48
Visit Check-in / Check-out
FE-14 Care Execution and Reporting
Pet Sitter records arrival and departure times of a visit with a photo taken on the phone; check-in is allowed from 30 minutes before the visit start.
49
Visit Checklist and Photos
FE-14 Care Execution and Reporting
Pet Sitter completes the checklist (food, water, litter, play), uploads at least one photo and notes; a photo that cannot be uploaded is a skipped item with a reason.
50
Care Report
FE-14 Care Execution and Reporting
Customer and Staff view the submitted report of each visit.
51
Dog Walk Live Location
FE-14 Care Execution and Reporting
