During a dog walk, from check-in to check-out, the pet sitter's phone sends its location and the customer sees where the dog is on a map, live only: the route is not kept after check-out. Only for dog walks; home visits and vet trips show no location.
52
Report Incident
FE-15 Incident Handling
Pet Sitter or Customer reports an incident with description and photos.
53
Incident List
FE-15 Incident Handling
Staff lists open and resolved incidents.
54
Incident Details and Resolution
FE-15 Incident Handling
Staff records follow-up actions and the resolution and decides whether the visit is charged; money to be paid back (e.g., vet costs) is proposed by Staff with evidence, at most the bill of the booking, and approved by Admin; it is taken off the bill if it is not paid yet, or refunded. The bill waits while the incident is open.
55
Booking Payment
FE-16 Payment Processing
When a booking ends the system works out the bill (visits done, with the discount for many visits worked out again on the visits done and the customer's tier discount, + late cancellation fees − amounts approved by Admin for incidents or complaints); the customer sees the bill in the app (and by email when he or she has an email), may add a tip, pays through payOS within 48 hours and sees the payment result; a bill of 0 closes the booking; the bill waits while a complaint or an incident is open.
56
Unpaid Bookings
FE-16 Payment Processing
Staff lists bookings whose payment is waiting or overdue, with the amount and due date, to contact the customers.
57
Refund Processing
FE-17 Refund Processing
Admin approves, lowers or rejects (with a reason) every amount proposed by Staff for a complaint or an incident, at most the bill of the booking: it is taken off the bill if it is not paid yet, or recorded as a refund that Paw World's accounting pays back outside the system. The tab Order refunds lists the refunds of product orders already paid: prepaid orders that were cancelled or returned, and faulty product claims refunded, cash on delivery included; they are paid outside the system.
58
Rate Visit
FE-18 Ratings and Complaints
Customer rates a completed visit and leaves a comment.
59
Submit Complaint
FE-18 Ratings and Complaints
Customer files a complaint about a finished visit within 7 days after it ends (a visit closed by Staff from Booking Details, within 7 days after it was closed), or about a whole booking within 7 days after it ends (its last visit is finished or cancelled; when every visit was cancelled, only if its bill is above 0), later sees his or her complaints with their status and result, and can disagree with the result once.
60
Complaint Management
FE-18 Ratings and Complaints
Staff tracks each complaint to resolution and proposes any money to pay back, which Admin approves (at most the bill); a complaint with an amount stays open until the Admin decides, then the customer gets the result; a complaint the customer disagrees with goes to Admin for the final decision.
61
Notification Center
FE-19 Notifications
Users view booking, offer, payment, pet sitter change, incident, product order and shop appointment notifications; the Admin also sees goods receipts with rejected goods.
62
Admin Dashboard
FE-20 Admin Dashboard
Numbers of today's visits and shop appointments, visits without a pet sitter, open incidents and complaints (those open more than 3 days shown apart), revenue and product sales (no charts).
63
Operational and Financial Reports
FE-20 Admin Dashboard
Admin filters bookings, visits, payments and refunds by period.
64
Audit Log
FE-20 Admin Dashboard
Admin views important user actions (who opened the home access notes of a visit and when; the notes themselves are not shown).
65
Product List
FE-21 Product Catalogue
Guests and customers browse food and accessories by category and pet type, search them, and see the price and whether each product is in stock (on hand minus what unfinished orders hold).
66
Product Details
FE-21 Product Catalogue
Photos, description, price (with the customer's tier discount) and stock state of one product, with a button to add it to the cart.
67
Product Management
FE-21 Product Catalogue
Admin creates and edits products (code, name, category, pet type, description, photos, selling price) and hides or shows them. A new product must be in the catalogue before its goods can be received.
68
Grooming Booking
FE-22 Shop Grooming Appointments
Customer books bathing or grooming at the shop: pets, services and time slot, with short questions about each pet; the final price for each pet is shown before confirming. A very risky pet must be booked by calling the shop.
69
Grooming Appointments
FE-22 Shop Grooming Appointments
Staff see the day's shop appointments, record no-shows, and check each pet in with photos and its condition compared with the customer's answers; a listed surcharge needs the customer's consent (or the pet is cancelled at no charge); the pet goes to a pet sitter with grooming skills on shift.
70
Grooming Work
FE-22 Shop Grooming Appointments
Pet sitter on a shop shift sees the pets given to him or her, marks each one in progress or ready, stops a pet that is too dangerous to go on, and notes in the pet profile anything harder than declared.
71
Grooming Pick-up and Payment
FE-22 Shop Grooming Appointments
The customer is told the pet is ready; Staff give the pet back and take the payment at the counter (cash or VietQR), with a tip if the customer wants.
72
Shopping Cart
FE-23 Online Product Orders and Delivery
Customer adds, changes and removes products in the cart and sees the total with the tier discount.
73
Product Checkout
FE-23 Online Product Orders and Delivery
Customer chooses delivery (shipping fee by service area) or pick-up at the shop, and pays in advance through payOS, in cash on delivery or at the shop on pick-up. Cash on delivery and payment at the shop are not offered to a customer over the refusal limit. Only the quantity available can be ordered, and placing the order holds it; a prepaid order not paid by the deadline is cancelled.
74
My Orders
FE-23 Online Product Orders and Delivery
Customer lists his or her product orders and opens one: items, payment, status and the carrier's tracking code.
75
Order Management
FE-23 Online Product Orders and Delivery
Sale Staff prepare each order, book the delivery on the carrier's site and enter the tracking code, and update the status: handed to the carrier, delivered (cash on delivery collected), or returned with the reason and the quantity that can be sold again. Pick-up orders are marked ready (the customer is told) and handed over at the shop, taking the payment if it was not paid in advance. Stock goes down when the goods leave the shop; prepaid orders that fail are marked for a refund.
76
Faulty Product Claims
FE-23 Online Product Orders and Delivery
Customer reports a faulty or wrong product with photos within the time set by the Admin; Sale Staff check it and the Admin approves an exchange for the same product (Sale Staff mark the new goods sent, which takes them off the stock) or a refund paid outside PWC. Faulty goods do not go back to stock. Products are not taken back when the customer changes his or her mind.
77
Sale Staff Dashboard
FE-23 Online Product Orders and Delivery
Dashboard of a signed-in Sale Staff member: orders to prepare, orders ready for pick-up, parcels returned, faulty product claims to check and low-stock products, each linking to its list. Numbers only, no charts.
78
Counter Sale
FE-24 Counter Sales
Sale Staff look up products and the quantity available, create a counter order (the customer's phone number gives the tier discount), which holds the stock, and take cash or a VietQR payment; the customer can pay again or change the way to pay before the order is cancelled. When the order is paid, Sale Staff hand over the goods and complete the sale, which takes the goods off the stock; a cancelled one releases them.
79
Supplier Management
FE-25 Suppliers and Stock
Admin adds, edits and deactivates suppliers (name, contact, products supplied).
80
Goods Receipt
FE-25 Suppliers and Stock
Sale Staff (or Admin) record each delivery from a supplier: for each product the quantity received, accepted and rejected with a reason. A receipt is always recorded, even when everything is rejected; stock goes up by the accepted quantity; rejected goods are reported to the Admin and returned to the supplier outside PWC; a later make-up delivery is a new receipt.
81
Stock List
FE-25 Suppliers and Stock
Sale Staff and Admin see for each product the stock on hand, the quantity held by unfinished orders and the quantity available, low-stock products, and the history of receipts and adjustments.
82
Stock Adjustment
FE-25 Suppliers and Stock
Admin increases or decreases the stock of a product with a required reason (damaged, lost, internal use, wrong receipt). Old records are never edited and stock cannot go below zero; a decrease cannot be more than the quantity available. This is not a stock count.
5.2 External API Inventory
#
API Name
Calling Path
Description
1
payOS – create payment link
PWC → payOS
Creates a payment link with a VietQR code for the bill of a booking or a prepaid product order and returns the checkout page address; also creates the VietQR code for a payment at the counter.
2
payOS – get payment status
PWC → payOS
Reads the status of a payment before a booking is marked overdue or a prepaid product order is cancelled.
3
payOS – payment webhook
payOS → PWC
Sends the result of a payment for a booking bill, a product order or a counter payment. PWC checks the signature with the checksum key and records the payment only once.
4
Email service – send email
PWC → Email service
Sends sign-in codes, codes to confirm an email address, account emails such as a new Staff or Sale Staff account, the receipt and the result of a pet sitter application, bills and payment reminders.
5
SMS gateway – send SMS
PWC → SMS gateway
Sends sign-in codes and codes to confirm a phone number.
5.3 Background Job Inventory
#
Job Name
Group
Description
1
Send visit reminders
Scheduled
Every 15 minutes: sends one in-app reminder to the pet sitter and the customer of each visit that starts within 2 hours.
2
Offer visits and flag visits for Staff
Event and scheduled
When a visit is created or sent back: ranks the free pet sitters and offers the visit to the first one, or at once to all with the same score. Every 5 minutes: closes offers whose answer time has passed and, when no offer of the visit is still open, offers it to the next pet sitter, or puts it in Staff's Needs action list when nobody is left; puts in Needs action the visits that start within 24 hours without a pet sitter (the offers go on), the visits whose pet sitter has not checked in 30 minutes after the start, the visits not checked out 2 hours after their end and the handovers not confirmed 1 hour before the visit; expires booking change requests not handled 6 hours before the old time or the new time, whichever comes first.
3
Send payment reminders and mark overdue
Scheduled
Every hour: reminds customers 24 hours before the payment is due (in the app, and by email when the customer has an email), and marks a booking overdue after the due date once payOS confirms that it is not paid. Bookings On hold are skipped (a complaint or an incident is open, or its result is not final yet, GBR-26); when all their results are final, the bill is worked out again once: nothing to pay closes the booking, otherwise it is due 48 hours after the results became final and the customer is told; if payOS cannot be reached, nothing is marked and the job tries again the next hour.
4
Cancel expired product orders
Scheduled
Every 5 minutes: cancels the prepaid orders not paid by the payment deadline and the pick-up orders not collected by the pick-up deadline, ends their hold and tells the customer. A pick-up order not collected counts as a refusal; a prepaid order not paid in time does not.
5
Update customer tiers
Scheduled
Every night: sets each customer's tier (Member, Silver or Gold) from what he or she paid in the last 12 months for services and products, counter sales with the customer's phone number or email included.
6
Plan the daily visit order
Event
When the visits of a pet sitter on a day change: puts them in an order that keeps travel between homes short, shown in My Assignments and on the Pet Sitter Dashboard; a failed run is tried again 5 minutes later. PWC gives no turn-by-turn directions.
7
Alert Staff to late visits
Scheduled
Every 5 minutes: tells Staff in the app about each visit whose pet sitter has not checked in 15 minutes after the start, at most 5 minutes after that mark.
II. Use Case Specifications
This section specifies the use cases in the main business flows and the complex use cases, grouped by feature. Simple create, view and update use cases are described with their screens in Section III.
1. Registration and Authentication (FE-01)
1.1 Register Account (UC-01)
Primary Actors
Guest
Secondary Actors
Email Service, SMS Gateway
Description
As a pet owner, I want to create an account with my email or my phone number, without a password, so that I can book care for my pets.
The account is a Customer account. A person who wants to work as a pet sitter applies instead (UC-16).
Preconditions
The person is not signed in.
Normal Flow
Register a customer account
Guest clicks Register on the home page or on Login
System shows Customer Registration
Guest types the full name, chooses Email or Phone number, types it and clicks Continue
System checks the full name (MSG12) and the email or phone number (GBR-05)
System confirms the email or phone number (includes UC-05 Verify OTP)
System creates an active Customer account with this one confirmed value (GBR-27) and signs the customer in
System shows the Registration Success step with MSG47 and the button Go to my dashboard
Customer clicks Go to my dashboard; System shows the Customer Dashboard
Alternative Flows
Step 3.1_Guest cancels
Guest leaves the page before the code is confirmed
System creates no account
Step 4_The details are not accepted
System keeps the form and shows an error in one of these cases:
A field is blank (MSG01)
The full name is too short, too long or has digits or symbols (MSG12)
The email or the phone number has a wrong format (MSG02, MSG03)
It already belongs to an account (MSG05); the message has a link to Login
Guest corrects the details and goes back to step 3.
Step 5.1_The code is not confirmed
Verify OTP ends without a confirmed code (wrong or expired code, too many codes). Nothing is created; the guest can go back and try again.
Postconditions
The person has an active Customer account with one confirmed email or phone number and is signed in
The other email or phone number can be added later (UC-04)

1.2 Log In (UC-02)
Primary Actors
Customer, Pet Sitter, Staff, Sale Staff, Admin
Secondary Actors
Email Service, SMS Gateway
Description
As a registered user, I want to sign in with a code sent to my email or phone, so that I do not need a password.
Preconditions
The user is not signed in and has an account.
Normal Flow
Log in with a code
User opens Login, types the email or phone number and clicks Send code
System finds the account that uses this email or phone number
System confirms that the user owns it (includes UC-05 Verify OTP)
System starts a session of 30 days for a Customer or Pet Sitter, or 12 hours for Staff, Sale Staff or an Admin (GBR-08)
System shows the dashboard of the user's role
Alternative Flows
Step 2_No account uses this email or phone number
System shows MSG35 with the links Register and Become a pet sitter. No code is sent.
Step 2.1_The account is locked
System shows MSG17 and sends no code (GBR-04). A suspended pet sitter is not locked: he or she signs in normally but gets no new visits.
Step 3.1_The code is not confirmed
Verify OTP ends without a confirmed code. The user stays on Login and no session starts.
Step 4.1_The session ends later
When the time in GBR-08 has passed, or the account is locked, the next page shows the sign-in page with MSG16 or MSG17.
Postconditions
The user has an open session and sees his or her dashboard
Or no session is started and nothing is changed

1.3 Change Email or Phone Number (UC-04)
Primary Actors
Customer, Pet Sitter, Staff, Sale Staff, Admin
Secondary Actors
Email Service, SMS Gateway
Description
As a signed-in user, I want to add the email or phone number my account does not have yet, or change one, so that my sign-in codes still reach me.
A user who has lost the only confirmed email or phone number cannot sign in. He or she can contact Paw World, but PWC cannot restore the account in this version.
Preconditions
The user is signed in (UC-02 Log In) and the account is active.
Normal Flow
Add or change the email or phone number
User clicks Add or Change next to Email or Phone number on the Account Settings screen
System shows the Change Email or Phone Number screen with the current value, if any
User types the new email or phone number and clicks Send code
System checks the new value (GBR-05)
System confirms the new value (includes UC-05 Verify OTP)
System saves the new email or phone number as confirmed (GBR-06), shows MSG11 and returns to Account Settings
Alternative Flows
Step 1.1_The account has only one of the two
Account Settings shows Not added and an Add link for the missing one; the flow is the same, and the value is added instead of replaced.
Step 3.1_User cancels
User clicks Cancel before the code is confirmed
System returns to Account Settings; nothing is changed
Step 4_The new value is not accepted
System does not send a code and shows an error in one of these cases:
The field is blank (MSG01)
The email or the phone number has a wrong format (MSG02, MSG03)
It is the same as the current one (MSG04)
It already belongs to another account (MSG05)
User corrects the value and goes back to step 3.
Postconditions
The account uses the new email or phone number for sign-in codes and messages; a replaced one can no longer be used to sign in
The new email or phone number is marked as confirmed

1.4 Verify OTP (UC-05)
Primary Actors
None (included by UC-01 Register Account, UC-02 Log In and UC-04 Change Email or Phone Number)
Secondary Actors
Email Service, SMS Gateway
Description
The system checks that a person owns an email address or a phone number by sending a 6-digit code to it and asking the person to type it.
Preconditions
The including use case has an email or phone number to confirm, and it is not blocked (GBR-07).
Normal Flow
Confirm an email or phone number
System creates a 6-digit code valid for 5 minutes and sends it by email (Email Service) or by SMS (SMS Gateway)
System shows OTP Verification with the email or phone number partly hidden, MSG06 and a 60-second countdown
User types the code and clicks Confirm
System checks the code (GBR-07) and marks the email or phone number as confirmed
System returns to the including use case
Alternative Flows
Step 1.1_The code cannot be sent
The email service or the SMS gateway does not answer: System asks the user to try again in a few minutes and nothing is confirmed.
Step 2.1_The code does not arrive
After 60 seconds, User clicks Send code again
System sends a new code; the previous code stops working (GBR-07)
Return to step 3 of the normal flow
Step 2.2_Too many codes
5 codes have already been sent to this email or phone number in the last hour: System shows MSG10 and sends no code.
Step 4_The code is not accepted
System shows an error and the user can type the code again in one of these cases:
The code is wrong (MSG07)
The code has expired or a newer code was sent (MSG08)
After 5 wrong codes, the email or phone number is blocked for 15 minutes (MSG09) and the including use case ends.
Postconditions
The email or phone number is confirmed, or nothing is confirmed and the including use case does not go on

2. Pet Sitter Recruitment and Management (FE-05)
2.1 Submit Sitter Application (UC-16)
Primary Actors
Guest
Secondary Actors
Email Service
Description
As a person who wants to work for Paw World, I want to send my application online without creating an account, so that Paw World can call me for an interview.
Preconditions
The person is not signed in.
Normal Flow
Send a pet sitter application
Guest clicks Become a pet sitter
System shows Pet Sitter Application Form
Guest types the full name, date of birth, phone number, email, ID card number, service areas, work type (full-time or part-time) and experience, and adds the ID card photos (both sides), a portrait photo and any certificates
Guest clicks Send application
System checks the form: required fields and files (MSG01, MSG49), at least 18 years old, no open application (Pending, Under review or Interview) with the same phone number, email or ID card number, no rejection in the last 30 days (GBR-25), phone number and email not used by an account (GBR-05)
System saves the application as Pending with an application code
System shows MSG36, emails the same text with the code (Email Service) and notifies the Admins
Alternative Flows
Step 4.1_Too many applications
More than 3 applications have been sent from the same device or network in the last hour: System shows MSG50 and saves nothing.
Step 5_The application is not accepted
System keeps the form and shows an error in one of these cases:
A required field or file is missing (MSG01), or a file is not JPG, PNG or PDF or is larger than 5 MB (MSG49)
The person is under 18 (MSG39)
An open application (Pending, Under review or Interview) already uses this phone number, email or ID card number (MSG37)
The person was rejected less than 30 days ago (MSG38)
The phone number or the email already belongs to an account (MSG40)
Guest corrects the form and goes back to step 4.
Step 7.1_The receipt email cannot be sent
The application is still saved and shown on the page; the system tries to send the email again later.
Postconditions
The application is Pending and the Admins can see it in Pet Sitter Application List
The person has no account yet (GBR-28)

2.2 Approve or Reject Sitter Application (UC-18)
Primary Actors
Admin
Secondary Actors
Email Service
Description
As an Admin, I want to check each application, interview the person by phone and approve or reject it with a reason, so that only suitable people visit customers' homes.
Paw World's practice is to call at least twice on different days before choosing “Could not be reached”; the system does not check this.
Preconditions
The Admin is signed in and the application is open: Pending, Under review or Interview (GBR-25).
Normal Flow
Approve an application
Admin opens an application that has no result yet from Pet Sitter Application List (UC-17)
System shows Pet Sitter Application Review with the details, the documents and the checklist (valid ID card, at least 18 years old, portrait photo, experience with pets)
Admin clicks Start review on a Pending application; System marks it Under review
Admin ticks the checklist and clicks Move to interview; System marks the application Interview
Admin calls the applicant and records the interview result
Admin clicks Approve, which is shown only at Interview
System creates an active pet sitter account from the phone number and email of the application (GBR-28) and marks the application Approved
System emails a welcome message that explains how to sign in with a code (the email has no code)
System shows the account in the pet sitter list; the pet sitter gets no offers until he or she has service areas and either working hours (part-time) or shifts entered by an Admin (full-time, GBR-45)
Alternative Flows
Step 5.1_Admin has not decided yet
Admin saves the interview note; the application stays at Interview until the Admin approves or rejects it.
Step 6.1_Admin rejects the application
Admin clicks Reject and must choose a reason: Could not be reached, Documents not valid, Under 18, Not enough experience, Did not pass the interview or Other, and may add a note
System marks the application Rejected and emails the reason and the date from which the person can apply again (30 days later, GBR-25)
Step 7_The account cannot be created
The phone number or email has been used by another account in the meantime (GBR-05): System keeps the application at Interview and shows MSG05; the Admin contacts the person.
Postconditions
The application is Approved and the person has an active pet sitter account, or it is Rejected with a reason
The person has received the result by email

2.3 Review Pay Statement (UC-75)
Primary Actors
Pet Sitter
Secondary Actors
None
Description
As a pet sitter, I want to check my statement for each pay period and complain when something is wrong, so that I am paid for the work I did.
Preconditions
The pay period has ended: a week for a part-time pet sitter, a month for a full-time pet sitter.
Normal Flow
Check the statement
System makes the statement of the period: the visit pay (part-time) or the contract pay (full-time), the difficulty bonuses and the tips paid through PWC (GBR-31, GBR-43, GBR-44)
System sends the statement to the pet sitter in the app
Pet sitter opens the statement and checks each finished visit or groomed pet, its bonus and the tips
Pet sitter clicks Accept
System closes the statement
Alternative Flows
Step 4.1_The pet sitter finds a mistake
Pet sitter clicks Complain and writes the reason
System sends the complaint to the Admin, keeps the statement open and shows MSG66
The Admin answers it in Close Pay Statement (UC-76).
Step 4.2_The pet sitter does not answer
System closes the statement 3 days after it was sent (GBR-44).
Postconditions
The statement is closed, or waits for the Admin's answer to a complaint
Paw World pays it outside PWC (UC-76)

2.4 Close Pay Statement (UC-76)
Primary Actors
Admin
Secondary Actors
None
Description
As an Admin, I want to answer each complaint about a pay statement and record when it is paid, so that every statement ends with one clear amount.
Preconditions
A pet sitter has complained about a statement (UC-75), or a statement is closed and not paid yet.
Normal Flow
Answer a complaint and pay the statement
Admin opens a statement with a complaint
Admin checks the lines the pet sitter questions and the reason
Admin corrects the statement with a reason, or explains why it is right
System keeps the history of the statement, tells the pet sitter the answer and closes the statement (GBR-44)
Paw World pays the pet sitter outside PWC
Admin marks the statement paid
Alternative Flows
Step 1.1_The statement has no complaint
It is closed when the pet sitter accepts it or 3 days after it was sent (UC-75). The Admin goes to step 5.
Step 4.1_The pet sitter does not agree with the answer
There is no second review in PWC. The statement stays closed.
Step 6.1_A mistake is found after payment
The paid statement is not changed. The Admin corrects the amount in the statement of the next period (GBR-44).
Postconditions
The statement is closed, with the history of every correction
A paid statement is marked paid and is not changed again

3. Service and Policy Management (FE-06)
3.1 Configure Service Prices (UC-23)
Primary Actors
Admin
Secondary Actors
None
Description
As the Admin, I want to set the price list of each service, so that customers see a fixed price before they book and every booking is charged by the same rules.
The price of a visit is the base price plus the surcharges that apply (pet size, extra pets, travel); the total of a booking gets the discount for many visits (GBR-20). Pet sitter pay follows the contract of each pet sitter (UC-21), not this screen.
Preconditions
The Admin is signed in.
The service exists (UC-22) and is active.
Normal Flow
Configure service prices
Admin opens Service Price Configuration and chooses a service
System shows the current price list of the service: base price, pet size surcharges with their weight ranges, surcharge for each extra pet, travel surcharge for each service area and the discounts for many visits
Admin changes the values and clicks Save
System checks the values: base price above 0, surcharges from 0, weight ranges that do not overlap, discount rows with more visits than the row above and a discount from 1 to 50%
System saves the new price list with the time it takes effect and shows MSG51
System uses the new prices for bookings made from now on; bookings already made keep their prices (data rule Price snapshot)
Alternative Flows
Step 4_The values are not accepted
System keeps the form, shows MSG01 under an empty box or MSG52 under the table, and saves nothing. Admin corrects the values and goes back to step 3.
Step 1.1_The service has no price list yet
A new service starts with an empty price list. System shows it to customers only after its base price is saved, so it cannot be booked without a price.
Step 3.1_Admin leaves without saving
Admin clicks Cancel or leaves the screen; System keeps the price list in force and saves nothing.
Step 6.1_A customer is in the middle of a booking
The booking takes the prices in force when the customer clicks Confirm booking (UC-25), so the customer always confirms the total that is shown.
Postconditions
The service has one price list in force, saved with the time it takes effect
Bookings made before the change keep their prices

4. Service Booking (FE-07)
4.1 Create Booking (UC-25)
Primary Actors
Customer
Secondary Actors
None
Description
As a customer, I want to book at-home care for my pets for one or more days, so that a Paw World pet sitter looks after them while I am away.
The booking is confirmed at once. Nothing is paid until the booking ends (UC-49).
Preconditions
The customer is signed in and has at least one pet and one saved address.
Normal Flow
Create Booking
Customer clicks Book a Service and selects a service
System shows the booking form with the customer's pets and saved addresses
Customer selects the pets this service is for, the address, the dates and one or two visit times a day, answers the questions about each pet (GBR-43), and may ask for a pet sitter who has cared for the pets before
System checks the booking (GBR-09, GBR-10, GBR-11, GBR-33) and shows the visits, the total price, any discount for many visits (GBR-20) and the tier discount (GBR-42)
Customer checks the care instructions filled in from the pet profiles and confirms them (includes UC-40 Confirm Care Instructions)
Customer accepts the responsibility policy (GBR-34) and clicks Confirm booking
System saves the booking as Confirmed with a copy of the address, the prices, the length of a visit and the policy in force, splits it into visits and shows MSG22
System starts offering each visit to pet sitters (JOB-02)
Alternative Flows
Step 4_The booking cannot be made
System keeps the form and shows an error in one of these cases:
The address is not in any service area (MSG18)
The first visit starts less than 24 hours from now (MSG19)
The booking covers more than 30 days (MSG20)
The customer has an overdue bill (MSG21)
The customer has not paid any bill yet and asks for more than 7 days, or already has an unpaid booking (MSG67)
A chosen pet already has a booking at the same time (MSG68)
Visit 2 of a day starts before the expected end of visit 1 (MSG148)
Customer changes the details and goes back to step 3.
Step 6.1_Customer leaves before confirming
Customer closes the form or goes to another page
System saves nothing and no visit is created
Postconditions
The booking is Confirmed and its visits are Unassigned and being offered to pet sitters
The booking shows in the customer's Booking List; no money has been taken

5. Assignment and Conflict Checking (FE-10)
5.1 Assign Sitter to Visit (UC-31)
Primary Actors
Staff (an Admin can do the same)
Secondary Actors
None
Description
As a Staff member, I want to give a visit to a pet sitter by hand when the automatic offers did not fill it, so that the customer still gets the visit.
Preconditions
The visit is in the Needs action list and has not started.
Normal Flow
Assign a pet sitter by hand
Staff open the visit from the Needs action list on the Visit Board
System shows the planning board with the free pet sitters ranked with their reasons, as in the automatic offers
Staff choose a pet sitter, calling him or her first if needed, and click Assign
System checks again that the visit is still free and that the pet sitter has no overlapping visit (GBR-13, GBR-32)
System assigns the visit, closes its open offers as Withdrawn and removes it from Needs action
System notifies the pet sitter and the customer; if the visit had another pet sitter before, it is flagged for handover (UC-41)
Alternative Flows
Step 2.1_Nobody can take the visit
When no pet sitter is free and the visit starts within 12 hours, Staff cancel it without any charge (UC-39).
Step 4_A pet sitter or another Staff member took the visit at the same moment
The first one saved keeps the visit (GBR-13): System refuses this save, shows MSG25 with the visit as it is now, and the visit leaves Needs action.
Step 4.1_The pet sitter has an overlapping visit
System shows MSG46 and does not assign. Staff choose another pet sitter.
Step 4.2_Outside working hours or service areas
System shows MSG45
Staff confirm that they called the pet sitter and he or she agreed
Return to step 5 of the normal flow
Postconditions
The visit has exactly one assigned pet sitter, and the pet sitter and the customer know it
Or the visit is still in Needs action, or it is cancelled at no charge

6. Acceptance and Reassignment (FE-11)
6.1 Accept or Decline Visit Offer (UC-35)
Primary Actors
Pet Sitter
Secondary Actors
None
Description
As a pet sitter, I want to see the visits offered to me and accept or decline each one before its deadline, so that I only take visits I can do.
Preconditions
The pet sitter is signed in, is not suspended and has an offer that is still open.
Normal Flow
Accept a visit offer
Pet Sitter opens My Assignments from the notification or the dashboard
System shows the open offers with the visit time, service area, pets, service and the time left to answer
Pet Sitter opens an offer to see the visit details
Pet Sitter clicks Accept
System checks that the offer is still open and that the visit does not overlap the pet sitter's other visits (GBR-12, GBR-13)
System assigns the visit to the pet sitter, closes the other open offers of the visit as Withdrawn and shows MSG26
System notifies the customer that a pet sitter has been assigned
Alternative Flows
Step 4.1_Pet Sitter declines
Pet Sitter clicks Decline and gives a reason (GBR-12)
System closes the offer as Declined
When no other offer of the visit is still open, System offers the visit to the next pet sitter (JOB-02)
Step 5_The visit cannot be accepted
System does not assign the visit and shows MSG24 when:
The answer time has passed
Staff or another pet sitter took the visit first (GBR-13)
The pet sitter now has another visit at that time, travel time included
Postconditions
The offer is Accepted and the visit is Assigned to the pet sitter, or the offer is Declined and, when no other offer of the visit is still open, the visit is offered to the next pet sitter

6.2 Withdraw from Visit (UC-82)
Primary Actors
Pet Sitter
Secondary Actors
None
Description
As a pet sitter, I want to withdraw from a visit I accepted when I cannot go, so that Paw World has time to find another pet sitter.
Preconditions
The pet sitter has accepted the visit, and the visit has not been checked in.
Normal Flow
Withdraw 12 hours or more before the visit
Pet sitter opens the visit and clicks Withdraw
System checks that the visit starts 12 hours or more from now (GBR-21)
Pet sitter gives a reason and confirms
System takes the pet sitter off the visit, flags the visit for handover and offers it to the next pet sitter (GBR-12, JOB-02)
System shows MSG57 and counts the withdrawal in the Pet Sitter List
Alternative Flows
Step 2.1_The visit starts in less than 12 hours
Pet sitter gives a reason and sends the request
System puts the request in Staff's Needs action list and shows MSG56 (GBR-21)
Staff try to replace the pet sitter by hand (Reassign Sitter, UC-38). If nobody can take the visit, Staff cancel it at no charge (UC-39, GBR-15).
Step 4.1_Nobody is left to offer the visit to
The visit goes to Staff's Needs action list (GBR-14).
Step 4.2_Another pet sitter accepts the visit
He or she confirms the handover at least 1 hour before the visit (UC-41, GBR-24).
Postconditions
For an early withdrawal, the pet sitter is removed and the visit is offered again with a handover note
For a late withdrawal, the request goes to Staff; Staff either reassign the visit or cancel it at no charge

7. Booking Changes and Cancellations (FE-12)
7.1 Cancel Booking or Visit (UC-39)
Primary Actors
Customer, Staff
Secondary Actors
None
Description
As a customer, I want to cancel a whole booking or one visit and see the cost before I confirm, so that the bill holds no surprise.
Staff also use this use case to cancel, at no charge, a visit that still has no pet sitter 12 hours before it starts or whose pet sitter has not arrived 30 minutes after the start (GBR-29).
Preconditions
The booking is Confirmed and the visit has not been checked in.
Normal Flow
Customer cancels a booking or a visit
Customer opens the booking and clicks Cancel for the whole booking or for one visit
System works out the cost of cancelling (GBR-15) and asks for confirmation with MSG27
Customer confirms and may give a reason
System marks the visits Cancelled, closes their open offers, takes them out of any waiting change request (GBR-19) and adds any late cancellation fee to the booking
System notifies the pet sitters of those visits and shows MSG28
Alternative Flows
Step 1.1_Staff cancel a visit at no charge
Staff open a visit that still has no pet sitter 12 hours before it starts, or whose pet sitter has not arrived 30 minutes after the start, from the Needs action list
Staff click Cancel visit and give a reason
System cancels the visit without any charge (GBR-14, GBR-15), closes its open offers and notifies the customer
Step 1.2_The visit has already started
Cancel is not shown. The customer can contact Staff or report an incident (UC-47).
Step 4.1_Every visit of the booking is cancelled
The booking ends. A bill of 0 closes it; otherwise it goes to payment (UC-49).
Postconditions
The cancelled visits are no longer offered to pet sitters
Only the late cancellation fees, if any, are added to the bill

7.2 Request Booking Change (UC-84)
Primary Actors
Customer
Secondary Actors
None
Description
As a customer, I want to move a booking or a visit to another time, so that I do not have to cancel and book again.
Preconditions
The booking is Confirmed, and the visit to move has not started.
Normal Flow
Ask to move a booking or a visit
Customer opens the booking and clicks Change time for the whole booking or for one visit
Customer picks the new dates and times
System checks that the request is sent at least 12 hours before the old time, that the new time is at least 24 hours away, that the visit has not been moved before and that the new time follows the booking rules (GBR-09, GBR-22, GBR-33)
System shows that the price stays the same, and the customer sends the request
System saves the request, puts it in Staff's Needs action list and shows MSG53
Alternative Flows
Step 1.1_The visit starts in less than 12 hours
Change time is not shown. The customer can still cancel the visit (UC-39).
Step 3.1_The request breaks a time rule
System shows MSG54. The visit keeps its old time.
Step 3.2_The visit has already been moved once
System shows MSG55.
Step 3.3_A pet of the booking has another booking at the new time
System shows MSG68. The visit keeps its old time (GBR-33).
Step 3.4_Visit 2 of a day would start before the expected end of visit 1
System shows MSG148. The visit keeps its old time (GBR-09).
Step 3.5_A chosen visit is already in a waiting request
System shows MSG149. A visit can be in only one waiting request (GBR-22).
Step 3.6_The booking would cover more than 30 days
System shows MSG20 (GBR-09).
Step 3.7_The customer has not paid any bill yet and the booking would cover more than 7 days
System shows MSG67 (GBR-33).
Step 3.8_A day would have more than two visits of the booking
System shows MSG150 (GBR-09).
Step 5.1_Staff do not answer before the request expires
The request expires 6 hours before the old time or the new time, whichever comes first; the visit keeps its old time and the customer is told (JOB-02, GBR-19).
Postconditions
A change request waits for Staff; the visit keeps its old time until Staff approve it (UC-85)

7.3 Approve Booking Change (UC-85)
Primary Actors
Staff (an Admin can do the same)
Secondary Actors
None
Description
As a Staff member, I want to approve or reject each change request in time, so that every visit still has a pet sitter at its new time.
Preconditions
A change request is waiting and has not expired (GBR-19).
Normal Flow
Approve a change request
Staff open the request from the Needs action list or from Booking Details
System shows the old and new times and whether the pet sitter of each visit is free at the new time
Staff click Approve
System checks the new time again against GBR-09 and GBR-33, because another booking or another approved change may have come since the request
System moves the visits to the new time, keeps the price and marks each visit as moved once (GBR-22)
System keeps the pet sitter who is free at the new time; a visit whose pet sitter is not free is offered again (GBR-12, JOB-02)
System tells the customer and the pet sitters in the app
Alternative Flows
Step 1.1_The request is not handled before it expires
The system expires it 6 hours before the old time or the new time, whichever comes first, and the visit keeps its old time (JOB-02, GBR-19).
Step 3.1_Staff reject the request
Staff click Reject
System keeps the old time and tells the customer
Step 4.1_The new time no longer follows a rule
System shows MSG151 and moves nothing. The reason in MSG151 names the rule that fails, in words for Staff, not the customer's message: the pet has another booking at that time, visit 2 starts before visit 1 ends, a day has more than two visits, the booking covers more than 30 days, or it covers more than 7 days for a customer who has not paid any bill yet. Staff reject the request (Step 3.1).
Postconditions
An approved request: the visits have the new time, the same price and updated assignments
A rejected or expired request: the visits keep the old time

8. Care Instructions and Handover (FE-13)
8.1 Confirm Handover (UC-41)
Primary Actors
Pet Sitter (replacement)
Secondary Actors
None
Description
As a replacement pet sitter, I want to read what the customer asked and what happened at earlier visits before I go, so that the pets get the same care.
Preconditions
The visit is flagged for handover, is assigned to this pet sitter and has not been checked in.
Normal Flow
Confirm the handover
Pet Sitter opens the visit from the notification or My Assignments
System shows the confirmed care instructions, the handover note and the care reports of the earlier visits of the booking
Pet Sitter ticks “I have read the instructions and the handover note” and clicks Confirm handover
System saves the time of the confirmation and removes the handover flag
System opens the home access notes from 1 hour before the visit (UC-37) and allows check-in, and tells Staff
Alternative Flows
Step 3.1_Not confirmed 1 hour before the visit
System puts the visit in Staff's Needs action list (GBR-24). Staff call the pet sitter or send another one.
Step 4.1_The customer changes the instructions after the confirmation
The pet sitter must confirm the new version before checking in (GBR-23).
Postconditions
The handover is confirmed with its time, and the pet sitter can see the home access notes and check in

9. Care Execution and Reporting (FE-14)
9.1 Check In (UC-42)
Primary Actors
Pet Sitter
Secondary Actors
None
Description
As a pet sitter, I want to record that I have arrived, with a photo, so that the customer and Staff know the visit has started on time.
Preconditions
The pet sitter is assigned to the visit and, if he or she is a replacement, has confirmed the handover (UC-41).
The customer has confirmed the care instructions for the visit, and the visit has not been checked in.
Normal Flow
Check in
Pet Sitter opens today's visit at the customer's address and clicks Check in
System checks that he or she is the assigned pet sitter and that the time is not more than 30 minutes before the start (GBR-16)
System asks for a photo
Pet Sitter takes a photo with the phone
System saves the check-in time and photo, marks the visit Arrived and notifies the customer
Alternative Flows
Step 1.1_The pet sitter is late or does not come
15 minutes after the start without check-in, System shows the visit in red on Staff's Visit Board and Staff call the pet sitter (GBR-29)
30 minutes after the start, System puts the visit in Needs action
Staff send another pet sitter (UC-38) or cancel the visit without any charge (UC-39)
Step 2_Check-in is too early
System shows MSG29 and does not save the check-in.
Step 1.2_The pet sitter cannot get into the home
Pet Sitter reports an incident (UC-47) instead of checking in. Staff follow it up (UC-48) and decide, when resolving it, whether the visit is charged.
Postconditions
The check-in time and photo are saved
The visit is Arrived, and the customer can follow it live

9.2 Check Out (UC-43)
Primary Actors
Pet Sitter
Secondary Actors
None
Description
As a pet sitter, I want to record that I am leaving, with a photo, once the care tasks are done, so that the customer gets the care report and the visit counts as done.
Preconditions
The pet sitter has checked in to the visit (UC-42).
The pet sitter has completed the visit checklist (UC-44).
Normal Flow
Check out
Pet Sitter clicks Check out on the visit
System checks that every required checklist item is done or skipped with a reason (GBR-16)
System asks for a photo
Pet Sitter takes a photo with the phone
System saves the check-out time and photo and marks the visit Finished
System builds the care report from the check-in, the checklist and the photos and sends it to the customer (Report ready)
Alternative Flows
Step 2_The checklist is not complete
System shows MSG30 and does not check out. Pet Sitter finishes the checklist (UC-44) and goes back to step 1.
Step 1.1_No check-out 2 hours after the end
System puts the visit in Staff's Needs action list (GBR-24)
Staff call the pet sitter, then close the visit with a reason
If the visit has a check-in photo, it counts as done. Otherwise, it is cancelled at no charge, with no visit pay or difficulty bonus (GBR-24, GBR-31, GBR-43)
Postconditions
The check-out time and photo are saved
The visit is Finished and the customer has the care report

10. Incident Handling (FE-15)
10.1 Report Incident (UC-47)
Primary Actors
Pet Sitter, Customer
Secondary Actors
None
Description
As a pet sitter or a customer, I want to report a problem during a visit at once, with photos, so that Staff can act and the bill waits until it is settled.
Preconditions
The user is signed in, and the visit is his or hers and is today or has just ended.
Normal Flow
Report an incident
User opens the visit and clicks Report incident
System shows Report Incident with the types Sick or injured pet, Cannot get into the home, Damage, Pet lost and Other
User chooses the type, writes a description (required) and may add photos
User clicks Send
System saves the incident as Open, puts the bill of the booking on hold (GBR-17) and notifies Staff and the other party of the visit
Alternative Flows
Step 1.1_The pet sitter cannot get into the home
The pet sitter reports the incident instead of checking in; Staff follow it up (UC-48) and decide, when resolving it, whether the visit is charged.
Step 3.1_The pet is sick or injured
System shows MSG43 with Paw World's hotline. Pet sitters do not treat pets (Report 1 LI-03); Staff decide what to do with the customer.
Step 4_Required details are missing
System shows MSG01 under the description and saves nothing.
Postconditions
The incident is Open, Staff have been told, and the bill of the booking is on hold

10.2 Resolve Incident (UC-48)
Primary Actors
Staff
Secondary Actors
None
Description
As a Staff member, I want to record what was done about an incident and its result, so that the customer is treated fairly and the bill can be settled.
Preconditions
The incident is Open or In progress.
Normal Flow
Resolve an incident
Staff open the incident from Incident List
System shows Incident Details and Resolution with the visit, the report and the photos
Staff record the actions taken and the result, and decide whether the visit is charged
Staff may propose an amount to pay back with the reason and evidence (at most the bill, GBR-30)
Staff click Resolve
System sends any proposed amount to the Admin (UC-51); when there is none, or once the Admin has decided, System marks the incident Resolved
System notifies the customer and the pet sitter of the result. The bill waits until the result is final: the customer has not disagreed within 72 hours, or the Admin has decided after the customer disagreed (GBR-26). It is released only when the results of all incidents and complaints about the booking are final
Alternative Flows
Step 3.1_The pet sitter was at fault
Staff propose to the Admin to suspend the pet sitter (UC-19).
Step 4_The amount is more than the bill
System shows MSG44 and does not send the proposal.
Step 7.1_The customer disagrees with the result
The customer can disagree once; the Admin then makes the final decision (GBR-26).
Postconditions
The incident is Resolved with its actions and result
Any amount to pay back has been decided by the Admin; once the result is final, the bill is released or worked out again (GBR-26)

11. Payment Processing (FE-16)
11.1 Pay for Booking (UC-49)
Primary Actors
Customer
Secondary Actors
payOS Gateway
Description
As a customer, I want to pay the bill online once my booking ends, so that I only pay for the visits that were done.
Preconditions
The booking has ended: its last visit is finished or cancelled.
Its bill is final and above 0.
Normal Flow
Pay the bill
System works out the bill (minus any amount the Admin approved, GBR-17), sets the due date 48 hours later and notifies the customer in the app, and by email when the customer has an email
Customer opens the bill and clicks Pay
System asks payOS for a payment link and shows the payOS checkout page with a VietQR code
Customer scans the code with a banking app and pays
payOS sends the payment result to the system
System checks the signature, records the payment once (GBR-18), marks the booking Paid and shows MSG31
Alternative Flows
Step 1.1_A complaint or an incident about the booking is open
If the booking is Awaiting payment, the bill is on hold and shows MSG34; it cannot become overdue, and the 48 hours start when the result of the case is final (GBR-26). A booking already Overdue stays Overdue and keeps its due time; an approved amount is taken off the bill (GBR-30).
Step 1.2_A reminder before the due date
24 hours before the due date the system reminds the customer in the app, and by email when he or she has one (JOB-03).
Step 1.3_The bill is 0
System closes the booking. Nothing is paid.
Step 3_payOS cannot create the link
System shows MSG33. The bill stays unpaid; the customer can try again, and Staff see the booking in Unpaid Bookings (UC-50).
Step 5_The payment fails or is cancelled
System marks the payment Failed or Cancelled and shows MSG32. The bill stays unpaid and the customer can pay again with a new link.
Step 5.1_payOS sends the result late or more than once
System records the payment only once (GBR-18).
Step 6.1_The bill is not paid in 48 hours
The system asks payOS, then marks the booking Overdue (JOB-03). The customer cannot book again until he or she pays (GBR-11).
Postconditions
The booking is Paid with exactly one payment record
Or the bill is still unpaid, on hold or overdue as described above

12. Refund Processing (FE-17)
12.1 Process Refund (UC-51)
Primary Actors
Admin
Secondary Actors
None
Description
As an Admin, I want to decide every amount paid back to a customer, so that no money leaves Paw World without my check.
Preconditions
Staff have proposed an amount for an incident or a complaint.
Normal Flow
Decide an amount to pay back
Admin opens the proposal from Refund Processing
System shows the booking, its bill, the case, the reason and the evidence
Admin approves the amount or lowers it (never above the bill, GBR-30) and clicks Approve
System takes the amount off the bill if it is not paid yet; if it is paid, System records a refund for Paw World's accounting
System closes the proposal and notifies Staff; the incident or the complaint is then marked Resolved and the customer gets the result with the amount decided (UC-48, UC-53)
Alternative Flows
Step 3_The amount is more than the bill
System shows MSG44 and does not save.
Step 3.1_Admin rejects the proposal
Admin clicks Reject and writes the reason
System closes the proposal without any change to the bill and notifies Staff; the incident or the complaint is then marked Resolved and the customer gets the result without an amount (UC-48, UC-53)
Step 4.1_The Admin changed the amount
The bill or the refund uses the amount the Admin approved, and Staff see the change.
Postconditions
Every proposed amount is approved, lowered or rejected by an Admin
The bill or the refund record matches the approved amount

13. Ratings and Complaints (FE-18)
13.1 Submit Complaint (UC-52)
Primary Actors
Customer
Secondary Actors
None
Description
As a customer, I want to complain about a booking or a visit after it ends, and to disagree once with the answer, so that a problem is settled before I pay.
Preconditions
The customer is signed in and the visit is his or hers.
Normal Flow
Submit a complaint
Customer opens the visit or the booking and clicks Complain
System shows the complaint form
Customer writes what went wrong, may add photos and clicks Send
System checks the time limit and open complaints (GBR-26), saves the complaint as Open and puts the booking On hold if it is Awaiting payment; a booking already Overdue stays Overdue
System notifies Staff and shows the complaint in the customer's list with its status
Alternative Flows
Step 4_The complaint cannot be sent
System shows an error in one of these cases:
More than 7 days have passed since the visit ended or Staff closed it, or since the booking ended for a complaint about the whole booking (MSG41)
The visit, or the booking for a complaint about the whole booking, already has an open complaint (MSG42)
Step 5.1_The customer disagrees with the result
When the complaint is Resolved, Customer clicks Disagree once and writes why
System marks the complaint Escalated and sends it to the Admin for the final decision (UC-53)
Postconditions
The complaint is Open, Staff have been told and the bill waits
Or nothing is saved

13.2 Handle Complaint (UC-53)
Primary Actors
Staff; Admin for an escalated complaint
Secondary Actors
None
Description
As a Staff member, I want to follow each complaint until it has an answer, so that every customer gets a result.
Preconditions
The complaint is Open, or Escalated for the Admin.
Normal Flow
Handle a complaint
Staff open the complaint from Complaint Management
System shows the complaint with the booking, the visits and their care reports
Staff contact the customer or the pet sitter if needed and write the handling notes and the result
Staff may propose an amount to pay back (at most the bill, GBR-30)
Staff click Resolve. Without an amount, System marks the complaint Resolved, notifies the customer and shows MSG131. With an amount, System sends it to the Admin (UC-51) and shows MSG147; the complaint stays Open until the Admin decides, then System marks it Resolved and sends the result with the amount decided to the customer
System releases the bill only when the results of all complaints and incidents about the booking are final (no disagreement within 72 hours, or the Admin's final decision, GBR-26)
Alternative Flows
Step 1.1_The complaint is Escalated
Admin opens the complaint, reads the customer's reason and decides the final result
If the Admin changes the amount, the bill or the refund is worked out again
System marks the complaint Resolved, the result is final (GBR-26), and notifies the customer
Step 4_The amount is more than the bill
System shows MSG44 and does not send the proposal.
Postconditions
The complaint is Resolved with a result the customer can read
Any amount has been decided by the Admin

14. Shop Grooming Appointments (FE-22)
14.1 Book Grooming Appointment (UC-58)
Primary Actors
Customer
Secondary Actors
None
Description
As a customer, I want to book bathing or grooming at the shop and see the final price of each pet before I confirm, so that I know what I will pay when I collect my pets.
Preconditions
The customer is signed in, and the pets to bring are in his or her pet profiles.
Normal Flow
Book an appointment
Customer opens Grooming Booking and chooses the pets and the services for each pet
Customer answers the short questions about each pet
System shows the time slots in which a pet sitter with grooming skills is free on a shop shift (GBR-40, GBR-45)
Customer chooses a slot
System works out the final price of each pet with the tier discount, when the customer gets one (GBR-42), and shows the total
Customer checks the price of each pet and confirms
System saves the appointment, holds its place in the slot and shows MSG65. No deposit is taken
Alternative Flows
Step 2.1_The answers show a very risky pet
System shows MSG63. This pet can only be booked by calling the shop (GBR-40).
Step 7.1_The slot is no longer free
System shows MSG64 and the customer chooses another slot.
Step 7.2_A chosen pet already has a shop appointment at that time
System shows MSG152, saves nothing and the customer chooses another slot. A pet cannot have two shop appointments that overlap; a pet cancelled at no charge does not count (GBR-40).
Step 7.3_The price changed after it was shown
System shows MSG153 with the new final price and saves nothing. The customer checks the price and confirms again (GBR-40).
Postconditions
The appointment is saved with the final price of each pet and holds its place in the slot
Nothing is paid until the pets are handed back (UC-60)

14.2 Check In Pet at Shop (UC-59)
Primary Actors
Staff (an Admin can do the same)
Secondary Actors
None
Description
As a Staff member, I want to check each pet in and compare it with the customer's answers, so that the price and the work fit the real pet.
Preconditions
The appointment is booked for today.
Normal Flow
Check a pet in
Staff open Grooming Appointments and choose the appointment when the customer arrives
Staff take photos of each pet and record its condition compared with the customer's answers
Staff confirm the difficulty level of each pet (GBR-43)
Staff hand the pet to a pet sitter with grooming skills on a shop shift
System records the check-in and shows the pet in that pet sitter's Grooming Work (UC-91)
Alternative Flows
Step 1.1_The customer does not come in the allowed time
Staff mark the appointment as a no-show
System records a no-show for the customer (GBR-40)
Step 2.1_The pet is different from the answers
Staff tell the customer the surcharge in the price list for this pet
Customer agrees
System records the consent and adds the surcharge to this pet's price (GBR-40)
Step 2.2_The customer does not agree to the surcharge
Staff cancel this pet at no charge (GBR-40). The other pets go on.
Postconditions
Each pet is checked in with photos, its condition and its confirmed level, and has a pet sitter
Or the pet is cancelled at no charge, or the appointment is recorded as a no-show

14.3 Hand Back Pet and Take Payment (UC-60)
Primary Actors
Staff (an Admin can do the same)
Secondary Actors
payOS Gateway
Description
As a Staff member, I want to take the payment and hand back the pets, so that each appointment closes with the right amount.
Preconditions
Every pet of the appointment has ended: ready, cancelled or stopped.
Normal Flow
Take the payment and hand back the pets
System tells the customer to collect the pets and works out the amount to pay
Customer arrives; Staff open the appointment and show the amount
Customer pays in cash or by VietQR, and may add a tip
Staff confirm the cash received, or System shows the VietQR code and receives the result from payOS
System records the payment and any tip once
Staff hand back all the pets, also the cancelled and stopped ones
System closes the appointment and adds the pet sitters' earnings for it; a tip is shared equally among the pet sitters who did the work (GBR-31, GBR-43)
Alternative Flows
Step 1.1_No pet is ready
Staff hand back the pets, and the system closes the appointment with nothing to pay (GBR-40).
Step 4.1_The payment is not complete
For a failed VietQR payment the system shows MSG32. The customer pays again or pays another way. The pets are handed back only when the amount is paid in full (GBR-40).
Postconditions
The appointment is closed, paid in full or with nothing to pay
The payment and any tip are recorded once

14.4 Update Grooming Status (UC-91)
Primary Actors
Pet Sitter
Secondary Actors
None
Description
As a pet sitter on a shop shift, I want to mark each pet I work on, so that the customer is told when the pets are ready.
Preconditions
The pet sitter is on a shop shift, and a checked-in pet has been given to him or her (UC-59).
Normal Flow
Groom a pet
Pet sitter opens Grooming Work and sees the pets given to him or her
Pet sitter marks a pet in progress and bathes or grooms it
Pet sitter marks the pet ready
System records the pet as ready. When every pet of the appointment has ended, the system tells the customer to collect the pets (UC-60)
Alternative Flows
Step 2.1_The pet is too dangerous to go on
Pet sitter stops the pet and writes the reason
System records the pet as stopped and adds the reason to the pet profile (GBR-40)
Step 3.1_The pet is harder than declared
Pet sitter notes it in the pet profile
The pet keeps its level for this appointment. The level goes up from the next booking (GBR-43).
Postconditions
Each pet given to the pet sitter is ready or stopped
Notes about a harder or dangerous pet are in the pet profile

15. Online Product Orders and Delivery (FE-23)
15.1 Place Product Order (UC-62)
Primary Actors
Customer
Secondary Actors
payOS Gateway
Description
As a customer, I want to order food and accessories online for delivery or pick-up, so that I can buy without coming to the shop.
Preconditions
The customer is signed in, and the cart has at least one product (UC-61).
Normal Flow
Place an order
Customer opens the cart and clicks Check out
Customer chooses delivery to an address in a service area, or pick-up at the shop (GBR-10)
Customer chooses the way to pay: in advance through payOS, cash on delivery for a delivery, or at the shop for a pick-up (GBR-36)
System checks that each quantity is still available (GBR-35), works out the total with the tier discount, when the customer gets one (GBR-42), and, for a delivery, the shipping fee of the service area, and shows it
Customer checks the total and places the order
System creates the order, holds the quantities and shows MSG59
For payment in advance, System asks payOS for a payment link and shows the checkout page with a VietQR code
Customer pays; payOS sends the result; System records the payment once and marks the order Paid
Alternative Flows
Step 2.1_The address is not in a service area
System shows MSG18. The customer chooses another address or pick-up.
Step 3.1_The customer is over the refusal limit
Cash on delivery and payment at the shop are not offered, and the system shows MSG60 (GBR-36). The customer gets no tier discount while over the limit (GBR-42).
Step 4.1_A quantity is more than the quantity available
System shows MSG58. The order is not placed.
Step 7.1_Cash on delivery or payment at the shop
Nothing is paid now. The order goes to the Sale Staff to prepare (UC-64) or to make ready for pick-up (UC-67).
Step 8.1_The payment fails before the payment deadline
System shows MSG32. The customer can pay again with a new link.
Step 8.2_The order is not paid by the payment deadline
The system asks payOS, then cancels the order and ends its hold (JOB-04). This does not count as a refusal (GBR-36).
Postconditions
The order is placed and holds its quantities
A prepaid order is Paid, or is cancelled when the payment deadline passes

15.2 Prepare and Ship Order (UC-64)
Primary Actors
Sale Staff (an Admin can do the same)
Secondary Actors
None
Description
As a Sale Staff member, I want to prepare each delivery order and record its tracking code, so that the customer can follow the parcel.
Preconditions
A delivery order is placed and has not been handed to the carrier.
Normal Flow
Prepare and ship an order
Sale Staff open Order Management and choose an order to prepare
Sale Staff pack the goods and book the delivery on the carrier's site (outside PWC)
Sale Staff enter the tracking code
Sale Staff hand the parcel to the carrier and mark the order handed to the carrier
System ends the hold, takes the goods off the stock on hand (GBR-35) and shows the tracking code to the customer in My Orders
Alternative Flows
Step 1.1_A prepaid order is not paid yet
It cannot be prepared. It waits for the payment or is cancelled at the payment deadline (JOB-04).
Step 5.1_The parcel is delivered or comes back
Sale Staff record the carrier's status in Update Delivery Status (UC-65). The stock does not change there.
Postconditions
The parcel is with the carrier and has a tracking code
Its hold has ended and the stock on hand is lower by its quantities

15.3 Confirm Returned Order (UC-66)
Primary Actors
Sale Staff (an Admin can do the same)
Secondary Actors
None
Description
As a Sale Staff member, I want to check a returned parcel and count only the goods that can be sold again, so that the stock stays right.
Preconditions
The order is recorded as being returned (UC-65), and the parcel is back at the shop.
Normal Flow
Confirm a returned order
Sale Staff open the returned order in Order Management
Sale Staff check the goods and record the reason for the return and, for each product, the quantity that can be sold again
Sale Staff confirm
System adds only that quantity back to the stock on hand and marks the order returned (GBR-35)
System counts a refusal against the customer only when the customer refused the parcel (GBR-36)
For a prepaid order, System marks the order for a refund. The amount depends on why the order came back (GBR-37), and Paw World's accounting pays it outside PWC
Alternative Flows
Step 1.1_The order has already been confirmed returned
Confirm is not shown. The stock is never added back twice.
Postconditions
The order is returned, and only the goods that can be sold again are back in stock
A prepaid order is marked for a refund

15.4 Hand Over Pick-up Order (UC-67)
Primary Actors
Sale Staff (an Admin can do the same)
Secondary Actors
payOS Gateway
Description
As a Sale Staff member, I want to hand over a pick-up order only when it is paid in full, so that no goods leave the shop unpaid.
Preconditions
A pick-up order is placed. A prepaid order is paid.
Normal Flow
Hand over a pick-up order
Sale Staff pack the goods and mark the order ready in Order Management
System tells the customer in the app that the order can be collected until the pick-up deadline
Customer comes to the shop, and the Sale Staff member finds the order
System shows that the order is paid in full
Sale Staff hand over the goods
System ends the hold, takes the goods off the stock on hand and marks the order Picked up (GBR-35)
Alternative Flows
Step 4.1_The order is not paid yet
Customer pays in cash or by VietQR
Sale Staff confirm the cash received, or System receives the VietQR result from payOS
System records the payment once
A failed VietQR payment shows MSG32 and can be made again. The goods are handed over only when the order is paid in full (GBR-36).
Step 3.1_The customer does not come by the pick-up deadline
The system cancels the order and ends its hold (JOB-04). It counts as a refusal, and a prepaid order is marked for a refund (GBR-36, GBR-37).
Postconditions
The order is Picked up, its hold has ended and the stock on hand is lower by its quantities
Or the order is cancelled at the pick-up deadline

15.5 Report Faulty Product (UC-92)
Primary Actors
Customer
Secondary Actors
None
Description
As a customer, I want to report a faulty or wrong product with photos, so that Paw World exchanges it or refunds me.
Preconditions
The order has been delivered or picked up.
Normal Flow
Report a faulty or wrong product
Customer opens the order in My Orders and clicks Report faulty product
Customer chooses the product and the quantity, says whether it is faulty or wrong, and adds photos
Customer sends the report
System saves the claim, tells the Sale Staff and shows MSG62
Alternative Flows
Step 1.1_The time set by the Admin has passed
System shows MSG61. The report cannot be sent (GBR-49).
Postconditions
The claim waits for the Sale Staff to check it (UC-93)
Products are not taken back because the customer changed his or her mind (GBR-49)

15.6 Handle Faulty Product Claim (UC-93)
Primary Actors
Sale Staff, Admin
Secondary Actors
None
Description
As the Sale Staff and the Admin, we want to check each claim and decide an exchange, a refund or a rejection, so that the customer gets a clear answer.
Preconditions
A claim is waiting (UC-92).
Normal Flow
Exchange the product
Sale Staff open the claim, check the photos and the order, and write what they found
Admin opens the checked claim and approves an exchange for the same product
Sale Staff send new goods of the same product and click Exchange sent, which is shown only when the quantity available covers the claim (GBR-35)
System takes the new goods off the stock on hand (GBR-49), marks the claim Exchange sent and records who sent them and when
System tells the customer the result
Alternative Flows
Step 2.1_The Admin approves a refund
System records the refund to pay and tells the customer
Paw World's accounting pays it outside PWC.
Step 2.2_The Admin rejects the claim
System tells the customer the result
Step 3.1_The customer sends the faulty goods back
They are not added back to the stock on hand, so no stock adjustment is made for them (GBR-49).
Postconditions
The claim ends with an exchange, a refund or a rejection
The stock on hand goes down only by the new goods of an exchange

16. Counter Sales (FE-24)
16.1 Sell at Counter (UC-68)
Primary Actors
Sale Staff (an Admin can do the same)
Secondary Actors
payOS Gateway
Description
As a Sale Staff member, I want to sell products at the counter from the same stock as online orders, so that the quantity available is always right.
Preconditions
The customer is at the counter with the products he or she wants.
Normal Flow
Sell at the counter
Sale Staff look up each product and see its price and the quantity available (GBR-35)
Sale Staff enter the customer's phone number or email if the customer gives one
Sale Staff create the counter order with the products and quantities
System finds the customer, applies the tier discount when the customer gets one (GBR-42), holds the quantities and shows the total
Customer pays in cash or by VietQR
Sale Staff confirm the cash received, or System receives the VietQR result from payOS
System records the payment once
Sale Staff hand the goods to the customer and complete the sale
System ends the hold and takes the goods off the stock on hand (GBR-35, GBR-38). The sale counts toward the customer's tier when the customer was found
Alternative Flows
Step 1.1_Not enough stock
A product cannot be added beyond the quantity available. The customer may choose other products.
Step 4.1_No customer is found, or none is given
The order has no tier discount and does not count toward a tier.
Step 6.1_The payment is not complete
For a failed VietQR payment the system shows MSG32. The customer pays again or changes the way to pay (GBR-38).
Step 6.2_The customer does not pay in the end
Sale Staff cancel the order
System ends the hold; the stock on hand does not change
Postconditions
A completed sale: its hold has ended and the stock on hand is lower by its quantities
A cancelled order: its hold has ended and the stock on hand is the same

17. Suppliers and Stock (FE-25)
17.1 Record Goods Receipt (UC-70)
Primary Actors
Sale Staff (an Admin can do the same)
Secondary Actors
None
Description
As a Sale Staff member, I want to record every delivery from a supplier with what I accepted and rejected, so that the stock goes up only by the goods we keep.
Preconditions
The supplier is in the supplier list (UC-69).
Normal Flow
Record a goods receipt
Sale Staff open Goods Receipt and choose the supplier
Sale Staff check the goods and enter, for each product, the quantity received and the quantity accepted. The system calculates the rejected quantity as the received quantity minus the accepted quantity. If any goods are rejected, Sale Staff enter the reason
Sale Staff save the receipt
System saves the goods receipt and adds the accepted quantity to the stock on hand (GBR-39)
Alternative Flows
Step 2.1_A product is not in the catalogue
The Admin adds it first (Manage Products, UC-57). Then its goods can be received (GBR-39).
Step 2.2_A quantity or the reason is not valid
The quantity received must be a whole number greater than 0 (MSG113). The quantity accepted must be a whole number from 0 to the quantity received (MSG119). When the rejected quantity is more than 0, the reason is required (MSG120). The receipt cannot be saved until they are corrected.
Step 4.1_Some goods are rejected
System sends the Admin MSG122 for each product line with rejected goods, with the receipt code, the quantity rejected and the reason. The goods go back to the supplier outside PWC.
Step 4.2_Everything is rejected
The receipt is still saved. The stock does not change.
Step 4.3_A saved receipt is wrong
The receipt is not edited. The Admin corrects the stock with a stock adjustment (UC-95, GBR-50).
Step 4.4_The supplier sends a make-up delivery
It is a new delivery with a new receipt.
Postconditions
Every delivery has a goods receipt
The stock on hand goes up only by the accepted quantity

III. Functional Requirements
This section describes the screens of PWC, grouped by feature and sub-feature. Each screen has a mockup, a short description linked to its use cases and a field table. The background jobs and the external APIs are described in the last two sections. The user interface is in Vietnamese. This specification uses English names for screens, fields and buttons, with Vietnamese labels in parentheses where needed to match the mockups.
1. Registration and Authentication (FE-01)
1.1 Register Account
1.1.1 Customer Registration Screens



The mockup contains two variants of the Customer Registration Screen: Register by Email and Register by Phone Number. In both variants, a Guest enters a full name and chooses exactly one contact method. Only the input for the selected method is shown. The system does not ask the Guest to create a password. On submission, the system validates the data, creates a pending registration, and sends an OTP through the selected channel. These screens support UC-01 Register Account.



Field Name
Description
Full name 
Required text input. 2 to 50 letters and spaces (MSG12).
Contact method 
Required single choice: Email or Phone number. The mockup shows separate variants with one method selected. The selected option determines which contact input is shown and where the OTP is sent. 
Email address 
Required when Email is selected; hidden when Phone number is selected. A valid email (MSG02) not used by another account (GBR-05, MSG05). The system normalizes the value before checking it.
Phone number 
Required when Phone number is selected; hidden when Email is selected. 10 digits starting with 0 (MSG03), not used by another account (GBR-05, MSG05).
Create account 
Register button. Submit the registration form. The system displays field-level validation feedback if required data is missing, invalid, or already registered. 
Sign in 
Link to the sign-in screen for users who already have an account. 

1.1.2 Verify Contact Screen

This screen allows the Guest to confirm the single contact method selected on the Customer Registration Screen. The displayed destination and delivery-channel message must match that selection: show only the masked email for email registration or only the masked phone number for phone registration. The current mockup illustrates the phone/SMS variant. A valid OTP completes registration and activates the Customer account. This screen supports UC-01 Register Account.

Field Name
Description
Contact destination
Read-only masked email address or phone number entered on the previous screen. Show exactly one destination and identify the matching channel (email or SMS).
Verification code
Required input of exactly six decimal digits. OTP validity: five minutes. The OTP can be used once; issuing a replacement invalidates the previous code.
Verify
Confirm button. Submits the OTP for validation. If valid and unexpired, the system confirms the chosen contact and completes registration. Otherwise, it displays the applicable error state.
Resend code
Resend Code action. Requests another OTP through the same selected contact channel. Resend is available after 60 seconds; the system allows no more than five OTP sends per account/contact per hour. The mockup displays the countdown and sent-code count.
Change contact information
Update contact information action. Return to the registration form so the Guest can correct the selected contact. Any OTP for a superseded contact/registration attempt is no longer accepted.

1.1.3 Registration Success Screen

This screen confirms that registration has completed after successful OTP verification. It displays the Customer's name, the single contact method confirmed during registration, the Customer role, and an action to open the Customer Dashboard; the customer is already signed in. It supports UC-01 Register Account.

Field Name
Description
Registration success message
Confirms that the Customer account has been activated successfully.
Full name
Read-only name provided on the registration screen.
Verified contact
Read-only, masked email address or phone number selected and verified during registration. Show only the selected method.
Role
Read-only account role: Customer.
Go to my account
Go to my account (Vào trang khách hàng) button. Opens the Customer Dashboard (UC-01).

1.2 Authentication
1.2.1 Login / Logout

This screen allows any registered user to:
Choose How to Get the Code: get the code by email or by SMS.
Enter a Contact: type the confirmed email or phone number of the account.
Get a Code: ask for a one-time code and go on to OTP Verification.
Register: open the registration form when there is no account yet.
Use cases: UC-02 Log In, UC-03 Log Out. No password is used (GBR-27). The code is typed on OTP Verification (UC-05). The user then reaches the page of his or her role and stays signed in for 30 days (Customer, Pet Sitter) or 12 hours (Staff, Sale Staff, Admin) (GBR-08). Log out (Đăng xuất) is in the account menu of the header on every signed-in page; it ends the session and opens the Home Page (UC-03).
Field Description
Field Name
Description
Get the code by (Nhận mã bằng)
Two options: Email and SMS. Initial value: Email.
Email, Phone number (Email, Số điện thoại)
