Required. The confirmed email of an account or, when SMS is chosen, its confirmed phone number (GBR-27). Format checks: MSG02, MSG03.
No account uses it: MSG35. A locked Customer, Staff or Sale Staff account: MSG17 (GBR-04).
Send OTP (Gửi mã OTP)
Button. Sends a 6-digit code to the email or phone number (GBR-07, MSG06) and opens OTP Verification.
Register (Đăng ký)
Link. Opens Register Account (UC-01).

1.2.2 OTP Verification

This screen allows a user who is registering, signing in, or adding or changing an email or phone number to:
Enter Code: type the 6-digit code sent to the email or phone number shown on the screen.
Send Code Again: get a new code when the 60-second countdown ends, at most 5 codes an hour.
On the screen, he or she can also
Go back to correct the email or phone number by clicking Change contact (Đổi liên hệ).
The screen is opened from Customer Registration, Login and Change Email or Phone Number. Use case: UC-05 Verify OTP (Section II), included by UC-01, UC-02 and UC-04. The picture shows the screen opened from Login.
The system shall accept a code only within 5 minutes after it is sent and only if it is the latest code sent (GBR-07).
The system shall send a new code only 60 seconds after the last one, and at most 5 codes an hour to one email or phone number (GBR-07).
The system shall block the email or phone number for 15 minutes after 5 wrong codes (GBR-07).
The system shall show only part of the email or phone number on the screen.
Field Description
Field Name
Description
Sent to
Static text: the code was sent to {email or phone number}; check the inbox and the spam folder.
Only part of the email or phone number is shown, for example lan•••@gmail.com or 0912 ••• 678; the picture still shows the whole email.
Code (Mã OTP 6 chữ số)
One box for the 6 digits. Required, digits only. The code is valid for 5 minutes (GBR-07).
Do not ask again on this device (Không hỏi lại trên thiết bị này trong 30 ngày)
Check box, shown when signing in. For a personal device only. The user stays signed in on this device as GBR-08 allows; Staff, Sale Staff and Admin accounts stay limited to 12 hours. Logging out or a locked account ends the session.
Verify and log in (Xác thực & đăng nhập)
Button. Enabled when 6 digits are typed. Checks the code (GBR-07): a right code returns to the use case that opened the screen; a wrong code shows MSG07, an expired or old code MSG08, and the fifth wrong code MSG09.
Send code again (Gửi lại sau …s)
Link with a countdown from 60 seconds after each code (GBR-07). Active when the countdown ends. Sends a new code; the previous code stops working and MSG06 is shown.
At 5 codes in the last hour, MSG10 is shown instead of sending.
Change contact (Đổi liên hệ)
Link. Returns to the previous screen with the value kept, so the user can correct it.
Register (Đăng ký)
Link, shown when signing in. Opens Register Account (UC-01).

1.3 Sign-in Details
1.3.1 Change Email or Phone Number

This screen allows a signed-in user (Customer, Pet Sitter, Staff, Sale Staff or Admin) to:
Add or Change Email: enter an email the account does not have yet, or a new one, and confirm it on OTP Verification.
Add or Change Phone Number: enter a phone number the account does not have yet, or a new one, and confirm it on OTP Verification.
The form opens on Account Settings when the user clicks Change email or Change phone number (Đổi email, Đổi số điện thoại), or Add. The picture shows the form for the email. Use cases: UC-04 Change Email or Phone Number (Section II) and UC-05 Verify OTP.
The system shall add or replace an email or phone number only after the code sent to the new value is confirmed (GBR-06).
The system shall refuse an email or phone number that already belongs to another account (GBR-05).
The system shall let a user who has only one of the two add the other one.
Field Description
Field Name
Description
Title (Đổi email, Đổi số điện thoại)
Static text: the contact the user clicked on Account Settings.
Current email / Current phone number
Read only, shown above the form on Account Settings.
When the account has none, it shows Not added and the title reads Add email or Add phone number.
New email (Email)
Shown for the email. Required.
Valid email format, at most 100 characters.
Must be different from the current email and must not belong to another account (GBR-05).
Help text: the code is sent to this address.
New phone number (Số điện thoại)
Shown for the phone number (not in the picture). Required.
10 digits, starting with 0.
Must be different from the current number and must not belong to another account (GBR-05).
Send code (Gửi mã xác minh)
Button. Checks the new value (MSG01 to MSG05) and opens OTP Verification (UC-05).
After the code is confirmed, the new value is saved (GBR-06) and Account Settings shows MSG11.
Cancel (Hủy)
Button. Closes the form without any change.

1.4 Authorization
Role-based access has no screen of its own; it applies to every screen and API of PWC. The rights of each role are given in the Permission Matrix (I.4.4), the rules in GBR-01 to GBR-04 and GBR-08, and the security requirements in IV.2.3.
When a user opens a page or calls an API that his or her role does not allow, the system does not carry out the action and shows MSG15. When the session has ended or the account has been locked, the system shows the sign-in page with MSG16 or MSG17. After signing in, each role lands on its own dashboard: Customer, Pet Sitter, Staff, Sale Staff or Admin Dashboard.
The system shall check the user's role, and the record itself for Restricted rights, on every page and API request (GBR-02).
The system shall end every open session of an account at once when the account is locked (GBR-04).
The system shall send a signed-out user who opens a private page to the sign-in page (GBR-01).
2. Public Service Information (FE-02)
2.1 Service Catalogue
2.1.1 Service List

This page allows a guest or a customer to:
See the Services: see the three home services and bathing and grooming at the shop, each with a short description and a starting price.
Open or Book a Service: open the details of a service or start a booking.
Use case: UC-07 View Service List. The page is public (GBR-01); a guest sees Log in (Đăng nhập) and Register (Đăng ký tài khoản) in the header. The prices come from the price list the Admin sets (UC-23); the amounts in the mockup are samples.

Description

Button. Opens Service Details (UC-08).
Book service (Đặt dịch vụ)
Button. Opens Create Booking (UC-25) for a home service, or Grooming Booking (UC-58) for the shop. A guest is asked to log in or register first.

2.1.2 Service Details


This page allows a guest or a customer to:
Read What Is Included: see the tasks of a visit, or the services done at the shop, and what is not included.
Check the Price: see the starting price, the surcharges that can apply and the discounts (GBR-20, GBR-40, GBR-42).
Switch or Book: open another service or start a booking.
Use case: UC-08 View Service Details. Opened from Service List. The mockup shows a home service (At-home pet care) and, second, bathing and grooming at the shop, which uses the same page. The final price is shown on Create Booking (UC-25) or Grooming Booking (UC-58). The amounts in the mockup are samples; the real prices come from the price list the Admin sets (UC-23).

Description
Other services (Dịch vụ khác)
Four tabs: At-home pet care, Dog walking, Vet trip, and Bathing and grooming.

Read only. The standard tasks of a visit of a home service.
For bathing and grooming the section is Shop services (Dịch vụ tại cửa hàng): the services the Admin has set, such as bathing, grooming, nail trimming and ear cleaning.
Service scope and limitations (Phạm vi dịch vụ và giới hạn)

Pricing and surcharges (Giá và phụ phí)
Read only. Starting price, the surcharges for pet size, extra pets and travel (by service area), and the discount for many visits, all from the price list (GBR-20).
For bathing and grooming: the price by pet size, the surcharge when the pet is different from the answers (GBR-40), the tier discount (GBR-42) and payment at the shop when the pet is handed back.
Book service (Đặt dịch vụ, Đặt lịch tại cửa hàng)
Button. Opens Create Booking (UC-25) for a home service, or Grooming Booking (UC-58) for bathing and grooming. A guest is asked to log in or register first.

This page allows a guest or a customer to:
Learn About Paw World: see what Paw World does: visits and walks for dogs and cats, and the shop with food and accessories.
Find a Service or a Product: open the list of services, the details of a service or the product list.
Start a Booking: start a booking for a service shown on the page.
Use case: UC-06 View Home Page. The page is public (GBR-01). A guest who starts a booking is asked to log in or register first. The page is shown in three parts. The services and prices on it come from the price list the Admin sets (UC-23); the ones in the picture are samples.

Description

A guest sees the cart, Log in (Đăng nhập) and Register (Đăng ký); a signed-in user sees the cart, the bell and the account menu.

Static text in four cards: visits and play, accessories, food, and pick-up or delivery of orders.

Static text in three steps.

Two tabs: Services (Dịch vụ) and Products (Sản phẩm).

2.2.2 Service Areas and Policies


This page allows a guest or a customer to:
See the Service Areas: see the service areas where Paw World works (GBR-10).
Read the Booking Rules: see the booking time limits (GBR-09), the cancellation policy (GBR-15), the payment term (GBR-17) and the refund policy (GBR-30).
Read the Responsibility Policy: see what Paw World is responsible for, what it does not do and what the customer must do (GBR-34).
Use case: UC-09 View Service Areas and Policies. The page is public (GBR-01). The service areas and the policy texts come from the booking policies the Admin sets (UC-24); the customer accepts the responsibility policy when booking (UC-25). The mockup is shown in two parts.
Field Description
Field Name
Description
Service areas (Khu vực phục vụ)
Read only. The service areas the Admin has set, each a group of wards and communes.
Booking time limits (Thời hạn đặt lịch)
Read only. Book at least 24 hours ahead. A booking covers at most 30 days, from its first to its last visit (GBR-09).
A separate note for new customers: until the first bill is paid, a booking covers at most 7 days and only one booking can be unpaid (GBR-33).
Cancellation policy (Chính sách huỷ)
Read only. Free 24 hours or more before a visit; later, 30% of that visit's price; free when Staff cancel because no pet sitter is free or the pet sitter did not come (GBR-15).
Payment (Thanh toán)
Read only. The bill is due 48 hours after it is final (GBR-17).
Refund policy (Chính sách hoàn tiền)
Read only. Before payment the amount is taken off the bill; after payment an Admin records it and Paw World's accounting pays it back by bank transfer outside PWC (GBR-30).
Responsibility policy (Chính sách trách nhiệm)
Read only. What Paw World is responsible for, what it does not do (no medicine or treatment, no overnight stay; Report 1, LI-03) and what the customer must do (GBR-34).

3. User Accounts and Customer Profiles (FE-03)
3.1 My Account
3.1.1 Account Settings

This screen allows a signed-in user (Customer, Pet Sitter, Staff, Sale Staff or Admin) to:
View Account Details: see his or her full name, role, and which email and phone number are confirmed.
On the screen, he or she can also
Add the missing email or phone number, or change one, in the form of Change Email or Phone Number.
Use case: UC-04 Change Email or Phone Number (Section II). Opened from Contact and security (Liên hệ & bảo mật) in the account menu. The screen has no Save button: a customer changes the full name on Customer Profile (UC-11), and the Admin changes it for a pet sitter, a Staff or a Sale Staff member (UC-10 Manage User Accounts), because a pet sitter's name must match the ID card checked at approval.
The system shall show the full name and the role as read only.
The system shall show the email and the phone number as read only: Confirmed with a Change link, or Not added with an Add link.
The system shall let only a customer change his or her own full name (on Customer Profile); the Admin changes it for pet sitters, Staff and Sale Staff.
Field Description
Field Name
Description
Full name (Họ và tên), Role (Vai trò)
Read only. Role: Customer, Pet Sitter, Staff, Sale Staff or Admin.
Static text under them: a customer changes the name on Customer Profile; this screen does not change the name or the role.
Email
Read only, partly hidden, with Confirmed (Đã xác minh) and Change email (Đổi email).
When the account has no email, it shows Not added and an Add link (not in the picture). Both open the form of Change Email or Phone Number for the email.
Phone number (Số điện thoại)
Read only, partly hidden, with Confirmed (Đã xác minh) and Change phone number (Đổi số điện thoại).
When the account has no phone number, it shows Not added and an Add link (not in the picture). Both open the form of Change Email or Phone Number for the phone number.
Help text
Static text: a new contact replaces the old one of the same kind only after a valid code. A user who cannot reach the only confirmed email or phone number is asked to contact Paw World. PWC cannot restore such an account in this version.
Protected contact details (Thông tin liên hệ được bảo vệ)
Static text: the screen shows only the contacts of the signed-in account, partly hidden.

3.3.2 Service Addresses and Home Access

This screen allows a customer to:
See the Saved Addresses: see each address with its service area, or Not served (Ngoài khu vực phục vụ) when it is outside every service area (GBR-10).

Write the Home Access Note: write how to get in, for example a gate code or where the key is.
Remove an Address: remove an address that no unfinished booking uses.
Use case: UC-12 Manage Service Addresses. Create Booking (UC-25) lets the customer choose only an address in a service area. Among pet sitters, only the pet sitter of a visit reads the home access note (UC-37); Staff see it only while handling that visit.

Service area (Khu vực phục vụ)
Read only. Found from the ward or commune, or Not served (GBR-10).
Home access note (Ghi chú vào nhà)
Optional. Free text. Only the pet sitter of the visit sees it, from 1 hour before the visit until check-out.
Note
Static text: a booking keeps a copy of its address, so edits here do not change it; an address used by a booking that is not finished cannot be removed.
Save address (Lưu địa chỉ)
Button. Saves the address and shows MSG51.
Remove address (Xoá địa chỉ)
Button. Asks MSG73, then removes the address. Greyed out while a booking that is not finished uses the address.

5. Pet Sitter Recruitment and Management (FE-05)
5.1 Submit Pet Sitter Application
5.1.1 Pet Sitter Application Form

This screen allows a guest to apply to become a pet sitter, without an account, for Admin review. Both an email address and a phone number are required. The application is sent in one step; there is no draft. This screen supports UC-16 Submit Pet Sitter Application.

Field Name
Description
Full name
Required text input for the Applicant's full name.
Date of birth
Required date input. Applicants must be at least 18 years old on the submission date (MSG39).
Phone number
Required. 10 digits starting with 0 (MSG03). It must not belong to an account (MSG40) or to another open application (GBR-25, MSG37).
Email address
Required. A valid email (MSG02). It must not belong to an account (MSG40) or to another open application (GBR-25, MSG37).
ID card number
Required. The number of the CCCD or passport. One ID card number can have at most one open application (GBR-25, MSG37).
Current address
Optional text input for the Applicant's current address.
Service areas
Required. One or more of the service areas the Admin has set, where the Applicant wants to work.
Work type (Hình thức làm việc)
Required (not in the mockup). Full-time (Toàn thời gian) or Part-time (Bán thời gian): how the applicant wants to work. The Admin can filter the applications by it (UC-17).
Years of experience
Required input describing the Applicant's years of pet-care experience.
Pet species experience
Optional multi-select limited to Dog and Cat. The Applicant selects the species for which they have experience. No other species are offered.
Experience description
Required text area describing the Applicant's pet-care experience.
Qualifications and certificates
Optional text area for relevant qualifications, certificates or training.
Portrait photo
Required file upload. Accepts JPG, PNG or PDF, up to 5 MB per file (MSG49).
Identity document
Required file upload. The Applicant provides images/files for both sides of a CCCD, or the information page of a passport. Accepts JPG, PNG or PDF, up to 5 MB per file (MSG49).
Supporting documents
Optional file upload for certificates or other supporting materials. Accepts JPG, PNG or PDF, up to 5 MB per file (MSG49).
Submit Application
Submit your application (Gửi đơn ứng tuyển) button. Checks the required fields, the age, the contact details, the ID card number and the files. If they pass and no open application uses the same phone number, email or ID card number, the system saves the application as Pending, emails a receipt with the application code and shows the confirmation screen.

5.1.2 Application Submission Confirmation Screen

This screen confirms that the application was saved and is waiting for Admin review. It shows the application code and the Pending status. It does not promise a review deadline. This screen supports UC-16 Submit Pet Sitter Application.

Field Name
Description
Submission confirmation
MSG36: the application, with its code, has been received and Paw World will call to arrange an interview.
Application code
Read only. The code of the application, also sent in the email receipt.
Application status
Read-only status: Pending (Chờ duyệt).
Applicant name
Read-only name from the submitted form, if shown in the application summary.
Return to Home
Return to the Paw World Care home page (Về trang chủ Paw World Care) button. Opens the Home Page.

5.1.3 Pending Application Notice

If the Applicant attempts to submit another application while an earlier application is pending, the system does not create a duplicate. It displays a notice that an application is already under review and provides a return-to-home action. Do not display internal exception identifiers such as E5 to the Applicant. This state supports exception E5 of UC-16 Submit Pet Sitter Application.

Field Name
Description
Pending application notice
Explains that an application with this phone number, email or ID card number is already waiting for review (MSG37) and asks the Applicant not to send it again.
Return to Home
Return to the Paw World Care home page (Về trang chủ Paw World Care) button. Opens the Home Page.

5.2 Pet Sitter Screening
5.2.1 Pet Sitter Application List

This screen allows the Admin to:
View Application List: see the applications sent from the pet sitter application form.

Search Applications: by full name, phone number or application code.
Sort Application List: by submitted date, by clicking the column header.
On the screen, he or she can also
Choose to open an application on the Pet Sitter Application Review screen by clicking View, to review it (UC-18).
Use case: UC-17 View Sitter Applications. Only the Admin can open this screen (Permission Matrix, I.4.4).
The system shall show the Admin every pet sitter application, Pending ones by default, oldest first.
The system shall let the Admin filter applications by status, service area and work type and search them by full name, phone number or application code.
The system shall show this list to Admins only (GBR-02).

Field Name
Description
Status tabs

Default: Pending.
Search
Text box, at most 50 characters. Looks for the text in the full name, phone number and application code.

5.2.2 Pet Sitter Application Review

This screen allows an Admin to:
Check the Application: see the applicant's details, service areas, experience and documents.
Move Through the Steps: start the review of a Pending application, then move it to the interview step.
Tick the Checklist: tick the four checks: valid ID card, at least 18 years old, portrait photo, experience with pets.
Record the Interview: record the time of the phone interview, the result and a note.
Approve or Reject: at the interview step, approve the application, or reject it with a reason from a list.
Use cases: UC-18 Approve or Reject Sitter Application, UC-19 Suspend or Reinstate Pet Sitter. Opened with View (Xem) on Pet Sitter Application List (UC-17); the mockup shows an application at the Interview step. For an approved application the right side shows Suspend (Tạm ngưng) with a reason instead: the pet sitter gets no new visits and the visits he or she has accepted are offered again (GBR-04, UC-19). Reinstate (Khôi phục) asks for a note.
Field Description
Field Name
Description
Status
Read only. Pending (Chờ duyệt), Under review (Đang thẩm định), Interview (Phỏng vấn), Approved (Đã duyệt) or Rejected (Từ chối), with the application code and the time it was sent.
Review steps (Tiến trình xét duyệt)
Read only. Received (Tiếp nhận), Review (Thẩm định), Interview (Phỏng vấn) and Decision (Quyết định), with the time of each step reached.
Applicant details (Thông tin ứng viên)
Read only. Date of birth and age, phone number, email, ID card number, service areas and experience, as sent on the application form (UC-16).
Documents (Giấy tờ)
Read only. Front and back of the ID card, portrait photo, and certificates if any. Clicking one opens it full size.
Start review (Bắt đầu thẩm định)
Button (not in the mockup). Shown on a Pending application. Marks it Under review.
Move to interview (Chuyển sang phỏng vấn)
Button (not in the mockup). Shown on an application Under review. Marks it Interview.
Checklist (Kiểm tra hồ sơ)
Four check boxes: valid ID card, at least 18 years old, portrait photo, experience with pets.
Call time (Thời gian gọi)
Required to approve. Date and time of the phone interview.
Interview result (Kết quả)
Required to approve. Passed (Đạt), Not passed (Không đạt) or Could not be reached (Không liên lạc được).
Note (Ghi chú)
Optional. Free text.
Approve (Duyệt đơn)
Button. Shown only at Interview, and enabled only when every check box is ticked, the call time is entered and the result is Passed.
Creates an active pet sitter account from the phone number and email of the application, emails a welcome message (GBR-28) and shows MSG74.
Reason (Lý do)
Required to reject. Chosen from a list, for example Could not be reached (Không liên lạc được).
Reject (Từ chối đơn)
Button. Shown only at Interview. Marks the application Rejected, emails the reason and the date from which the person can apply again, 30 days later (GBR-25), and shows MSG75.

5.3 Contracts and Pay
5.3.1 Pet Sitter Contracts

This screen allows an Admin to:
See the Contracts: see the contract of each pet sitter: type, pay, start and end dates.

Use case: UC-21 Manage Pet Sitter Contracts. Opened from Pet sitters (Người chăm sóc) in the Admin menu. Paw World pays the pet sitters outside PWC; PWC uses the contract to make the pay statements (GBR-31, GBR-44, UC-75). A full-time pet sitter works the shifts the Admin enters in Shift Schedule (UC-80); a part-time pet sitter sets his or her own working hours (UC-29, GBR-45). The amounts in the mockup are samples.

Description

6. Service and Policy Management (FE-06)
6.1 Service Configuration
6.1.1 Service Management

This screen allows an Admin to:
See the Services: see the home services and the shop services, each Active (Đang mở) or Off (Đã tắt).
Add or Edit a Service: set the name, the pets it is for, a short description, the length of a visit (services at home) and the tasks of each visit.
Turn a Service Off: stop new bookings of a service.
Use case: UC-22 Manage Services. The prices are set on Configure Service Prices (UC-23). The tasks become the checklist of each visit (GBR-16); the customer can add tasks on Visit Care Instructions (UC-40).
Field Description
Field Name
Description
Add service (+ Thêm dịch vụ)
Button. Opens an empty form on the right.
Service list (Danh sách dịch vụ)
Read only. Two groups, At home (Tại nhà) and At the shop (Tại cửa hàng); each service shows Active (Đang mở) or Off (Đã tắt). Clicking a service opens it in the form.
Service name (Tên dịch vụ)
Required. Free text.
Group (Nhóm)
Required when the service is added: At home or At the shop. Read only after that.
For (Dành cho)
Required. Dog (Chó), Cat (Mèo) or both. Create Booking shows a pet the service is not for greyed out (UC-25).
Short description (Mô tả ngắn)
Optional. Shown on Service List and Service Details (UC-07, UC-08).
Length of a visit (Thời lượng mỗi buổi)
Required for the services at home (not in the mockup); not used for the services at the shop. Minutes. A visit lasts from its start time for this length; PWC uses it to check that a pet sitter or a pet is free and to find the end of a visit (GBR-24, GBR-32, GBR-33). For a vet trip it is the planned length, used to plan the visit.
A booking keeps the length that applied when it was made.
Tasks of each visit (Việc trong mỗi buổi)
List of tasks, each Required (Bắt buộc) or Not required (Không bắt buộc). Add task (+ Thêm việc) adds one; the bin icon removes one.
Each required task must be done, or skipped with a reason, before check-out (GBR-16).
Note
Static text: a service that is off cannot be booked; bookings already made keep their price and terms.
Save service (Lưu dịch vụ)
Button. Saves the service and shows MSG51.
Turn off (Tắt dịch vụ)
Button. Asks MSG76, then marks the service Off. On a service that is off the button is Turn on (Mở lại).

6.2 Configure Service Prices
6.2.1 Service Prices List


This screen allows an authorized Admin to view services and their current price configuration, then open a service to configure its prices. Any sample service or sample configuration shown in the mockup is illustrative only and must not be treated as an approved price. This screen supports UC-23 Configure Service Prices.


Field Name
Description
Service name
Name of the existing service whose price configuration is displayed.
Base price
Displays the configured base price for the service, or an unconfigured/illustrative placeholder where no approved value is available. The currency and pricing unit follow the project configuration; this screen does not define them.
Pet-size surcharges
Displays the configured surcharge values associated with the pet-size categories configured for the service. The screen does not define category names, weight ranges or the number of categories.
Additional-pet surcharge
Displays the configured surcharge for additional pets, if configured.
Travel surcharge
Displays that the travel surcharge is set for each service area (GBR-20).
Visit-count discounts
Displays configured discount thresholds and percentages. The mockup may use the UC-23 examples: 7 visits — 5% and 20 visits — 10%; these are configurable values, not immutable system defaults.
Configure prices
Configure Prices action. Opens the selected service's price configuration form.
6.2.2 Service Price Configuration

This screen allows an authorized Admin to enter or update the price components for the selected service. It provides actions to save valid changes or cancel and discard unsaved changes. A saved configuration applies to bookings created after it is saved; existing bookings retain the price snapshot captured when they were created. This screen supports UC-23 Configure Service Prices.

Field Name
Description
Selected service
Read-only service name identifying the configuration being edited.
Base price
Input for the service's base price. The screen does not define an unconfirmed currency or pricing unit.
Pet-size surcharge groups
Repeatable rows for the pet-size categories used by the service. Each row contains a category label and its surcharge value. Admin can add or remove rows. Category labels, weight ranges and the number of categories are not predefined by this requirement.
Additional-pet surcharge
Input for the surcharge for additional pets.
Travel surcharge
Input for the travel surcharge of each service area (GBR-20).
Visit-count discount thresholds
Editable rows containing a visit-count threshold and discount percentage. The mockup may illustrate 7 visits — 5% and 20 visits — 10%. Admin can add or update discount rows.
Save
Save action. Validates the configuration and saves all changes together. On success, the system confirms that the configuration was saved. If saving fails, it reports the failure and leaves the previously saved configuration in effect.
Cancel
Cancel action. Discards unsaved changes and keeps the previously saved configuration in effect.
Booking price notice
Read only. A saved configuration applies to bookings created after it is saved; bookings already created keep their prices.

6.3 Policy Configuration
6.3.1 System Settings

This screen allows an Admin to:
Set the Booking Rules: set the booking notice, the longest booking, the free cancellation window, the late cancellation fee and the payment deadline (GBR-09, GBR-15, GBR-17).
Set the Offer Rules: set the answer time for offers, the travel time between two visits, and how late a pet sitter may withdraw and a customer may ask to move a visit (GBR-12, GBR-21, GBR-22).
Set the Service Areas: group wards and communes into service areas (GBR-10).
Write the Policies: write the refund policy and the responsibility policy shown to customers (GBR-30, GBR-34).
Set the Shop Rules: set the refusal limit for cash on delivery, the payment and pick-up deadlines of orders and the time to report a faulty product (GBR-36, GBR-49).
Use case: UC-24 Configure Booking Policies. A booking keeps a copy of the rules in force when it was made. Service Areas and Policies (UC-09) shows the service areas and the policies to everyone. The travel time and the shop values in the mockup are samples.
Field Description
Field Name
Description
Booking and cancellation (Đặt lịch và huỷ)
Minimum booking notice (Đặt trước tối thiểu)
Required. Hours (GBR-09).
Longest booking (Thời gian dài nhất của một lịch đặt)
Required. Days (GBR-09).
Free cancellation window (Huỷ miễn phí nếu huỷ trước buổi)
Required. Hours (GBR-15).
Late cancellation fee (Phí huỷ muộn)
Required. Percentage of the visit price (GBR-15).
Payment deadline (Hạn thanh toán sau khi hoá đơn chốt)
Required. Hours after the bill is final (GBR-17).
Offers and pet sitters (Lời mời và người chăm sóc)
Answer time (Thời gian trả lời lời mời)
Required. Hours, and minutes for a visit that starts within 24 hours (GBR-12).
Travel time between two visits (Thời gian di chuyển giữa hai buổi)
Required. Minutes. Used when PWC checks that a pet sitter is free (GBR-12, GBR-32).
Withdrawal limit, Change limit (Rút khỏi buổi, Đổi giờ buổi)
Required. Hours before the visit (GBR-21, GBR-22).
Shop (Cửa hàng)
Refusal limit (Số lần từ chối nhận hàng tối đa)
Required. Number of refusals: parcels refused on delivery and pick-up orders not collected (GBR-36, MSG60).
Prepaid payment deadline, Pick-up deadline (Hạn thanh toán đơn trả trước, Hạn đến lấy đơn tại cửa hàng)
Required. Hours and days. The payment deadline counts from the time the order is placed; the pick-up deadline counts from the time Sale Staff mark the order ready (GBR-36, JOB-04).
Faulty product report time (Thời hạn báo sản phẩm lỗi)
Required. Days (GBR-49, MSG61).
Service areas (Khu vực phục vụ)
Service area
Name and its wards and communes. Edit (Sửa) changes them; Add service area (+ Thêm khu vực) adds one.
A ward or commune belongs to one service area only (MSG78). Addresses in other wards and communes are Not served (GBR-10).
When a ward or commune moves to another service area, the saved addresses in it follow the new service area, and so do the pet sitters who can get new offers there. Bookings already made keep their copy of the address, and visits already accepted by a pet sitter are not cancelled or moved.
Policies for customers (Chính sách cho khách)
Refund policy (Chính sách hoàn tiền)
Required. Free text, shown on Service Areas and Policies (UC-09, GBR-30).
Responsibility policy (Chính sách trách nhiệm)
Required. Free text, shown on Service Areas and Policies and accepted by the customer when booking (UC-25, GBR-34).
Save settings (Lưu cài đặt)
Button. Checks each value (MSG77), saves the settings for new bookings and orders and shows MSG51.

7. Service Booking (FE-07)
7.1 Booking
7.1.1 Create Booking

This screen allows a customer to:
Choose the Service and Pets: pick one of the three home services and the pets it is for; a dog walk is only for dogs.
Set the Address and Time: choose a saved address in a service area, the first and the last day, and one or two visit times a day (GBR-09, GBR-10, GBR-33).
Describe the Pets: answer short questions about the behaviour and health of each pet and add care requirements.
Ask for a Pet Sitter: choose, if wanted, a pet sitter who has cared for these pets before (GBR-12).
Check the Price and Confirm: see the number of visits, the price of a visit, the discount for many visits and the total (GBR-20), accept the responsibility policy (GBR-34) and confirm.
Use case: UC-25 Create Booking (Section II). Before the booking is saved, the customer confirms the care instructions filled in from the pet profiles (Visit Care Instructions, UC-40). Nothing is paid until the booking ends (UC-49).
Field Description
Field Name
Description
Service and pets
Service (Dịch vụ)
Required. Three option cards: At-home pet care, Dog walking, Vet trip.
Only the services the Admin has made active are shown.
Pets (Thú cưng)
Required. One option for each pet of the customer; at least one.
A pet the service is not for, such as a cat for Dog walking, is shown greyed out.
Address and time
Address (Địa chỉ)
Required. The customer's saved addresses with their service area.
An address outside the service areas cannot be chosen (GBR-10, MSG18).
From date, To date (Từ ngày, Đến ngày)
Required. The first visit starts at least 24 hours from now (MSG19). The booking covers at most 30 days (MSG20).
A customer who has not paid any bill yet can book at most 7 days and have one unpaid booking (GBR-33, MSG67).
Visits per day (Số buổi mỗi ngày)
Required. Two options: 1 visit, 2 visits.
Visit 1 time, Visit 2 time (Giờ buổi 1, Giờ buổi 2)
Required. The start time of each visit of the day. Visit 2 time is shown only when 2 visits is chosen.
Each visit lasts the length set for the service (UC-22). Visit 2 starts at or after the expected end of visit 1 (its start time plus the length of a visit): MSG148 (GBR-09).
About the pets
Pet questions
Required. Short questions for each chosen pet about its behaviour and health, each with fixed answers. PWC sets the difficulty level of the pet from the answers (GBR-43).
Care requirements (Yêu cầu chăm sóc)
Optional. Free text.
Preferred pet sitter (Người chăm sóc mong muốn)
Optional. Lists only the pet sitters who have cared for one of the chosen pets before.
This pet sitter gets the first offer when he or she can take the visit (GBR-12).
Summary and price
Summary and price (Tóm tắt và giá)
Read only. Service, pets, dates, visit times, number of visits, price of a visit, subtotal, discount for many visits, tier discount (GBR-42, not in the mockup) and total (GBR-20). It changes each time a field changes.
Responsibility policy (Chính sách trách nhiệm)
Required check box. The link opens the policy (GBR-34).
Confirm booking (Xác nhận đặt lịch)
Button. Checks GBR-09, GBR-10, GBR-11 and GBR-33, then saves the booking as Confirmed and shows MSG22.
A customer with an overdue bill sees MSG21. A pet that already has a booking at the same time gives MSG68.

7.1.2 Booking List

This screen allows a customer or Staff to:
See the Bookings: see bookings by status, newest first: a customer sees his or her own bookings, Staff see all bookings.
Find a Booking: for Staff: search bookings by customer, booking code and date.
Open a Booking: open a booking to see its visits, pet sitters, reports and bill.
Use cases: UC-26 View My Bookings, UC-32 View All Bookings. The customer opens it from My bookings (Lịch đặt của tôi) in the header; Staff open it from Bookings (Lịch đặt) in the Staff menu. The Visit Board shows visits, not bookings (UC-30, UC-33). The mockup shows the customer's view; the Staff view adds the search, a date filter and the customer column.
Field Description
Field Name
Description
Book a service (+ Đặt dịch vụ)
Button, customer only. Opens Create Booking (UC-25).
Tabs
All (Tất cả), Confirmed (Đã xác nhận), Awaiting payment (Chờ thanh toán), Paid (Đã thanh toán) and Other (Khác: Closed, On hold, Overdue), each with its count.
Search and date filter
Staff only. Free text: customer name, phone number or booking code; first and last day.
Bookings
Read only table: booking code, service and length of a visit, pets, dates and visit times, number of visits, status. Staff also see the customer.
A booking awaiting payment shows when the bill is due, 48 hours after it is final (GBR-17). A closed booking shows why, for example cancelled at no charge.
When nothing matches, MSG88 is shown.
View (Xem)
Link. Opens Booking Details (UC-27).

7.1.3 Booking Details

This screen allows a customer, and Staff, to:
View the Booking Terms: see the service, pets, address and service area, dates, visit times, preferred pet sitter, care requirements and when the responsibility policy was accepted.
Follow the Visits: see each visit with its time, pet sitter, status and report.
See the Price and the Bill: see the number of visits, the discount, the total and the state of the bill (GBR-17).
Act on the Booking: ask to change the time (UC-84), confirm care instructions (UC-40), cancel (UC-39) or send a complaint about a visit or the whole booking (UC-52).
Use case: UC-27 View Booking Details. The customer opens it from Booking List (UC-26 View My Bookings); Staff open it from Booking List (UC-32 View All Bookings) or from the Visit Board.
Field Description
Field Name
Description
Status
Read only. The status of the booking, for example Confirmed (Đã xác nhận).
Booking terms (Thông tin lịch đặt)
Read only. The copy saved when the booking was confirmed (UC-25): service, pets, address with its service area, dates, visit times, preferred pet sitter, care requirements and the time the policy was accepted.
Visits (Các buổi chăm sóc)
Read only table: date, time, pet sitter, status, report. View (Xem) opens the report of a finished visit.
Send complaint (Gửi khiếu nại), customer only (not in the mockup), is on each visit that is done, or that Staff closed and that counts as done, within 7 days after it ended or was closed (GBR-26). A cancelled visit has none. It opens Submit Complaint (UC-52) for that visit; when the booking has ended, the form also lets the customer choose the whole booking, from any of these visits, within 7 days after the booking ends (its last visit is finished or cancelled). On a visit that already has an open complaint, it is shown only when the whole booking can be complained about, and only the whole booking can be chosen. When every visit was cancelled and the bill is above 0 (late cancellation fees), Send complaint is shown above the table and opens Submit Complaint for the whole booking.
Show all visits (Xem cả … buổi) opens Visit Schedule (UC-28).
Price and payment (Giá và thanh toán)
Read only. Number of visits and price of a visit, discount for many visits, tier discount (GBR-42, not in the mockup), total (GBR-20) and bill status.
The bill is made when the booking ends (its last visit is finished or cancelled) and is due 48 hours later (GBR-17).
Change time (Đổi giờ)
Button. Opens the change request (UC-84). Not shown for a visit that starts in less than 12 hours (GBR-22).
Care instructions (Hướng dẫn chăm sóc)
Button. Opens Visit Care Instructions (UC-40).
Cancel booking (Huỷ lịch đặt)
Button. Starts UC-39 and shows the cost with MSG27 before the customer confirms (GBR-15).

7.2 Dashboard
7.2.1 Customer Dashboard

This screen allows a customer to:
See What Comes Next: see the next visits and shop appointments, and the bookings still waiting for a pet sitter.
See What to Pay: see the bills to pay and the bills on hold.
Follow the Orders: see the product orders in progress.
Start Something New: book a service, open the shop or the pet list.
Use case: UC-77 View Customer Dashboard. The first screen after a customer signs in. Numbers and links only, no charts. The names, codes and numbers in the mockup are samples.

Description

Tiles
Read only: next visits, next shop appointments, bookings waiting for a pet sitter and bills to pay, each with a short note (for example a bill on hold, GBR-26).

8.1.2 Visit Status Tracker

This screen allows a customer to:

Use case: UC-78 Track Visit Status. Opened from a visit on Booking Details or Visit Schedule, and from the notifications of the visit. The status changes with the pet sitter's actions: check-in makes the visit Arrived (UC-42), the first save of the checklist makes it In progress (UC-44), check-out makes it Finished (UC-43) and the care report makes it Report ready (UC-45). The page updates by itself. The times, names and rating in the mockup are samples.

A visit that has no pet sitter yet shows Finding a pet sitter (Đang tìm người) instead (not in the mockup).

9. Pet Sitter Availability (FE-09)
9.1 Availability
9.1.1 Working Hours Calendar

This screen allows a part-time pet sitter to:
Set Weekly Hours: turn each day on or off and add one or more time slots to it.
Choose Service Areas: choose, among the service areas the Admin has set, where he or she takes visits.
Save: save the hours and the service areas.
Use case: UC-29 Set Working Hours. A full-time pet sitter sees the shifts of the contract here instead and does not set hours (GBR-45, UC-80 Manage Shift Schedule). Offers go only to pet sitters inside their working hours and service areas (GBR-12). An Admin can change the service areas of a pet sitter.
Field Description
Field Name
Description
Status summary (Trạng thái cài đặt)
Read only. Number of working days a week and of service areas chosen.
Day row
One row for each day: On or Off switch (Bật, Tắt) and time slots From–To (Từ–Đến).
A day that is off shows Not working (Không làm).
Add time slot (+ Thêm khung giờ)
Adds a time slot to the day. The bin icon removes one.
Service areas (Khu vực phục vụ)
Check boxes over the service areas the Admin has set. The chosen ones are listed under Currently serving (Đang phục vụ).
Note on accepted visits
Static text: a change that touches visits already accepted flags them for Staff; the system does not cancel or reassign them.
Save changes (Lưu thay đổi)
Button. Saves the working hours and the service areas and shows MSG51.

9.1.2 Day Off Request

This screen allows a pet sitter to:
Ask for Days Off: choose the first and the last day off and give a reason.
See the Visits Hit: see the accepted visits on those days before sending the request.
Follow the Requests: see each request with its status and the Staff note.
Use case: UC-79 Request Day Off. A day off that touches a visit starting in less than 12 hours cannot be asked here; the pet sitter withdraws from that visit instead (UC-82, GBR-21).
Field Description
Field Name
Description
From date, To date (Từ ngày, Đến ngày)
Required. From today on. To date is the same as or after From date.
Reason (Lý do)
Required. Free text.
Visits on these days
Read only note. Lists the accepted visits on the chosen days.
If one of them starts in less than 12 hours, the request cannot be sent and MSG69 is shown (GBR-21).
Send request (Gửi đơn)
Button. Saves the request as Pending (Chờ duyệt) and shows MSG70.
Sent requests (Đơn đã gửi)
Read only table: days off, reason, sent at, status (Pending, Approved, Rejected) and the Staff note.

9.1.3 Day Off Request – Staff Review

This screen allows Staff to:
Review Requests: see the pending requests with the pet sitter, the days, the reason and the accepted visits on those days.
Approve or Reject: approve a request, or reject it with a note to the pet sitter.
Use case: UC-79 Request Day Off. When Staff approve, the pet sitter is taken off the accepted visits on those days. The system offers them again automatically with a handover note (GBR-12, GBR-21, JOB-02) and Staff are told. A visit goes to the Needs action list of the Visit Board only under GBR-14.
Field Description
Field Name
Description
Tabs
Pending (Chờ duyệt) with the count, Approved (Đã duyệt), Rejected (Từ chối).
Requests
Read only table: pet sitter and contract type, days off, reason, visits hit, sent at.
Sorted by the first day off.
Approve (Duyệt)
Button. Marks the request Approved, takes the pet sitter off the accepted visits on those days and offers them again (JOB-02). Tells the pet sitter in the app.
Note
Static text: affected visits are offered again automatically. A visit enters Needs action when GBR-14 applies.
Reject (Từ chối)
Link. Asks for a note, marks the request Rejected and tells the pet sitter in the app.

9.1.4 Shift Schedule

This screen allows an Admin to:
See the Shifts: see the fixed weekly shifts of each full-time pet sitter, at home or at the shop.
Add, Change or Remove a Shift: add a shift, change its day, time or place, or remove it.
Use case: UC-80 Manage Shift Schedule. Opened from Shift schedule (Lịch ca) in the Admin menu. Only pet sitters with an active full-time contract are listed (UC-21). They are offered visits only inside their shifts and do not set working hours; they see their shifts on Working Hours Calendar (UC-29, GBR-45).
Field Description
Field Name
Description
Place filter (Tất cả nơi làm)
All places, at home (Tại nhà) or at the shop (Cửa hàng).
Legend (Nơi làm)
Static. The colour of home and shop shifts.
Add shift (+ Thêm ca)
Button. Opens the shift form empty.
Weekly shifts (Ca cố định hằng tuần)
Read only grid: one row for each full-time pet sitter (name, code, usual place), one column for each day from Monday to Sunday. A day can have more than one shift; a day without a shift shows Off (Nghỉ).
Clicking a shift opens it in the form.
Pet sitter (Người chăm sóc)
Required. A pet sitter with an active full-time contract.
Day (Ngày)
Required. Monday to Sunday.
Start time, End time (Giờ bắt đầu, Giờ kết thúc)

Remove shift (Xoá ca)

10. Assignment and Conflict Checking (FE-10)
10.1 Assignment
10.1.1 Visit Board

This screen allows Staff to:
See What Needs Action: see the visits the automatic offers could not handle, with the reason and the time left (GBR-11, GBR-14, GBR-21, GBR-24, GBR-29).
Follow Today's Visits: see every visit of the day with its live status; a visit not checked in 15 minutes after its start is shown in red (GBR-16, GBR-29).
Act on a Visit: assign or reassign a pet sitter (UC-31, UC-38), cancel a visit at no charge (UC-39), call the pet sitter or the customer, or close a visit that was not checked out.
Use cases: UC-30 View Unassigned Visits, UC-33 Monitor Today's Visits. JOB-02 and JOB-07 fill the Needs action list (Background Job Inventory).
Field Description
Field Name
Description
Tabs
Needs action (Cần xử lý), Today's visits (Buổi hôm nay), All visits (Tất cả buổi), each with its count.
Search
Free text: booking code, customer or pet sitter.
Service area filter
All service areas, or one service area.
Reason filter
All reasons, or one reason.
Needs action list (Cần xử lý)
Table: visit (booking code, date, time), customer, reason, time left or time late, pet sitter, action.
Reasons: nobody left to offer; no pet sitter 24 hours before the start; not checked in 30 minutes after the start; withdrawal less than 12 hours before the visit; change of working hours; not checked out 2 hours after the end; handover not confirmed 1 hour before the visit; booking change request waiting for Staff (UC-84, not in the mockup); customer with an overdue bill.
Sorted by the time left. Late items are in red.
Today's visits (Buổi hôm nay)
Table: time, booking, service, service area, pet sitter, live status.
Actions (Thao tác)
Assign (Giao người) opens UC-31. Reassign or Cancel (Giao lại / Huỷ) opens UC-38 or UC-39.
Call (Gọi …) shows the phone number. Close visit (Đóng buổi) opens the dialog below.
Open request (Xem yêu cầu) opens a booking change request (UC-85) (not in the mockup).

Description

10.1.3 Assign Pet Sitter

This screen allows Staff to:
See the Visit: see the visit that needs a pet sitter, why it is in the Needs action list and the time left.
Choose a Pet Sitter: see the pet sitters who can take it, ranked with reasons in the same order as the automatic offers (GBR-12), and choose one.
Assign: give the visit to the chosen pet sitter (GBR-13, GBR-32).
Use case: UC-31 Assign Sitter to Visit (Section II). Opened with Assign (Giao người) in the Needs action list of the Visit Board. If nobody can take the visit and it starts within 12 hours, Staff cancel it at no charge from the Visit Board (UC-39, GBR-14). An Admin can do the same (GBR-03).
Field Description
Field Name
Description
Visit to assign (Buổi cần giao)
Read only. Booking, service, date and time, pets, customer, address and service area, the reason it is in Needs action and the time left.
Handover note
Static text, shown when the visit had another pet sitter before: the new pet sitter must confirm the handover at least 1 hour before the visit (UC-41, GBR-24).
Pet sitters who can take it (Người chăm sóc có thể nhận)
Table: name and contract type, reasons for the rank (cared for the customer's pets before, rating, number of visits that day), working hours or shift, service areas, check.
Check: Can take (Nhận được); Call first (Cần gọi trước) when the visit is outside the pet sitter's working hours, shift or service areas; Overlap (Trùng giờ) when the pet sitter has another visit at that time, travel time included. A pet sitter with an overlap is greyed out and cannot be chosen (GBR-32).
Choose (radio button)
Selects one pet sitter. The chosen row is highlighted.
Assign (Giao buổi)
Button. Checks again that the visit is still free and that the pet sitter has no overlapping visit (MSG46).
For a pet sitter marked Call first, MSG45 asks Staff to confirm they called and he or she agreed (GBR-32).
If a pet sitter accepted an automatic offer or another Staff member assigned the visit just before, the save is refused, MSG25 is shown with the visit as it is now, and the visit leaves Needs action (GBR-13).
Otherwise assigns the visit, closes its open offers as Withdrawn, removes it from Needs action, tells the pet sitter and the customer, flags the visit for handover if it had another pet sitter, and shows MSG91.
Back (Quay lại)
Button. Goes back to the Visit Board without any change.

Field Name
Description

Offers (Lời mời) and Accepted (Đã nhận), each with its count.

A pet the pet sitter cared for before is marked (Bạn từng chăm thú này). An offer with little time left has a red frame.

Back (Quay lại)
Button. Closes the decline form.
Today (Hôm nay)
Read only. Today's visits in the order planned by JOB-06, each with time, service, booking, service area and status. Clicking one opens the visit.
View working calendar (Xem lịch làm việc)
Link. Opens Working Hours Calendar (UC-29).

11.1.2 Visit Details (Pet Sitter)

This screen allows a pet sitter to:
See the Visit: see the service, time, customer and address of an offered or accepted visit.
Know the Pets: see the pet profile: breed, weight, temperament, behaviour and vaccination.
Read the Instructions: read the care instructions confirmed for this visit, and confirm a new version before check-in (GBR-23).
Get In: see the home access notes from 1 hour before the visit until check-out (UC-37).
Act on the Visit: check in, report an incident or withdraw from the visit.
Use cases: UC-36 View Visit Details, UC-37 View Home Access Notes. Opened from My Assignments, the dashboard or a notification. A replacement pet sitter sees the home access notes and can check in only after confirming the handover (UC-41). The mockup shows an accepted visit less than 1 hour before it starts, after the customer changed the instructions.
Field Description
Field Name
Description
Visit information (Thông tin buổi)
Read only. Service, date and time, customer, and address with its service area.
Pets (Thú cưng)
Read only. Name, species, breed, weight, temperament, behaviour to watch and vaccination status.
View pet profile (Xem hồ sơ thú cưng) opens the full pet profile, read only.
Care instructions (Hướng dẫn chăm sóc)
Read only. The version confirmed for this visit and when the customer confirmed it: feeding, behaviour and the tasks, each marked required or not.
When the customer changed them after the pet sitter was assigned, the version is marked not read (chưa đọc) and MSG80 is shown.
I have read the new version (Tôi đã đọc bản mới)
Button. Saves the time the pet sitter read the version and enables Check in.
Home access notes (Ghi chú vào nhà)
Read only. Shown only to the pet sitter of the visit, from 1 hour before the visit until check-out; for a replacement, only after the handover is confirmed (UC-37, UC-41). At other times the notes are hidden and the time they open is shown.
Check in (Check-in)
Button. Opens the check-in step (UC-42). Enabled from 30 minutes before the start (GBR-16), once the confirmed instructions have been read.
Report incident (Báo sự cố)
Button. Opens Report Incident (UC-47).
Withdraw (Rút khỏi buổi)
Button. Opens Withdraw from Visit (UC-82). Less than 12 hours before the visit the request goes to Staff (GBR-21).
Offered visit
For a visit that is only offered, the actions are Accept (Nhận buổi) and Decline (Từ chối), as on My Assignments, and the home access notes are not shown.

11.1.3 Withdraw from Visit

This dialog allows a pet sitter to:
Withdraw in Time: withdraw from an accepted visit 12 hours or more before it starts, with a reason (GBR-21).
Opened with Withdraw (Rút khỏi buổi) on the visit page (View Visit Details, UC-36). Use case: UC-82 Withdraw from Visit (Section II).
Field Description
Field Name
Description
Time left
Read only. The hours left before the visit. With less than 12 hours the next dialog opens instead.
Reason (Lý do)
Required. Chosen from a list.
More details (Ghi chú thêm)
Optional. Free text.
Note
Static text: the visit is offered to another pet sitter with a handover note, and the withdrawal is counted in the pet sitter's record.
Withdraw (Rút khỏi buổi)
Button. Takes the pet sitter off the visit, flags it for handover, offers it to the next pet sitter (GBR-12, JOB-02) and shows MSG57.
Back (Quay lại)
Button. Closes the dialog without any change.

11.1.4 Withdraw from Visit – Less Than 12 Hours

This dialog allows a pet sitter to:
Ask Staff: send a withdrawal request to Staff when the visit starts in less than 12 hours (GBR-21).
Use case: UC-82 Withdraw from Visit, alternative flow 2.1 (Section II). Staff try to replace the pet sitter by hand (UC-38); if nobody can take the visit, they cancel it at no charge (UC-39).
Field Description
Field Name
Description
Note
Static text in red: the visit starts in less than 12 hours, so the request goes to Staff.
Reason (Lý do), More details (Ghi chú thêm)
As in the previous dialog.
Send to Staff (Gửi cho nhân viên)
Button. Puts the request in Staff's Needs action list and shows MSG56. The pet sitter stays on the visit until Staff reassign or cancel it.

11.2 Reassignment
11.2.1 Reassign Pet Sitter

This screen allows Staff to:
See the Visit: see the visit, its current pet sitter and why it needs another one.
Choose Another Pet Sitter: see the other pet sitters ranked with reasons, as on Assign Pet Sitter, and choose one.
Replace: give the visit to the new pet sitter with a reason; everyone concerned is told.
Use case: UC-38 Reassign Sitter. Opened with Reassign (Giao lại) on the Visit Board, for example after a withdrawal less than 12 hours before the visit, a change of working hours or a pet sitter not checked in 30 minutes after the start (GBR-14, GBR-21, GBR-29). If nobody can take the visit, Staff cancel it at no charge instead (UC-39). An Admin can do the same (GBR-03).
Field Description
Field Name
Description
Visit to reassign (Buổi cần đổi người)
Read only. Booking, service, date and time, pets, customer, address, the reason and the time left.
Current pet sitter (Người chăm sóc hiện tại)
Read only. Name, contract type and when he or she accepted the visit. Call (Gọi …) shows the phone number.
Choose a replacement (Chọn người thay)
Table as on Assign Pet Sitter, without the current pet sitter. The same checks apply: Overlap cannot be chosen (MSG46), Call first needs MSG45 (GBR-32).
Reason for the change (Lý do đổi người)
Required. Free text. If it is empty, MSG01 is shown.
Note
Static text: the customer and both pet sitters are told; the new pet sitter must confirm the handover at least 1 hour before the visit (GBR-24).
Change pet sitter (Đổi người chăm sóc)
Button. Checks again as on Assign Pet Sitter (MSG25 if the visit changed meanwhile). Takes the current pet sitter off the visit, gives it to the new one and flags it for handover with a note: the reason, the earlier pet sitter and the time of the change (UC-41). Tells the customer and both pet sitters and shows MSG92.
Back (Quay lại)
Button. Goes back to the Visit Board without any change.

11.3 Dashboard
11.3.1 Pet Sitter Dashboard

This screen allows a pet sitter to:
Answer Offers in Time: see the visit offers waiting for an answer, with the time left.
See Today: see today's visits in the planned order and the shop shifts.
See What Comes Next: see the next visits and open working hours, days off, earnings and pay statements.
Use case: UC-83 View Pet Sitter Dashboard. The first screen after a pet sitter signs in. The order of today's visits is planned by JOB-06 each time the visits of the day change. Numbers and links only, no charts. The names, addresses and times in the mockup are samples.
Field Description
Field Name
Description
Offers waiting (Lời mời chờ trả lời)
Read only list: service, date and time, service area, pets and the time left to answer. View (Xem) opens the offer on My Assignments (UC-35).
Today's visits (Buổi hôm nay, theo thứ tự đi)
Read only list in the planned order: time, customer and address, and status. Clicking a visit opens Visit Details (Pet Sitter).
Shop shifts (Ca ở cửa hàng)
Read only. The next shop shifts of a full-time pet sitter; a part-time pet sitter has none (Không có ca ở cửa hàng) (GBR-45). A shift opens Grooming Work.
Next visits (Buổi sắp tới)
Read only. The number of next visits and the first ones.
Quick links (Đi nhanh)
Working hours and areas (Giờ làm và khu vực) opens Working Hours Calendar, Day off (Xin nghỉ) opens Day Off Request, Earnings (Thu nhập) and Pay statements (Phiếu lương) open the pay screens.

12. Booking Changes and Cancellations (FE-12)
12.1 Change Request
12.1.1 Request Booking Change


This screen allows a customer, and Staff, to:
Ask for a New Time: for the customer: move the whole booking or one visit to another date and time, at the same price.
Answer the Request: for Staff: see the old and the new time and whether the pet sitter is free at the new time, then approve or reject the request.
Use cases: UC-84 Request Booking Change and UC-85 Approve Booking Change (Section II). The first picture is the customer's dialog, opened with Change time (Đổi giờ) on Booking Details. The second picture is the Staff dialog, opened from the Needs action list of the Visit Board or from Booking Details. Only the date and time change, so the price stays the same, and each visit can be moved only once (GBR-22). A request that Staff have not answered 6 hours before the old time or the new time, whichever comes first, expires, and the visits keep their old time (GBR-19, JOB-02). The dates and times in the mockups are samples.
Field Description
Field Name
Description
Change time for (Đổi giờ cho)
Required. The whole booking (Cả lịch đặt) or one visit (Một buổi).
For the whole booking, the customer picks a new first day and new visit times instead of one visit (not in the mockup).
Visit to change (Buổi cần đổi)
Required for one visit. The visits that have not started, with date, time and pet sitter. A visit that starts in less than 12 hours cannot be chosen (GBR-22). A visit that was already moved shows MSG55. A visit that is already in a waiting request shows MSG149 (GBR-22).
New date (Ngày mới), New time (Giờ mới)
Required. The request must be sent at least 12 hours before the old time, for a new time at least 24 hours from now: MSG54 (GBR-22).
The new time must also follow the booking rules: the booking covers at most 30 days (MSG20), a day has at most two visits (MSG150) and visit 2 of a day starts at or after the expected end of visit 1 (MSG148) (GBR-09); a customer who has not paid any bill yet keeps the booking within 7 days (MSG67), and no pet of the booking has another booking at that time (MSG68) (GBR-33).
Price note (Giá không đổi)
Read only. The price of a visit, which does not change, and the time rules.
Back (Quay lại)
Button. Closes the dialog without sending.
Send request (Gửi yêu cầu)
Button. Checks the rules again, saves the request, puts it in Staff's Needs action list and shows MSG53. The visits keep their old time until Staff approve.
Request (Staff dialog)
Read only. Booking, customer and time sent, what is moved, old time, new time and price.
Pet sitter at the new time (Người chăm sóc ở giờ mới)
Read only. For each visit, whether its pet sitter is free (Rảnh giờ mới) or busy (Bận giờ mới) at the new time. If the request is approved, the visit of a busy pet sitter is offered again (GBR-12, JOB-02).
Expiry (Hết hạn)
Read only. The time the request expires: 6 hours before the old time or the new time, whichever comes first; for several visits, the earliest of them (GBR-19).
Reject (Từ chối)
Button. Keeps the old time, tells the customer and shows MSG127.
Approve (Duyệt)
Button. Checks the new time again against GBR-09 and GBR-33, because another booking or another approved change may have come since the request; if a rule fails, shows MSG151, whose reason names the rule in words for Staff (not the customer's message), and moves nothing. Otherwise moves the visits to the new time at the same price and marks each visit as moved once (GBR-22). Keeps the pet sitters who are free at the new time and offers the other visits again. Tells the customer and the pet sitters in the app and shows MSG126.

12.2 Cancellation
12.2.1 Cancel Booking / Visit


This screen allows a customer, and Staff, to:
Cancel: for the customer: cancel the whole booking or one visit, after seeing what it costs.
Cancel at No Charge: for Staff: cancel a visit that still has no pet sitter 12 hours before it starts, or whose pet sitter has not arrived 30 minutes after the start.
Use case: UC-39 Cancel Booking or Visit (Section II). The first picture is the customer's dialog, opened with Cancel booking (Huỷ lịch đặt) on Booking Details. The second picture is the Staff dialog, opened from the Needs action list of the Visit Board. Cancelling a visit 24 hours or more before it starts is free; a later cancellation adds 30% of that visit's price before any discount to the bill. A visit cancelled by Staff costs nothing (GBR-14, GBR-15). A visit that has been checked in cannot be cancelled; the customer can contact Staff or report an incident (UC-47). The dates, times and amounts in the mockups are samples.
Field Description
Field Name
Description
Cancel (Huỷ)
Required. The whole booking (Cả lịch đặt) or one visit (Một buổi). For one visit, the customer chooses the visit (not in the mockup).
Visits to cancel (Các buổi sẽ huỷ)
Read only. Each visit that will be cancelled, with the time left before it starts and its cost: Free (Miễn phí) or the late cancellation fee. A visit that has been checked in is not listed.
Cost (MSG27)
Read only. The total added to the bill, shown with MSG27 before the customer confirms (GBR-15).
Reason (Lý do)
Optional. A reason chosen from the list.
Back (Quay lại)
Button. Closes the dialog without cancelling.
Confirm cancellation (Xác nhận huỷ)
Button. Marks the visits Cancelled, closes their open offers, takes them out of any waiting change request (GBR-19), adds any late cancellation fee to the booking, tells the pet sitters of those visits and shows MSG28.
When every visit of the booking is cancelled, the booking ends: a bill of 0 closes it, otherwise it goes to payment (UC-49).
Visit line (Staff dialog)
Read only. Booking, customer, date and time of the visit.
Reason for cancelling (Lý do huỷ)
Required. No pet sitter with less than 12 hours to go (Chưa có người chăm sóc, còn dưới 12 giờ đến giờ bắt đầu), or the pet sitter has not arrived 30 minutes after the start (Người chăm sóc chưa đến sau giờ bắt đầu 30 phút) (GBR-14).
Message to the customer (Lời nhắn cho khách)
Required: MSG01. Free text, sent to the customer.
Cancel visit (Huỷ buổi)
Button. Cancels the visit without any charge, closes its open offers, takes it out of any waiting change request (GBR-19), tells the customer and shows MSG133.

13. Care Instructions and Handover (FE-13)
13.1 Care Instructions
13.1.1 Visit Care Instructions

This screen allows a customer to:
Pick a Visit: choose a visit of the booking; a visit that has started is locked.
Write the Instructions: write feeding, medicine and behaviour notes for each pet, the tasks of the visit and the home access note.
Confirm: confirm the instructions for this visit and, if wanted, for the later visits of the booking (GBR-23).
Use case: UC-40 Confirm Care Instructions. Also opened while creating a booking (UC-25) and from Booking Details. The pet sitter reads them on the visit page and confirms a new version before check-in (UC-36, GBR-23).
Field Description
Field Name
Description
Visits (Các buổi)
List of the visits of the booking with the version each one keeps.
A visit that has started is locked (Đã khoá); a cancelled visit is marked cancelled.
Version
Read only. Version number, when the customer confirmed it and when the pet sitter read it.
Feeding, Medicine, Behaviour (Cho ăn, Thuốc, Hành vi)
Free text for each pet of the booking. Filled in from the pet profile the first time.
Medicine is for information only: pet sitters do not give medicine (Report 1, LI-03), so it is never a task.
Tasks (Việc cần làm trong buổi)
List of tasks, each marked required or not. Each required task must be done, or skipped with a reason, before check-out (GBR-16). Add task (+ Thêm việc) adds a line.
Home access note (Ghi chú vào nhà)
Optional. Only the pet sitter of the visit sees it, from 1 hour before the visit until check-out (UC-37).
Apply to later visits (Áp dụng cho các buổi sau)
Check box. Saves the same instructions for the later visits of the booking that have not started.
Confirm instructions (Xác nhận hướng dẫn)
Button. Saves a new version and shows MSG51. Less than 2 hours before the visit, the change applies from the next visit (GBR-23).
Discard changes (Huỷ thay đổi)
Button. Goes back to the last confirmed version.

13.2 Handover
13.2.1 Handover Confirmation

This screen allows a replacement pet sitter to:
Read the Handover Note: see why the visit changed pet sitter and who cared for the pets before.
Read the Instructions and Reports: read the confirmed care instructions and the care reports of the earlier visits of the booking.
Confirm: confirm the handover at least 1 hour before the visit (GBR-24).
Use case: UC-41 Confirm Handover (Section II). Opened from the notification or from My Assignments when a visit is flagged for handover: after a withdrawal, a day off, a suspension or a reassignment by Staff (UC-82, UC-79, UC-19, UC-38). If it is not confirmed 1 hour before the visit, the visit goes to Staff's Needs action list; the pet sitter can still confirm while he or she is assigned to the visit (UC-41, alternative flow 3.1). If the customer changes the instructions after the confirmation, the pet sitter confirms the new version on the visit page (GBR-23).
Field Description
Field Name
Description
Deadline note
Static text: the time by which the handover should be confirmed, 1 hour before the visit. After it, Staff are told and may call the pet sitter or send another one.
Handover note (Ghi chú bàn giao)
Read only. Why the pet sitter changed, the earlier pet sitter of the visit and when the visit was given to this pet sitter.
Confirmed care instructions (Hướng dẫn chăm sóc đã xác nhận)
Read only. The version confirmed for this visit, with its number and when the customer confirmed it.
Earlier care reports (Báo cáo các buổi trước)
Table: date, time, pet sitter, short summary. View (Xem) opens the care report (UC-45). The replacement can open the reports of the earlier visits of this booking before confirming (Permission Matrix, note ¹⁹).
I have read (Tôi đã đọc …)
Required check box: I have read the care instructions and the handover note.
Confirm handover (Xác nhận bàn giao)
Button. Enabled when the box is ticked. Saves the time, removes the handover flag, tells Staff and shows MSG81. The home access notes then open 1 hour before the visit, and check-in 30 minutes before (UC-37, GBR-16).
Pets (Thú cưng)
Read only. Name and breed of each pet. View pet profile (Xem hồ sơ thú cưng) opens the full profile.

14. Care Execution and Reporting (FE-14)
14.1 Visit Execution
14.1.1 Visit Check-in / Check-out

This screen allows a pet sitter to:
Check In: record arriving at the customer's home with a photo taken on the phone, from 30 minutes before the visit starts (GBR-16).
Follow the Visit: see the check-in, the checklist and the status the customer sees.
Check Out: record leaving with a photo, once each required checklist item is done or skipped with a reason (GBR-16).
Use cases: UC-42 Check In, UC-43 Check Out (Section II). Opened with Check in on the visit page (UC-36). Only the assigned pet sitter can check in, and a replacement only after confirming the handover (UC-41). The mockup shows a visit after check-in.
Field Description
Field Name
Description
Visit
Read only. Booking code, service, pet, address and live status.
Check-in
Before check-in: the photo, taken with the phone camera, and the Check in button. More than 30 minutes before the start, MSG29 is shown and nothing is saved.
Check in saves the time and the photo, marks the visit Arrived, notifies the customer and shows MSG82.
After check-in: read only, with the time, the pet sitter and the photo.
Checklist
Read only. Each task done or skipped with its reason, and the number of photos and notes.
Open checklist (Mở checklist) opens Visit Checklist and Photos (UC-44).
Check-out photo (Ảnh check-out)
Required. Taken with the phone camera. Retake (Chụp lại) takes it again.
Check-out
Button. If a required item is neither done nor skipped with a reason, MSG30 is shown; if the checklist has no photo and no reason, MSG84. The visit is then not checked out.
Otherwise it saves the time and the photo, marks the visit Finished, sends the care report to the customer (UC-45) and shows MSG83.
Visit status (Trạng thái buổi)
Read only. Assigned (Đã nhận), Arrived (Đã đến), In progress (Đang làm), Finished (Đã xong), Report ready (Có báo cáo), as the customer sees them (UC-78).
Check-in makes the visit Arrived; the first save of the checklist makes it In progress; check-out makes it Finished, and the care report makes it Report ready.
Times (Thời gian)
Read only. Planned time, check-in time and check-out time.
Report incident (Báo sự cố)
Button. Opens Report Incident (UC-47). A pet sitter who cannot get into the home reports an incident instead of checking in.

14.1.2 Visit Checklist and Photos

This screen allows a pet sitter to:
Tick the Tasks: tick each task of the visit, or skip a task with a reason.
Add Photos: add at least one photo, or give the reason when no photo can be uploaded.
Write a Note: write a note for the customer, which goes into the care report.
Use case: UC-44 Complete Visit Checklist. Opened from the visit page after check-in (UC-42). The tasks come from the care instructions the customer confirmed for this visit (UC-40).

Description
Tasks (Việc cần làm)
One line for each task of the confirmed instructions, marked Required (Bắt buộc) or not.
Check box. Ticked when the task is done.
Skip reason (Lý do bỏ qua)
Required when a required task is skipped. Free text. The task is marked Skipped (Bỏ qua).
Photos (Ảnh)
At least one photo, taken with the phone during the visit. Add photo (Thêm ảnh) adds one.
Cannot upload a photo (Không tải được ảnh?)
Link. Opens a text box for the reason. The visit can then be checked out without a photo, and Staff see that it has no photo.
Note for the customer (Ghi chú cho khách)
Optional. Free text, shown in the care report.
Customer's instructions (Hướng dẫn của khách)
Read only. The version confirmed for this visit.
Save checklist (Lưu checklist)
Button. Saves the checklist and shows MSG85. It can be saved many times during the visit.
The first save after check-in marks the visit In progress.
Go to check-out (Sang bước check-out)
Button. Opens the check-out step (UC-43). With no photo and no reason, MSG84 is shown.
Report incident (Báo sự cố)
Button. Opens Report Incident (UC-47).

14.1.3 Dog Walk Live Location

See the Dog: see on a map where the pet sitter and the dog are during a dog walk.

14.2 Care Report
