15. Incident Handling (FE-15)
15.1 Incident Reporting
15.1.1 Report Incident

This screen allows a pet sitter or a customer to:
Say What Happened: choose the type of problem and describe it, with photos if any.
Send It to Staff: send the report at once, so that Staff can act and the bill of the booking waits.
Use case: UC-47 Report Incident (Section II). A pet sitter opens it with Report incident (Báo sự cố) on the visit page, the check-in step or the checklist (UC-36, UC-42, UC-44); a pet sitter who cannot get into the home reports an incident instead of checking in. A customer opens it from the visit (UC-28). Only the pet sitter and the customer of the visit can report, on the day of the visit or just after it. A customer who is not happy with the result later sends a complaint instead (UC-52). The mockup shows the pet sitter's view.
Field Description
Field Name
Description
Visit line
Read only. Date, time, booking, service, pets and address of the visit.
Type of incident (Loại sự cố)
Required. One of Sick or injured pet (Thú cưng ốm hoặc bị thương), Cannot get into the home (Không vào được nhà), Damage (Hư hỏng đồ đạc), Pet lost (Thú cưng bị lạc), Other (Khác).
Choosing Sick or injured pet shows MSG43 with Paw World's hotline. Pet sitters do not treat pets (Report 1, LI-03).
Description (Mô tả)
Required. Free text. If it is empty, MSG01 is shown and nothing is saved.
Photos (Ảnh)
Optional. Add photo (Thêm ảnh) adds one.
Send report (Gửi báo cáo)
Button. Saves the incident as Open, puts the bill of the booking on hold (GBR-17), tells Staff and the other party of the visit in the app and shows MSG93.
Cancel (Huỷ)
Button. Goes back to the visit without saving.
Notes (Lưu ý)
Static text: the hotline for an emergency, that pet sitters give no medicine and no treatment, and that the bill waits until the result of the incident is final.
Pets (Thú cưng)
Read only. Name and breed of each pet of the visit.

15.2 Incident Management
15.2.1 Incident List

This screen allows Staff to:
See the Incidents: see the open, in progress and resolved incidents, with the visit, the type and who reported them.
Find an Incident: search by incident code, booking code, customer or pet sitter, and filter by type.
Open an Incident: open an incident to follow it up.
Use case: UC-86 View Incidents. Opened from Incidents (Sự cố) in the Staff menu or from the Staff Dashboard (UC-81). An Admin can do the same (GBR-03).
Field Description
Field Name
Description
Tabs
Open (Đang mở), In progress (Đang xử lý), Resolved (Đã xử lý) and All (Tất cả), each with its count.
Search
Free text: incident code, booking code, customer or pet sitter.
Type filter (Tất cả loại sự cố)
All types, or one of the five types of Report Incident.
Incidents
Read only table: code, visit (booking, date, time, customer), type, reported by (name and role), reported at, status. 20 incidents a page.
Incidents not resolved come first, by the time they were reported. An incident open more than 3 days shows how long it has been open, in red, as on the dashboards (GBR-26).
When nothing matches, MSG94 is shown.
View (Xem)
Link. Opens Incident Details and Resolution (UC-48).

15.2.2 Incident Details and Resolution

This screen allows Staff to:
Read the Report: see the visit, the type, the description and the photos, and open the care report of the visit.
Record the Actions: write down each action taken, such as calls to the customer and the pet sitter.
Decide the Result: record the result, decide whether the visit is charged and propose any amount to pay back.
Resolve: close the incident, or send the proposed amount to the Admin.
Use case: UC-48 Resolve Incident (Section II). Opened with View (Xem) on Incident List. The bill of the booking waits until the result of the incident is final (GBR-17, GBR-26). The amounts in the mockup are samples.
Field Description
Field Name
Description
Header
Read only. Incident code, visit line, customer and pet sitter, the status (Open, In progress or Resolved) and whether the bill of the booking is on hold.
Incident report (Báo cáo sự cố)
Read only. Type, who reported it and when, description and photos.
View the care report of the visit (Xem Care Report của buổi) opens Care Report (UC-45).
Actions taken (Các bước xử lý)
Read only list: time, Staff member and text of each action, oldest first.
Add an action (Thêm bước xử lý)
Free text. Save action (Lưu bước) adds it to the list and shows MSG51. The first action marks the incident In progress.
Result (Kết quả)
Required to resolve. Free text, sent to the customer and the pet sitter.
Charge this visit (Tính tiền buổi này)
Required. Yes (Có, tính tiền buổi như bình thường) or No (Không, đề xuất không tính tiền buổi này).
Staff cannot waive the price themselves: No adds to the amount proposed to the Admin the part of the bill that comes from this visit, after the discount for many visits and the tier discount (GBR-17, GBR-20, GBR-30). The same visit is not taken off twice.
Amount to pay back (Số tiền đề xuất trả lại)
Optional. For example vet costs. With the amounts already approved for the booking, the amount is at most its bill, or its expected bill before the bill is made; above it, MSG44 is shown and nothing is sent (GBR-30).
Reason and evidence (Lý do và bằng chứng)
Required when there is an amount. Free text and files (+ Thêm tệp).
Propose to suspend the pet sitter (Đề xuất Admin tạm ngưng người chăm sóc)
Link. When the pet sitter was at fault, sends a suspension proposal to the Admin (UC-19).
Resolve (Xử lý xong)
Button. Without an amount, marks the incident Resolved, sends the result to the customer and the pet sitter and shows MSG96.
With an amount, sends it to the Admin (UC-51) and shows MSG95; the incident stays In progress and becomes Resolved when the Admin approves, lowers or rejects the amount.
In both cases the bill keeps waiting until the result is final: the customer has not disagreed within 72 hours, or the Admin has decided after the customer disagreed. It is released only when the results of all incidents and complaints about the booking are final (GBR-26).
Contacts (Liên hệ)
Read only. Name and phone number of the customer and the pet sitter of the visit.
Bill (Hoá đơn)
Read only. Booking code, the bill, or the expected bill while it is not made (GBR-30), and the state of the bill (waiting until the result of every incident and complaint of the booking is final).

16. Payment Processing (FE-16)
16.1 Payment
16.1.1 Booking Payment

This screen allows a customer to:
See the Bill: see the visits done, the discounts, the late cancellation fees and the amounts approved for complaints or incidents.
Add a Tip: add a tip for the pet sitters who did the visits.
Pay Online: pay the bill through payOS by scanning a VietQR code, within 48 hours.
Use case: UC-49 Pay for Booking (Section II). Opened from the notification of the bill, from the email of the bill when the customer has an email, and from Booking Details. The bill is made when the booking ends, that is when its last visit is finished or cancelled (GBR-17) and is due 48 hours after it is final. While the result of a complaint or an incident about the booking is not final, the bill is on hold and shows MSG34 (not in the mockup); a booking already Overdue stays Overdue and keeps its due time (GBR-30). A payment is recorded only once (GBR-18). A customer with an overdue bill cannot make a new booking until he or she pays (GBR-11). The amounts in the mockup are samples.
Field Description
Field Name
Description
Back (Quay lại Lịch đặt …)
Link. Goes back to Booking Details.
Bill line
Read only. Booking code, service, dates, due date and the bill status: Waiting for payment (Chờ thanh toán), On hold, Paid or Overdue.
Bill (Hoá đơn)
Read only. Visits done and their price, the discount for many visits worked out again on the visits done, the tier discount (GBR-42), late cancellation fees, the amounts the Admin approved for complaints or incidents, and the total (GBR-17).
The same bill is emailed when the customer has an email.
Tip (Tiền tip cho người chăm sóc)
Optional. None (Không), a suggested amount or another amount (Số khác). A tip is shared equally among the pet sitters who did the visits of the booking.
Amount (Số tiền)
Read only. The bill plus the tip.
Pay with payOS (Thanh toán qua payOS)
Button. Opens the payOS checkout page with a VietQR code. If payOS cannot create the link: MSG33.
When payOS confirms the payment, the booking is marked Paid and MSG31 is shown. A failed or cancelled payment shows MSG32, and the customer can pay again.
Due note
Static text: the due date, and the reminder 24 hours before it (JOB-03).

16.1.2 Unpaid Bookings

This screen allows Staff to:
See the Unpaid Bills: see the bookings whose bill is waiting for payment or overdue, with the amount and the due date.
Contact the Customer: see the customer's phone number and open the booking.
Use case: UC-50 View Unpaid Bookings. Opened from Unpaid bills (Hoá đơn chưa trả) in the Staff menu and from the Staff Dashboard. A bill is due 48 hours after it is final (GBR-17). A booking is marked overdue only after payOS confirms that the bill is not paid; nothing is collected automatically, and Paw World collects the money outside PWC (GBR-18, JOB-03). A bill on hold, while the result of a complaint or an incident is not final, is not listed. The names, numbers and amounts in the mockup are samples.
Field Description
Field Name
Description
Tabs
All (Tất cả), Waiting for payment (Chờ thanh toán) and Overdue (Quá hạn), each with its count.
Search (Tìm theo mã lịch đặt, tên khách hoặc số điện thoại)
Free text. No booking matches: MSG137.
Unpaid table
Read only: booking code, customer, phone number, amount of the bill, due date and time, and status Waiting for payment (Chờ thanh toán) or Overdue (Quá hạn). Overdue bills come first.
View (Xem)
Link. Opens Booking Details (UC-27).
Note
Static text: PWC does not collect money from an overdue customer, and bills on hold are not listed.

17. Refund Processing (FE-17)
17.1 Refund
17.1.1 Refund Processing

This screen allows an Admin to:
See the Proposals: see every amount Staff proposed for an incident or a complaint.
Decide an Amount: approve the amount, lower it or reject it with a reason.
See Order Refunds: see the refunds of product orders already paid: prepaid orders that were cancelled or returned, and faulty product claims refunded, cash on delivery included, which Paw World's accounting pays back outside PWC.
Use case: UC-51 Process Refund (Section II). Opened from Refunds (Hoàn tiền) in the Admin menu and from the notification of a new proposal. The total paid back for a booking is at most its bill; before the bill is made, at most the expected bill: the bill worked out as in GBR-17 on the visits not cancelled (done or still to come), with the discounts that apply and the late cancellation fees so far, before the amounts approved are taken off. A new amount, together with the amounts the Admin has already approved for the booking, cannot be more than this limit. If the bill is not paid yet, the amount is taken off it; if it is paid, a refund is recorded and Paw World's accounting pays it outside PWC (GBR-30). The tab Order refunds (Hoàn tiền đơn hàng) lists the refunds of product orders already paid, cash on delivery included (GBR-37, GBR-49) (not in the mockup). The codes, names and amounts in the mockup are samples.
Field Description
Field Name
Description
Tabs
Waiting (Chờ duyệt), Decided (Đã quyết) and Order refunds (Hoàn tiền đơn hàng), each with its count.
Proposal list
Read only. The case (complaint or incident), customer, booking, amount and time sent, oldest first. Choosing a row opens it on the right.
Proposal details
Read only. Booking, bill and its state (not made yet, unpaid or paid; while it is not made, the expected bill, GBR-30), amounts already approved for the booking, the Staff member who proposed it and when, and the amount proposed.
Reason and evidence (Lý do và bằng chứng)
Read only. The text and files from Staff, with links to the customer's photos, the complaint or incident and the care report.
Approved amount (Số tiền duyệt)
Required to approve. A whole number of đồng, at most the amount proposed. With the amounts already approved for the booking, it cannot be more than its bill, or its expected bill before the bill is made: MSG44 (GBR-30).
Reason for rejecting (Lý do từ chối)
Required to reject: MSG01. Free text, sent to Staff.
How it is paid
Static text: taken off an unpaid bill, or recorded as a refund for a paid bill.
Reject (Từ chối)
Button. Closes the proposal without any change to the bill and tells Staff; the incident or the complaint is then marked Resolved and the customer gets the result without an amount. Shows MSG135.
Approve (Duyệt)
Button. Takes the approved amount off the bill, or records a refund if the bill is paid. Closes the proposal, tells Staff and the customer and shows MSG134. If the Admin lowered the amount, Staff see the change.

18. Ratings and Complaints (FE-18)
18.1 Rating
18.1.1 Rate Visit

This screen allows a customer to:
Rate the Visit: give a finished visit from 1 to 5 stars.
Leave a Comment: write a short comment about the visit.
Use case: UC-87 Rate Visit. A dialog opened with Rate visit (Đánh giá buổi) on Care Report. A customer can rate a finished visit once, within 7 days after it ends; pet sitters do not rate customers. The rating shows on the pet sitter's profile and in Pet Sitter List (GBR-48). The names and dates in the mockup are samples.
Field Description
Field Name
Description
Visit line
Read only. Service, date and time of the visit, and pet sitter.
Stars (Số sao)
Required: MSG01. From 1 to 5.
Comment (Nhận xét)
Optional. Free text.
Note
Static text: one rating per visit, the last date and time to rate, and where the rating shows.
Later (Để sau)
Button. Closes the dialog without saving.
Send rating (Gửi đánh giá)
Button. Saves the rating and shows MSG138. Rate visit is then hidden on Care Report (GBR-48).

18.2 Complaint
18.2.1 Submit Complaint

This screen allows a customer to:
Complain: send a complaint about a finished visit or a whole booking, with photos if needed.
Follow the Complaints: see his or her complaints with their status and result.
Disagree: disagree once with a result, within 3 days after receiving it.
Use case: UC-52 Submit Complaint (Section II). Opened with Send complaint (Gửi khiếu nại) on Care Report or Booking Details. A complaint about a visit can be sent within 7 days after it ends; for a visit closed by Staff without check-out, from Booking Details within 7 days after it was closed. A complaint about the whole booking can be sent within 7 days after the booking ends (its last visit is finished or cancelled); when every visit was cancelled, only if its bill is above 0. There is one open complaint per visit and one open complaint about the whole booking (GBR-26). While the result of a complaint is not final, the bill of the booking waits (GBR-17). A problem during a visit is reported as an incident instead (UC-47). The codes, texts and amounts in the mockup are samples.
Field Description
Field Name
Description
Back (Quay lại Báo cáo chăm sóc)
Link. Goes back to the screen the form was opened from.
Visit line
Read only. Service, date and time of the visit, pet sitter and booking code. For a complaint about the whole booking when every visit was cancelled, it shows the booking code, the service and the dates of the booking instead.
Complaint about (Khiếu nại về)
Required. This visit (Buổi này) or the whole booking (Cả lịch đặt). The whole booking is shown only within 7 days after the booking ends (its last visit is finished or cancelled; when every visit was cancelled, only if its bill is above 0), and the booking has no open complaint about the whole booking. When the visit already has an open complaint, only the whole booking can be chosen.
What went wrong (Điều chưa ổn)
Required: MSG01. Free text, up to 1,000 characters.
Photos (Ảnh)
Optional. The tile with + adds a photo.
Note
Static text: the date and time until which a complaint can be sent, 7 days after the visit ends or Staff closed it (for the whole booking, after the booking ends), and that the bill waits while the result is not final.
Cancel (Huỷ)
Button. Goes back without sending.
Send complaint (Gửi khiếu nại)
Button. More than 7 days after the visit ended or Staff closed it, or after the booking ended for the whole booking: MSG41. The visit, or the booking for the whole booking, already has an open complaint: MSG42 (GBR-26).
Otherwise saves the complaint as Open, puts the booking On hold if it is Awaiting payment (a booking already Overdue stays Overdue), tells Staff and shows MSG128.
My complaints (Khiếu nại của tôi)
Read only list, newest first: code, the visit or the booking, status and result. Status: Open (Đang mở), Resolved (Đã có kết quả), Escalated (Chuyển quản trị) or Final (Kết quả cuối). Final is a Resolved complaint that can no longer change: 72 hours passed without a disagreement, or the Admin made the final decision. A complaint with an amount stays Open until the Admin has decided the amount.
Disagree (Không đồng ý)
Button. Shown once on a Resolved complaint, for 3 days (72 hours) after the result is received. Opens a text box for the reason (not in the mockup). Sending it marks the complaint Escalated, sends it to the Admin for the final decision and shows MSG129 (GBR-26).
Without a disagreement in 72 hours, the result is final.

18.2.2 Complaint Management

This screen allows Staff, and an Admin, to:
See the Complaints: see the open, escalated and resolved complaints, and how long each has been open.
Handle a Complaint: read the complaint with its booking, visit and care report, then write the handling notes and the result.
Propose an Amount: propose an amount to pay back, which the Admin approves, lowers or rejects (UC-51).
Decide an Escalated Complaint: for the Admin: make the final decision when the customer disagrees.
Use case: UC-53 Handle Complaint (Section II). Opened from Complaints (Khiếu nại) in the Staff menu and from the notification of a new complaint. The mockup shows the Staff view of an open complaint. An Admin opens an Escalated complaint on the same screen, reads the customer's reason and records the final result; if the Admin changes the amount, the bill or the refund is worked out again (not in the mockup). No deadline stops the handling of a case; complaints open more than 3 days are marked here and shown apart on the Admin Dashboard (GBR-26). The codes, names and amounts in the mockup are samples.
Field Description
Field Name
Description
Tabs
Open (Đang mở), Escalated (Chuyển quản trị) and Resolved (Đã giải quyết), each with its count.
Search (Tìm theo mã khiếu nại, mã lịch đặt hoặc tên khách)
Free text. No complaint matches: MSG130.
Order (Mới gửi trước)
Newest first or oldest first.
Complaint list
Read only. Code, customer, booking, the visit or the whole booking, time sent and status. A complaint open more than 3 days shows the number of days (Mở … ngày). Choosing a row opens it on the right.
Complaint details
Read only. Code, status, customer and phone number, booking, the visit or booking complained about, time sent and the customer's text.
Links
Customer's photos (Ảnh của khách) opens the photos. View care report (Xem báo cáo chăm sóc) opens Care Report (UC-45). View booking (Xem lịch đặt) opens Booking Details (UC-27).
Handling notes (Ghi chú xử lý)
Required to resolve: MSG01. Free text for Staff and the Admin, for example calls to the customer or the pet sitter.
Result for the customer (Kết quả gửi khách)
Required to resolve: MSG01. Free text, sent to the customer.
Amount to pay back (Số tiền đề xuất hoàn)
Optional. A whole number of đồng. The bill of the booking is shown next to it, or its expected bill (dự kiến) while the bill is not made. With the amounts already approved for the booking, the amount is at most that bill; above it, MSG44 is shown and nothing is sent (GBR-30).
Reason and evidence (Lý do và bằng chứng)
Required when there is an amount: MSG01. Free text and files (+ Thêm tệp) (GBR-30).
Bill note
Static text: the bill of the booking waits until the result is final.
Resolve (Giải quyết)
Button. Without an amount, marks the complaint Resolved, sends the result to the customer and shows MSG131.
With an amount, sends the amount to the Admin (UC-51) and shows MSG147; the complaint stays Open. When the Admin approves, lowers or rejects it, PWC marks the complaint Resolved and sends the result with the amount decided to the customer; the 72 hours to disagree start then (GBR-26).
The bill is released only when the results of all complaints and incidents about the booking are final: the customer has not disagreed within 72 hours, or the Admin has decided (GBR-26).

21. Product Catalogue (FE-21)

Opened from the Home Page and from Products (Sản phẩm) in the header. Only the products the Admin shows are listed (UC-57). A product is In stock when the stock on hand minus the quantity held by unfinished orders is more than zero, and Out of stock (Hết hàng) when it is not (GBR-35). Field Description
Field Name
Description
Photos (Ảnh sản phẩm)
Read only. The photos of the product.
Name, category and pet type
Read only. Taken from the catalogue (UC-57).
Stock state (Còn hàng, Hết hàng)
Read only. When the product is out of stock, Add to cart cannot be used and MSG98 is shown (GBR-35).
Your price (Giá của bạn)
Read only. The selling price. For a customer who gets a tier discount: the price after the discount, the selling price crossed out and the name of the tier (GBR-42).
Quantity (Số lượng)
Required. A whole number from 1, changed with − and +. More than the quantity available: MSG58.
Add to cart (Thêm vào giỏ hàng)
Button. Puts the quantity in the cart (UC-61) and shows MSG99. A guest is asked to log in or register first. The quantity of the product in the cart cannot be more than the quantity available: MSG58.
Description (Mô tả sản phẩm)
Read only. The description, the product code (Mã sản phẩm), the category (Danh mục) and the pet type (Dành cho).
Back (Quay lại Sản phẩm)
Link. Goes back to Product List.

21.2 Product Management
21.2.1 Product Management

This page allows an Admin to:
Find a Product: search the products by name or code and filter them by category and pet type.
Add or Edit a Product: enter the name, category, pet type, description, photos and selling price of a product (UC-57).
Show or Hide a Product: choose whether guests and customers see the product on Product List (UC-55).
Use case: UC-57 Manage Products. Opened from Products (Sản phẩm) in the Admin menu. The table shows every product, shown or hidden; choosing a row opens it on the right. PWC gives the product code when the product is first saved, and the code does not change. A new product is saved hidden, and the Admin shows it when it can be sold. A product must be in the catalogue before its goods can be received (GBR-39). There is no delete: the Admin hides the product. The stock is not changed here; it changes only with orders, goods receipts and stock adjustments (GBR-35, GBR-39, GBR-50). The products, codes and prices in the mockup are samples.
Field Description
Field Name
Description
Search (Tìm theo tên hoặc mã sản phẩm)
Free text: a part of the name or the code. No product matches: MSG97.
Category and pet type filters (Tất cả danh mục, Tất cả thú cưng)
Category: All, Food (Thức ăn) or Accessories (Phụ kiện). Pet type: All, Dog (Chó) or Cat (Mèo).
Products
Read only table: photo, name and code, category, pet type (Dành cho), selling price and Shown (Đang hiện) or Hidden (Đang ẩn).
Add product (+ Thêm sản phẩm)
Button. Opens an empty form on the right (not in the mockup).
Product code (Mã sản phẩm)
Read only. Given by PWC when the product is first saved.
Product name (Tên sản phẩm)
Required: MSG01.
Category (Danh mục), Pet type (Dành cho)
Required. Food or Accessories; Dog or Cat.
Selling price (Giá bán)
Required. A whole number of đồng greater than 0: MSG113. A new price counts for orders placed after it is saved; orders already placed keep their price.
Description (Mô tả)
Free text. Shown on Product Details (UC-56).
Photos (Ảnh sản phẩm)
The photos shown on Product List and Product Details. The tile with + adds a photo.
Show on Product List (Hiện trên trang Sản phẩm)
Switch. On: the product is Shown. Off: the product is Hidden, after MSG114. Orders already placed are not changed.
Save changes (Lưu thay đổi)
Button. Checks the fields, saves the product and shows MSG115.

22. Shop Grooming Appointments (FE-22)
22.1 Grooming Booking
22.1.1 Grooming Booking

This screen allows a customer to:
Choose Pets and Services: choose the pets to bring and the services for each pet.
Answer the Questions: answer short questions about each pet (GBR-43).
Pick a Time: choose a day and a free time slot (GBR-40, GBR-45).
Check the Final Price: see the price of each pet, the tier discount and the total, then confirm (GBR-42).
Use case: UC-58 Book Grooming Appointment (Section II). Nothing is paid now; the customer pays at the shop when the pets are handed back (UC-60).
Field Description
Field Name
Description
Pets (Thú cưng)
Required. One box for each pet of the customer; at least one.
Services
Required for each chosen pet: at least one of the shop services the Admin has set.
Pet questions
Required. Fixed answers. When the answers show a very risky pet, MSG63 is shown and the pet cannot be booked online (GBR-40).
Day and time slot (Chọn giờ hẹn)
Required. Only the slots in which a pet sitter with grooming skills is free on a shop shift are shown; a full slot is greyed out (GBR-40, GBR-45).
Final price (Giá cuối)
Read only. The price of each service by pet size, the price of each pet, the tier discount when the customer gets one (GBR-42) and the total.
Confirm booking (Xác nhận đặt lịch)
Button. Saves the appointment, holds its place in the slot and shows MSG65. If the slot was just taken, MSG64. If a chosen pet already has a shop appointment at that time, MSG152. If the price changed after it was shown, MSG153 and the new price.

22.2 Shop Check-in
22.2.1 Grooming Appointments

This screen allows Staff to:
See the Day: see today's shop appointments and their state.
Check Pets In: take photos of each pet, record its condition compared with the customer's answers, confirm its difficulty level and give it to a pet sitter with grooming skills on shift.
Handle the Exceptions: add a listed surcharge when the customer agrees, cancel a pet at no charge, or record a no-show.
Use case: UC-59 Check In Pet at Shop (Section II). Opened from Shop schedule (Lịch spa) in the Staff menu. The price shown when booking is the final price; it changes only when the pet is different from the customer's answers, only by a surcharge in the price list, and only if the customer agrees; otherwise the pet is cancelled at no charge (GBR-40). The difficulty level of each pet is confirmed at check-in (GBR-43). The names, times and amounts in the mockup are samples.
Field Description
Field Name
Description
Tabs
Today (Hôm nay), In progress (Đang làm) and Ready to hand back (Sẵn sàng trả), each with its count.
Appointment list
Read only: time, customer, number of pets and state (Not arrived, Arrived, In progress, Ready to hand back, Paid). Choosing a row opens it on the right. A ready appointment opens Grooming Pick-up and Payment (UC-60).
Appointment line
Read only. Customer, phone number, when it was booked and the final price given.
Customer's answers (Khách khai)
Read only, for each pet.
Photos (Ảnh)
Required, for each pet. The tile with + adds a photo.
Condition compared with the answers (Tình trạng so với khách khai)
Required: MSG01. Free text, for each pet. The difficulty level is confirmed here (not in the mockup).
Surcharge (Phụ phí)
Shown when the pet is different from the answers: the surcharge from the price list and the customer's answer. Yes (Có) adds it to the pet's price; No (Không) cancels the pet at no charge, and the other pets go on.
Given to (Giao cho)
Required, for each pet. A pet sitter with grooming skills on the current shop shift.
Record no-show (Ghi khách không đến)
Button, shown only while the customer has not arrived (not in the mockup). When the customer has not come in the allowed time, records a no-show for the customer and shows MSG142 (GBR-40).
Check in and assign (Check-in và giao việc)
Button. Records the check-in, shows each pet in the Grooming Work of its pet sitter and shows MSG143.

22.2.2 Grooming Pick-up and Payment

This screen allows Staff to:
See What to Collect: see the price of each pet, the tier discount and the tip.
Take the Payment: take the payment in cash or by VietQR at the counter.
Hand Back the Pets: mark each pet handed back and close the appointment.
Use case: UC-60 Hand Back Pet and Take Payment (Section II). Opened from Grooming Appointments when every pet of the appointment has ended: ready, cancelled or stopped. The customer is told when the pets are ready. Pets are handed back only when the amount is paid in full; if no pet is ready, the appointment is closed with nothing to pay (GBR-40). The names and amounts in the mockup are samples.
Field Description
Field Name
Description
Appointment line
Read only. Time and date of the appointment, customer and phone number.
Pets (Thú cưng)
Read only. Each pet with its services, the pet sitter who groomed it and its state Ready (Sẵn sàng), Stopped or Cancelled.
Check box Handed back (Đã trả thú) for each pet; stopped and cancelled pets are handed back too.
Amount (Thanh toán tại quầy)
Read only. The price of each ready pet by pet size, the tier discount (GBR-42), the tip and the amount to collect. A stopped or cancelled pet is not charged.
Way of paying (Cách trả)
Required. Cash (Tiền mặt) or VietQR.
Tip (Tiền tip)
Optional. A whole number of đồng. A tip is shared equally among the pet sitters who did the work.
Cash given (Khách đưa)
For cash. The change (Tiền thối) is worked out from it.
Confirm paid (Xác nhận đã thu …)
Button. For cash, records the payment and any tip once and shows MSG139. For VietQR, shows the payOS VietQR code; when payOS confirms, the payment is recorded once. A failed VietQR payment shows MSG32, and the customer pays again or pays another way.
Hand back and close (Trả thú và đóng lịch hẹn)
Button. Turned off until the amount is paid in full and every pet is marked handed back. Then closes the appointment and shows MSG140.
Note
Static text: pets are handed back only when the amount is paid in full.

22.3 Grooming
22.3.1 Grooming Work

This screen allows a pet sitter on a shop shift to:
See the Pets: see the pets given to him or her, with the services and the customer's answers.
Mark the Work: mark each pet in progress, then ready.
Stop a Pet: stop a pet that is too dangerous to go on, with the reason.
Note the Pet: note in the pet profile anything harder than declared.
Use case: UC-91 Update Grooming Status (Section II). Opened from the shop shift on the Pet Sitter Dashboard. A pet appears here once Staff check it in and give it to the pet sitter (UC-59). When every pet of the appointment has ended, the customer is told to collect the pets (UC-60). A pet found harder keeps its level for this appointment; the level goes up from the next booking (GBR-40, GBR-43). The names and times in the mockup are samples.
Field Description
Field Name
Description
Shift line
Read only. Time and date of the shop shift and the number of pets given to the pet sitter.
Pet card
Read only. Pet, services, customer, check-in time, state Waiting (Chờ làm), In progress (Đang làm), Ready or Stopped, and the customer's answers with the notes of the check-in.
Start (Bắt đầu)
Button. Marks the pet In progress.
Done, ready (Xong, sẵn sàng)
Button. Marks the pet Ready and shows MSG144.
Stop as dangerous (Dừng vì nguy hiểm)
Button. Opens a text box for the reason (Required: MSG01) (not in the mockup). Marks the pet Stopped, adds the reason to the pet profile and shows MSG145 (GBR-40).
Note in the pet profile (Ghi vào hồ sơ thú cưng)
Free text. Save note (Lưu ghi chú) adds it to the pet profile and shows MSG51 (GBR-43).

23. Online Product Orders and Delivery (FE-23)
23.1 Online Orders
23.1.1 Shopping Cart

This page allows a customer to:
Check the Cart: see each product with its price, its quantity and the amount of its line.
Change the Cart: change a quantity or take a product out.
See the Total: see the total with the tier discount (GBR-42) and go on to the checkout.
Use case: UC-61 Manage Shopping Cart. Opened from the cart icon (Giỏ hàng) in the header, which shows the number of products in the cart, and from Add to cart on Product Details. The shipping fee is not in the total here: it is worked out on Product Checkout for a delivery (UC-62). Nothing is held for the customer until the order is placed (GBR-35). The amounts in the mockup are samples.
Field Description
Field Name
Description
Product line
Read only. Photo, name, product code (Mã sản phẩm), price per item after the tier discount with the selling price crossed out, quantity and the amount of the line.
Quantity (Số lượng)
Required. A whole number from 1, changed with − and +. More than the quantity available: MSG58 (GBR-35).
Remove (trash icon)
Icon button. Takes the product out of the cart. When the last product is taken out, MSG100 is shown.
Subtotal (Tạm tính)
Read only. The sum of the selling prices of all lines.
Tier discount (Giảm giá hạng)
Read only. The discount of the tier of the customer, named in the label (GBR-42). None while the customer is blocked from it.
Total (Tổng)
Read only. The subtotal minus the tier discount.
Check out (Thanh toán)
Button. Opens Product Checkout (UC-62).
Continue shopping (Tiếp tục mua sắm)
Link. Opens Product List.

23.1.2 Product Checkout

This page allows a customer to:
Choose How to Receive: choose delivery to an address in a service area, or pick-up at the shop (GBR-10).
Choose How to Pay: pay in advance through payOS, in cash on delivery, or at the shop on pick-up (GBR-36).
Place the Order: check the total, with the tier discount and the shipping fee, and place the order (GBR-35, GBR-42).
Use case: UC-62 Place Product Order (Section II). Opened from Shopping Cart. The mockup shows a delivery paid in advance; for a pick-up the address and the shipping fee are not shown. Cash on delivery and payment at the shop are not offered to a customer over the refusal limit, who then sees MSG60 and gets no tier discount (GBR-36, GBR-42). Placing the order holds the quantities (GBR-35). For payment in advance the customer then pays on the payOS page with a VietQR code; an order not paid by the payment deadline is cancelled (JOB-04, GBR-36). The shipping fee comes from the service area the Admin sets (UC-23); the amounts in the mockup are samples.
Field Description
Field Name
Description
How to receive (Cách nhận hàng)
Required. Delivery (Giao tận nơi) or pick-up at the shop (Nhận tại cửa hàng).
Delivery address (Địa chỉ nhận hàng)
Required for a delivery: one of the addresses of the customer. An address outside the service areas: MSG18. None chosen: MSG101. The order keeps a copy of the address.
Under the box: the service area of the address, which sets the shipping fee.
Way to pay (Cách thanh toán)
Required. For a delivery: in advance through payOS (Thanh toán trước qua payOS) or cash on delivery (Thanh toán khi nhận hàng). For a pick-up: in advance, or at the shop on pick-up (Trả tại cửa hàng khi lấy hàng).
A customer over the refusal limit can only pay in advance: MSG60 (GBR-36).
Your order (Đơn hàng của bạn)
Read only. Each product with its quantity and the amount at the selling price, the subtotal, the tier discount (GBR-42), the shipping fee of the service area for a delivery, and the total.
Place order (Đặt hàng)
Button. Checks each quantity (MSG58 when one is more than the quantity available, and the order is not placed), creates the order, holds the quantities and shows MSG59. For payment in advance it opens the payOS page; when the link cannot be made, MSG33 is shown.
Back (Quay lại giỏ hàng)
Link. Goes back to Shopping Cart without placing the order.

23.1.3 My Orders

This page allows a customer to:
See the Orders: see the product orders with their code, date, way to receive, total and status.
Open an Order: see the items, the payment, the status and the carrier's tracking code of one order.
Report a Faulty Product: report a faulty or wrong product of an order (GBR-49).
Use case: UC-63 View My Orders. A customer sees only his or her own orders (GBR-02). The list and the detail of the chosen order are on the same page; View (Xem) chooses the order. Opened from the Customer Dashboard. The status of a delivery is Placed, Handed to the carrier (Đang vận chuyển), Delivered (Đã giao hàng), or Being returned (Đang hoàn về) and Returned (Đã hoàn về); the status of a pick-up is Placed, Ready (Sẵn sàng nhận) and Picked up (Đã nhận hàng). An order not paid or not collected in time is Cancelled (Đã huỷ) (JOB-04). The picture shows a pick-up order that is ready; the order codes, dates and amounts in it are samples.
Field Description
Field Name
Description
Orders (Đơn hàng của tôi)
Read only table: order code (Mã đơn), date (Ngày đặt), way to receive (Cách nhận), total (Tổng tiền) and status (Trạng thái). View (Xem) shows the order on the right. No order yet: MSG102.
Under the table: the number of orders shown and the page buttons.
Status (Trạng thái)
Read only. One of the statuses above.
Order details
Read only. Code, status, date, way to receive, the address for a delivery, and the payment: the way to pay and whether it is paid (Đã thanh toán, Chưa thanh toán).
Tracking code (Mã vận đơn)
Read only. Shown after Sale Staff hand the parcel to the carrier (UC-64). A pick-up has none.
Items and amounts
Read only. Each product with its quantity and the amount at the selling price when ordered, the subtotal (Tạm tính), the tier discount (not in the picture), the shipping fee for a delivery and the total (Tổng).
Report faulty product (Báo sản phẩm lỗi)
Button (not in the picture). Opens Faulty Product Claims for this order (UC-92). After the time set by the Admin, MSG61 is shown (GBR-49).

23.2 Order Handling
23.2.1 Order Management


This screen allows a Sale Staff member or an Admin to:
Find Orders: see the online orders by tab and search them by order code, customer name or phone number.
Ship a Delivery: enter the tracking code after booking the delivery on the carrier's site, and mark the parcel handed to the carrier (UC-64).
Record the Carrier's Status: mark a delivery delivered, with cash on delivery collected, or being returned with the carrier's reason (UC-65).
Confirm a Returned Order: check the goods of a returned parcel and record the quantity that can be sold again (UC-66).
Hand Over a Pick-up Order: mark a pick-up order ready, then hand it over at the shop when it is paid in full (UC-67).
Use cases: UC-64 Prepare and Ship Order, UC-65 Update Delivery Status, UC-66 Confirm Returned Order, UC-67 Hand Over Pick-up Order (Section II). Opened from Product orders (Đơn hàng sản phẩm) in the menu and from the Sale Staff Dashboard. The first picture shows the tab To prepare and a delivery paid by cash on delivery; the second is the dialog that confirms a returned order. A prepaid order that is not paid yet cannot be prepared (UC-64). The stock on hand goes down only when the goods leave the shop, and a returned parcel adds back only the quantity that can be sold again, once for each order (GBR-35). A refusal counts against the customer only when he or she refused the parcel or did not collect the order, and a prepaid order that was paid and is then cancelled or returned is marked for a refund (GBR-36, GBR-37). The names, codes and amounts in the pictures are samples.
Field Description
Field Name
Description
Tabs
To prepare (Cần chuẩn bị), Waiting for pick-up (Chờ khách lấy), Out for delivery (Đang giao) and Being returned (Đang hoàn về), each with its count, and All (Tất cả).
Search (Tìm theo mã đơn, tên khách hoặc số điện thoại)
Free text: order code, customer name or phone number.
Orders
Read only table: order code, customer, way to receive, payment and total, with View (Xem). Choosing a row shows the order on the right.
Under the table: the number of orders shown and the page buttons.
Order details
Read only. Code, status, customer and phone number, way to receive, address for a delivery, payment and whether it is collected, items, shipping fee and total (Tổng thu khi giao, the amount to collect for cash on delivery).
Tracking code (Mã vận đơn)
Required before a delivery is marked handed to the carrier: MSG103. The customer sees it in My Orders.
Help text: book the delivery on the carrier's site, enter the tracking code, then hand the parcel to the carrier.
Handed to the carrier (Đã giao cho hãng vận chuyển)
Button, for a delivery that is placed and, when paid in advance, paid. Ends the hold, takes the goods off the stock on hand (GBR-35) and shows MSG106.
Delivered, Being returned
Buttons in the tab Out for delivery (not in the picture). Delivered records the cash on delivery as collected; Being returned asks for the carrier's reason, which is required. The stock does not change here (UC-65).
Mark ready, Hand over
Buttons for a pick-up order (not in the picture). Mark ready tells the customer that the order can be collected until the pick-up deadline. Hand over works only when the order is paid in full, otherwise MSG105 (GBR-36): the customer pays in cash, confirmed by Sale Staff, or by VietQR (MSG32 when it fails). Handing over ends the hold and takes the goods off the stock on hand (GBR-35).
Carrier's reason (Lý do của hãng)
Read only in the dialog: the reason recorded when the delivery was marked Being returned.
Reason (Lý do hoàn về)
Required in the dialog. One of three reasons: Customer refused (Khách từ chối nhận), which counts as one refusal (GBR-36); Carrier could not deliver (Hãng không giao được); Paw World sent the wrong goods or faulty goods (Paw World giao sai hoặc hàng lỗi). For a prepaid order, PWC only marks the refund and keeps the reason; Paw World's accounting works out the amount and pays it outside PWC (GBR-37).
Quantity that can be sold again (Số bán lại được)
Required for each product, with the quantity returned shown above it (Số lượng hoàn về). A whole number from 0 to the quantity returned: MSG104.
Note (Ghi chú)
Optional. Free text.
Confirm returned (Xác nhận đã hoàn về)
Button. Adds that quantity back to the stock on hand, marks the order Returned and, for a prepaid order, marks it for a refund; shows MSG106. It is not shown for an order already confirmed returned (UC-66).
Cancel (Huỷ) or Close (Đóng) closes the dialog without any change.

23.2.2 Faulty Product Claims


This screen allows a customer, a Sale Staff member or an Admin to:
Report a Problem: the customer reports a faulty or wrong product of an order with photos (UC-92).
Check a Claim: the Sale Staff member checks the photos and the order and writes what was found (UC-93).
Decide: the Admin approves an exchange for the same product, approves a refund or rejects the claim (UC-93).
Use cases: UC-92 Report Faulty Product, UC-93 Handle Faulty Product Claim (Section II). The first mockup is the view of a Sale Staff member with the tab Waiting for check; the second is the dialog the customer opens from My Orders. The Admin sees the same page and decides the claims in the tab Waiting for the Admin. An approved exchange waits in the tab Done until the Sale Staff record the new goods sent. A claim can be sent only within the time set by the Admin (MSG61). Products are not taken back when the customer changes his or her mind, and faulty goods are not added back to the stock on hand (GBR-49). The new goods of an exchange leave the stock on hand; a refund is paid by Paw World's accounting outside PWC. The claim and its codes are samples.
Field Description
Field Name
Description
Tabs
Waiting for check (Chờ kiểm tra), Waiting for the Admin (Chờ Admin duyệt) and Done (Đã xử lý), each with its count.
Claims
Read only table: claim code, order, customer and the date of the report.
Claim details
Read only. Order and its delivery date, customer and phone number, product and quantity, the problem, the customer's description and photos.
What was found (Kết quả kiểm tra)
Required before sending: MSG107.
Send to the Admin (Gửi Admin duyệt)
Button. Moves the claim to Waiting for the Admin and shows MSG108.
Admin decision
Buttons for the Admin (not in the mockup): approve an exchange, approve a refund, or reject. Saves the decision, tells the customer and shows MSG109. For an exchange the Sale Staff send new goods of the same product.
Exchange to send (Chờ gửi hàng đổi)
Read only label (not in the mockup) on a claim in the tab Done whose exchange is approved and not sent yet.
Exchange sent (Đã gửi hàng đổi)
Button for the Sale Staff (not in the mockup). Shown only when the quantity available covers the quantity of the claim (GBR-35).
Takes the new goods off the stock on hand (GBR-49), records who sent them and when, marks the claim Exchange sent and tells the customer.
Product (Sản phẩm)
Required in the customer's dialog. One product of the order.
Quantity (Số lượng)
Required. From 1 to the quantity ordered.
Problem (Vấn đề)
Required. Faulty product (Sản phẩm lỗi) or wrong product (Giao sai sản phẩm).
Description (Mô tả)
Free text.
Photos (Ảnh)
Required. At least one photo; Add photo (Thêm ảnh) adds one.
Send report (Gửi báo lỗi)
Button. Saves the claim, tells the Sale Staff and shows MSG62. After the time set by the Admin: MSG61 (GBR-49).
