24. Counter Sales (FE-24)
24.1 Counter Sales
24.1.1 Counter Sale


This screen allows a Sale Staff member or an Admin to:
Look Up Products: search the products and see the selling price and the quantity available (GBR-35).
Create the Order: find the customer by phone number or email for the tier discount, add the products and create the order, which holds the stock (GBR-38, GBR-42).
Take the Payment: take cash or a VietQR payment and complete the sale, or cancel the order, which only ends the hold (GBR-38).
Use case: UC-68 Sell at Counter (Section II). The first mockup shows an order created for a customer found by phone number and waiting for cash (Chờ thanh toán); the second shows the same order after the cash is recorded (Đã thanh toán), with Complete sale. The customer is optional: without one, the order gets no tier discount and does not count toward a tier (GBR-42). When the payment is not complete, the customer can pay again or change the way to pay before the order is cancelled (GBR-38). The codes, prices, stock figures and amounts in the mockup are samples.
Field Description
Field Name
Description
Product search (Tra sản phẩm)
Free text: product name or code.
Products
Read only: name, code, selling price (Giá bán) and quantity available (Có thể bán). Add (Thêm) puts one item in the order. A product with nothing available shows Out of stock (Hết hàng) and cannot be added.
Customer (Số điện thoại hoặc email của khách)
Optional. Find (Tìm) looks up the customer: when found, the name and the tier are shown; when not found, MSG110
Order lines
Quantity changed with − and +. More than the quantity available: MSG58.
Subtotal, tier discount, total
Read only (GBR-42).
Create order (Tạo đơn)
Button, before the order exists (not in the mockup). Creates the order, holds the quantities and shows the total (GBR-35).
Way to pay (Cách thanh toán)
Required. Cash (Tiền mặt) or VietQR. VietQR shows the payOS code; a failed payment shows MSG32.
Cash received (Đã nhận đủ tiền mặt)
Button. Records the cash payment once; a VietQR payment is recorded when payOS sends the result (GBR-18). The order becomes Paid (Đã thanh toán).
Complete sale (Hoàn tất bán)
Button, after the order is paid (second mockup). The Sale Staff member hands the goods to the customer and completes the sale: the hold ends, the goods leave the stock on hand (GBR-35, GBR-38) and MSG111 is shown.
Cancel order (Huỷ đơn)
Button, while the order is not paid. Ends the hold; the stock on hand does not change. Shows MSG112.

25. Suppliers and Stock (FE-25)
25.1 Suppliers
25.1.1 Supplier Management

This page allows an Admin to:
See the Suppliers: see the suppliers by status, with the contact and the number of products each one supplies.
Add or Edit a Supplier: enter the name, the contact and the products the supplier delivers (UC-69).
Stop Working with a Supplier: make a supplier inactive, or active again.
Use case: UC-69 Manage Suppliers. Opened from Suppliers (Nhà cung cấp) in the Admin menu. Choosing a row or Edit (Sửa) opens the supplier on the right. An inactive supplier cannot be chosen on a new goods receipt; its goods receipts and the stock history stay as they are (UC-70, UC-71). PWC sends no orders to suppliers: the Admin orders more goods outside PWC. The names and contacts in the picture are samples.
Field Description
Field Name
Description
Tabs
Active (Đang hợp tác), Inactive (Ngừng hợp tác) and All (Tất cả), each with its count.
Suppliers
Read only table: name and contact person, phone number, number of products supplied and status, with Edit (Sửa) on each row.
Under the table: the number of suppliers shown and the page buttons.
Add supplier (+ Thêm nhà cung cấp)
Button. Opens an empty form on the right.
Close (Đóng)
Link. Closes the form without saving.
Supplier name (Tên nhà cung cấp)
Required: MSG01. Two suppliers, active or inactive, cannot have the same name: MSG116.
Contact person (Người liên hệ)
Free text.
Phone number (Số điện thoại), Email
At least one of the two is required: MSG125. A phone number that is not valid: MSG03. An email that is not valid: MSG02.
Products supplied (Sản phẩm cung cấp)
Products of the catalogue (UC-57) that the supplier delivers, each with its code and name. Add product (+ Thêm sản phẩm) adds one; the cross takes one out.
Save changes (Lưu thay đổi)
Button. Saves the supplier and shows MSG117.
Stop working (Ngừng hợp tác)
Button. After MSG118, the supplier becomes Inactive. An inactive supplier has Work again (Hợp tác lại) instead (not in the picture).

25.2 Stock
25.2.1 Goods Receipt

This page allows a Sale Staff member or an Admin to:
Record a Delivery: choose the supplier and enter, for each product, the quantity received and the quantity accepted (UC-70).
Record Rejected Goods: give the reason for the goods that are rejected, which are reported to the Admin (GBR-39).
See Recent Receipts: see the last goods receipts with the quantities received and rejected.
Use case: UC-70 Record Goods Receipt (Section II). Opened from Goods Receipt (Nhập hàng) in the menu. The Sale Staff member checks the goods when the supplier delivers them and records the real counts. Every delivery gets a receipt, even when everything is rejected, and the stock on hand goes up only by the accepted quantity (GBR-39). Rejected goods go back to the supplier outside PWC; a make-up delivery is a new receipt. A saved receipt is never edited: the Admin corrects a mistake with a stock adjustment (UC-95, GBR-50). The codes, suppliers and quantities in the mockup are samples.
Field Description
Field Name
Description
Supplier (Nhà cung cấp)
Required. One of the active suppliers (UC-69).
Date received (Ngày nhận), Recorded by (Người ghi)
Read only. Today's date and the signed-in user.
Product lines
One line for each product: name and code, quantity received, quantity accepted, quantity rejected and the reason.
Quantity received (Số nhận)
Required. A whole number greater than 0: MSG113.
Quantity accepted (Nhận vào kho)
Required. A whole number from 0 to the quantity received: MSG119.
Quantity rejected (Từ chối)
Read only. The quantity received minus the quantity accepted, so the quantity received is always the quantity accepted plus the quantity rejected.
Reason for rejection (Lý do từ chối)
Free text. Required when the quantity rejected is more than 0: MSG120.
Add product (Thêm sản phẩm)
Link. Adds a line for a product of the catalogue, shown or hidden. A product that is not in the catalogue must first be added by the Admin (UC-57, GBR-39).
Totals (Nhận vào kho, Từ chối)
Read only. The sums of the accepted and the rejected quantities.
Save goods receipt (Lưu phiếu nhập)
Button. Saves the receipt with a new code, adds the accepted quantities to the stock on hand (GBR-39) and shows MSG121. A receipt where everything is rejected is saved too, and the stock does not change. For each product line with a quantity rejected more than 0, the Admin gets MSG122 (UC-88, GBR-46).
Recent goods receipts (Phiếu nhập gần đây)
Read only. Code, date, supplier, and the quantities received and rejected of the last receipts. When the Admin opens MSG122 from Notification Center, this page shows that receipt with its lines (not in the mockup).

25.2.2 Stock List

This page allows a Sale Staff member or an Admin to:
See the Stock: see for each product the stock on hand, the quantity held by unfinished orders and the quantity available (GBR-35).
Find Low-Stock Products: see which products are low in stock or out of stock.
See the History: see the goods receipts and stock adjustments of a product (UC-70, UC-95).
Use case: UC-71 View Stock. Opened from Stock (Tồn kho) in the menu, from the Sale Staff Dashboard and, for the Admin, from Stock Adjustment. The quantity available is the stock on hand minus the quantity held by unfinished orders (GBR-35). The low-stock threshold is one fixed value shared by all products. PWC compares each product's quantity available (stock on hand minus quantity held by unfinished orders) with this threshold. A product is counted as low in stock when its quantity available is at or below the threshold, including products that are out of stock. There is no settings screen, and this document does not specify the numeric threshold. The Sale Staff Dashboard counts the same products (UC-94). The Sale Staff member tells the Admin, who orders more goods outside PWC. The numbers in the mockup are samples.
Field Description
Field Name
Description
Tabs
All (Tất cả) and Low in stock (Sắp hết hàng), each with its count.
Search (Tìm theo tên hoặc mã sản phẩm)
Free text: a part of the name or the code. No product matches: MSG97.
Stock table
Read only: product name and code, stock on hand (Tồn thực tế), quantity held (Đang giữ), quantity available (Có thể bán) and status, for shown and hidden products. Choosing a row shows its history on the right.
Status (Trạng thái)
Read only. In stock (Còn hàng), Low in stock (Sắp hết hàng) or Out of stock (Hết hàng). A product with a quantity available of 0 shows Out of stock and is also listed in the tab Low in stock.
History of receipts and adjustments (Lịch sử nhập và điều chỉnh)
Read only. The stock numbers of the chosen product, then its goods receipts and stock adjustments, newest first: code, date, supplier or reason, and the quantity added or taken off. Goods that leave with orders are not listed.

25.2.3 Stock Adjustment

This page allows an Admin to:
Adjust the Stock: increase or decrease the stock on hand of one product, with a reason (UC-95, GBR-50).
See Past Adjustments: see the adjustments already saved, which are never edited.
Use case: UC-95 Adjust Stock. Opened from Stock adjustment (Điều chỉnh tồn kho) in the Admin menu. Outside orders, goods receipts and the new goods of a faulty product exchange (GBR-49), only an Admin changes the stock on hand, always with a reason: damaged, lost, internal use or wrong goods receipt (GBR-50). A saved adjustment is never edited; a mistake is corrected with a new adjustment. The stock on hand cannot go below zero, and a decrease cannot take goods held by unfinished orders: it is at most the quantity available. An adjustment does not release held goods or change any order. This is not a stock count. Faulty goods of a claim are not adjusted here (GBR-49). The codes and numbers in the mockup are samples.
Field Description
Field Name
Description
Product (Sản phẩm)
Required. One product of the catalogue, shown or hidden.
Stock numbers
Read only. Stock on hand (Tồn thực tế), quantity held (Đang giữ) and quantity available (Có thể bán) of the chosen product (GBR-35).
Type (Loại điều chỉnh)
Required. Decrease (Giảm tồn) or increase (Tăng tồn).
Quantity (Số lượng)
Required. A whole number greater than 0: MSG113. A decrease cannot be more than the quantity available, checked again when the adjustment is saved: MSG123. An increase has no such limit.
Reason (Lý do)
Required. Damaged (Hàng hỏng), lost (Mất), internal use (Dùng nội bộ) or wrong goods receipt (Phiếu nhập ghi sai).
Stock on hand after saving (Tồn thực tế sau khi lưu)
Read only.
Save adjustment (Lưu phiếu điều chỉnh)
Button. Saves the adjustment with a new code, changes the stock on hand and shows MSG124.
Saved adjustments (Phiếu đã ghi)
Read only. Code, date, product, reason, Admin and the quantity added or taken off, newest first.
View stock (Xem tồn kho)
Button. Opens Stock List (UC-71).

26. Background Job Inventory
26.1 Send Visit Reminders (JOB-01)
Trigger: Scheduled — every 15 minutes
Purpose: Reminds the pet sitter and the customer of each visit that starts within the next 2 hours.
Processing steps:
Find the visits that start within the next 2 hours, have a pet sitter and have not been reminded yet.
For each visit, create one in-app notification for the pet sitter and one for the customer, with the time, the address and the pets.
Mark the visit as reminded, so that it is never reminded twice.
Failure behavior:
Scenario
System behavior
A notification cannot be saved (database error)
The visit is not marked as reminded; the next run tries again.
The visit gets a new pet sitter after the reminder
The new pet sitter is reminded in the next run; the customer is not reminded again.
No visit starts in the next 2 hours
The job ends at once with no action; this is normal.

26.2 Offer Visits and Flag Visits for Staff (JOB-02)
Trigger: Event — when a visit is created or sent back; Scheduled — every 5 minutes
Purpose: Fills visits with pet sitters automatically and puts the visits that need Staff in the Needs action list (GBR-12, GBR-14, GBR-19, GBR-29).
Processing steps:
When a visit is created or sent back: leave out the pet sitters who are off, outside their service areas or busy (travel time included), rank the others (the pet sitter the customer asked for first when he or she can take the visit; then cared for the customer's pets before, rating from customers, fewer visits that day, weighed by the ranking algorithm; never a pet sitter who already declined the visit or let its offer expire, also before the visit was sent back) and send the offer with its answer deadline to the first one, or at once to all the pet sitters with the same score at that place (GBR-12).
Every 5 minutes: close the offers whose answer time has passed as Expired; when no offer of the visit is still open, send it to the next pet sitter, or to the next pet sitters with the same score; when nobody is left, put the visit in Needs action.
Put in Needs action the visits that start within 24 hours without a pet sitter (the offers go on, with 30 minutes to answer), the visits whose pet sitter has not checked in 30 minutes after the start, the visits not checked out 2 hours after their end and the handovers not confirmed 1 hour before the visit.
Expire the booking change requests that Staff have not handled 6 hours before the old time or the new time, whichever comes first (for several visits, the earliest of them), and tell the customer; the visits keep their old time (GBR-19).
Failure behavior:
Scenario
System behavior
A pet sitter accepts at the moment the job passes the visit on
The first one saved keeps the visit (GBR-13); the other open offers of the visit are closed as Withdrawn.
No pet sitter can be ranked (all off, outside their service areas or busy)
The visit goes straight to Needs action.
Database error during a run
Nothing is half saved for a visit; the next run, 5 minutes later, takes the same visits again.

26.3 Send Payment Reminders and Mark Overdue (JOB-03)
Trigger: Scheduled — every hour
Purpose: Reminds customers before the payment deadline, marks the bookings that are not paid in time as Overdue, and works out the bill again when the results of the incidents and complaints of a booking On hold become final (GBR-11, GBR-17, GBR-18, GBR-26).
Processing steps:
Find the bookings Awaiting payment whose due time is within 24 hours and that have not been reminded; send an in-app reminder, and an email when the customer has an email.
Find the bookings Awaiting payment whose due time has passed; skip the bookings On hold (a complaint or an incident is open, or its result is not final yet, GBR-26).
For each of them, ask payOS for the payment status (API-02): if it is paid, record the payment once and mark the booking Paid; if not, mark the booking Overdue and tell the customer.
Find the bookings On hold whose incidents and complaints all have a final result (no disagreement within 72 hours after the customer received it, or the Admin's final decision, GBR-26). For each of them, once: work out the bill again with the amounts the Admin approved (GBR-30); if nothing is left to pay, close the booking; otherwise mark it Awaiting payment, due 48 hours after the moment the last result became final (not the time of the run), and tell the customer in the app, and by email when he or she has one. A booking already Overdue is not On hold and keeps its due time.
Failure behavior:
Scenario
System behavior
payOS cannot be reached
Nothing is marked Overdue in this run; the job tries again the next hour (GBR-18).
The email service fails
The in-app reminder is still sent; the email is sent again in the next run.
The payment result arrives while the job runs
The payment is recorded only once (GBR-18); a booking that is already Paid is skipped.
The job runs again, or two runs overlap, for the same booking
The bill is worked out only once: a booking that is no longer On hold is skipped, so there is no second notice and the due time does not move.

26.4 Cancel Expired Product Orders (JOB-04)
Trigger: Scheduled — every 5 minutes
Purpose: Cancels the product orders that are not paid or not collected in time, so that their goods can be sold again (JOB-04, GBR-35, GBR-36).
Processing steps:
Find the prepaid orders not paid by the payment deadline. For each of them, ask payOS for the payment status (API-02): if it is paid, record the payment once and keep the order; if not, cancel the order. The order is cancelled only if it is still unpaid at the moment the cancellation is saved.
Find the pick-up orders not collected by the pick-up deadline and cancel them. Count a refusal against the customer (GBR-36); a pick-up order that was paid in advance is marked for a refund (GBR-37).
For each cancelled order, end its hold so the quantity is available again (GBR-35), and tell the customer in the app.
Failure behavior:
Scenario
System behavior
payOS cannot be reached
The prepaid orders are not cancelled in this run; the next run, 5 minutes later, tries again.
The payment result arrives while the job runs
The payment is recorded only once. An order paid before the cancellation is saved is kept, not cancelled.
Database error during a run
Nothing is half saved for an order; the next run takes the same orders again.

26.5 Update Customer Tiers (JOB-05)
Trigger: Scheduled — every night
Purpose: Sets the tier of each customer from what he or she paid in the last 12 months (JOB-05, GBR-42).
Processing steps:
For each customer, add up what he or she paid in the last 12 months for services and products: booking bills, product orders, and counter sales where the customer gave a phone number or an email.
Compare the total with the tier limits and save the tier: Member, Silver or Gold. Until the settings screen is built, the limits are fixed values in the system.
A customer who is blocked (overdue bill, or may only pay in advance) keeps the tier; the tier discount is left out when a price is worked out (GBR-42).
Failure behavior:
Scenario
System behavior
Database error during a run
No tier is saved from an incomplete total, so no customer is moved to a lower tier by mistake. The tiers already saved stay; the other customers keep their old tier until the next night.
A payment is recorded while the job runs
It counts from the next night.
A customer has paid nothing in the last 12 months
The tier is Member; this is normal.

26.6 Plan the Daily Visit Order (JOB-06)
Trigger: Event — when the visits of a pet sitter on a day change
Purpose: Puts the visits of a pet sitter on a day in an order that keeps travel between homes short (JOB-06).
Processing steps:
When a visit is accepted, assigned, withdrawn, cancelled or moved, take the visits of that pet sitter on that day.
Put them in an order that keeps travel between homes short, with each visit inside its time window. This document does not fix how the order is worked out; the visit order algorithm sets it.
Save the order and show it in My Assignments and on the Pet Sitter Dashboard. PWC gives no turn-by-turn directions; the pet sitter uses his or her own map app.
Failure behavior:
Scenario
System behavior
The order cannot be worked out (for example a missing location)
Until an order is saved, the visits are shown by start time. This is only a display order, not a planned route.
Two changes for the same pet sitter come close together
The saved order always matches the latest visits of that day: a run that started before a later change never replaces the order worked out for that change.
Database error
The old order stays, and the job tries again 5 minutes later with the visits as they are then.

26.7 Alert Staff to Late Visits (JOB-07)
Trigger: Scheduled — every 5 minutes
Purpose: Tells Staff about the visits whose pet sitter has not checked in 15 minutes after the start (JOB-07, GBR-29, GBR-47).
Processing steps:
Find today's visits that have a pet sitter, have not been checked in, started 15 minutes ago or more and have not been alerted yet.
For each visit, send one in-app notification to Staff with the time, the customer and the pet sitter. Because the job runs every 5 minutes, Staff are told at most 5 minutes after the 15-minute mark.
Mark the visit as alerted, so that it is never alerted twice. At 30 minutes the visit goes to Needs action (JOB-02).
Failure behavior:
Scenario
System behavior
A notification cannot be saved (database error)
The visit is not marked as alerted; the next run tries again.
The pet sitter checks in before the run
The visit is skipped; no alert is sent.
No visit is late
The job ends at once with no action; this is normal.

27. External API Inventory
27.1 payOS – Create Payment Link (API-01)
URL
POST: https://api-merchant.payos.vn/v2/payment-requests
Purpose
Creates a payment link with a VietQR code for the bill of a booking or a prepaid product order, and the VietQR code for a payment at the counter; a link ends at its payment deadline.
Caller
PWC server (UC-49, UC-60, UC-62, UC-68)
Auth
Yes: x-client-id and x-api-key headers; the request data is signed with the checksum key (HMAC-SHA256).

27.2 payOS – Get Payment Status (API-02)
URL
GET: https://api-merchant.payos.vn/v2/payment-requests/{orderCode}
Purpose
Reads the status of a payment before a booking is marked Overdue or a prepaid product order is cancelled.
Caller
PWC server (JOB-03, JOB-04)
Auth
Yes: x-client-id and x-api-key headers.

27.3 payOS Payment Webhook (API-03)
URL
POST: /api/v1/payments/payos/webhook
Purpose
Receives the result of a payment for a booking bill, a product order or a counter payment from payOS and records it once (GBR-18).
Caller
payOS payment gateway
Auth
Signature in the request checked with the checksum key (HMAC-SHA256); a request with a wrong signature is refused.

27.4 Email Service – Send Email (API-04)
URL
POST: send-email endpoint of the email provider (HTTPS)
Purpose
Sends sign-in codes, codes to confirm an email address, account emails, bills and payment reminders.
Caller
PWC server
Auth
Yes: API key of the email provider, kept on the server only.

27.5 SMS Gateway – Send SMS (API-05)
URL
POST: send-SMS endpoint of the SMS gateway (HTTPS)
Purpose
Sends sign-in codes and codes to confirm a phone number.
Caller
PWC server
Auth
Yes: API key of the SMS gateway, kept on the server only.

IV. Non-Functional Requirements
1. External Interfaces
User interface: PWC runs in a web browser on phones and computers (current Chrome, Edge, Safari and Firefox), on screens from 360 px wide. Pet sitters use it mostly on phones during visits, including the camera for check-in, check-out and visit photos.
payOS: PWC creates payment links and reads payment status through the payOS API over HTTPS, and receives payment results on a webhook signed with the checksum key (Section I.5.2).
Email service and SMS gateway: PWC sends sign-in codes and codes to confirm an email address, the application receipt and result emails, account emails, bills and payment reminders through an email service, and codes to sign in and to confirm a phone number through an SMS gateway. Both are called over HTTPS with an API key kept on the server (External API Inventory, Section III); the providers are chosen with the deployment in Report 4 and can be changed without changing the requirements.
Hardware: No special hardware. The only device feature used is the phone camera.
2. Quality Attributes
Each requirement below has a target that can be checked, and its reason comes from how Paw World works.
2.1 Usability
Requirement
Target
Condition
First booking
A first-time customer can make a booking of up to 3 visits within 5 minutes without help.
Acceptance test with at least 5 people who have not used PWC
Check-in and check-out
A pet sitter can check in or check out within 30 seconds and at most 3 taps, photo included.
On a phone, during acceptance test
Needs action
A Staff member can open a visit in the Needs action list and assign it by hand within 2 minutes.
Planning board with at least 20 visits that day
Phone screens
Every screen can be used on a 360 px wide screen without scrolling sideways.
Checked on every screen in system test
Interface language
All PWC-controlled user-facing screen labels, buttons, in-app messages, emails and SMS messages are in Vietnamese.
Checked during system testing against the approved mockups and message templates.

2.2 Performance
How the numbers were found. Paw World is a business the team assumes (Report 1), so the load is worked out from an assumed size for the first year in Hanoi: 40 active pet sitters doing at most 5 visits a day each, that is 200 visits a day at full capacity. On a normal day about half of that is used. Before Tet pet hotels are full (news reports cited in Report 1, Section II), so the busiest days are taken as full capacity: about 200 customers with a visit that day, about 100 customers making or changing a booking, 40 pet sitters and 6 Staff and Admins, that is about 350 users in the day. If 40% of them are active in the busiest hour, about 140 people use PWC at the same time; the target below is 200, which leaves some margin.
Requirement
Target
Condition
Page load time
95% of pages load within 2 seconds
Up to 200 users active at the same time
Confirming a booking
95% of bookings are saved and confirmed within 3 seconds after Confirm
Up to 200 users active at the same time
First offer
The first offer of a visit is sent within 1 minute after the visit is created or sent back
Any time
Expired offers
An offer whose answer time has ended is passed to the next pet sitter within 5 minutes
Job runs every 5 minutes
Payment result
A booking is marked Paid within 10 seconds after payOS sends the result
Normal load
Sign-in code
The code is handed to the email service or SMS gateway within 5 seconds after the request
Delivery time then depends on the provider
Users at the same time
PWC works fully with 200 users active at the same time
Single server deployment

2.3 Security
Requirement
Description
Sign-in required
Every page and action other than the public pages requires the user to be signed in (GBR-01).
Access by role
The rights of each role follow the Permission Matrix (I.4.4) and are checked by the server on every page and API request, not only by hiding menus or buttons (GBR-02). A refused request is not carried out and shows MSG15.
No passwords
Users sign in only with one-time codes sent to a confirmed email or phone number (GBR-07). Codes must never be stored or written to logs in a form that a person can read.
Session end
A session ends after the time in GBR-08, when the user signs out, and at once when an Admin locks the account (GBR-04).
Data in transit
All pages and API calls use HTTPS.
Home access notes
Among pet sitters, door codes and other home access notes are shown only to the pet sitter assigned to the visit, from 1 hour before the visit until check-out, and to a replacement pet sitter only after he or she has confirmed the handover. Staff and Admins see them only while handling that visit: a visit in Needs action, a late visit or a handover (data rule Private access notes, Permission Matrix note 2, UC-37).
Payment details
PWC never sees or stores bank or card details: customers pay on the payOS page. A payment result is accepted only with a valid payOS signature.
Application documents
ID card photos and other documents of pet sitter applicants can be opened only by Admins, and the system checks the right on every download; no document can be reached through a public or guessable link.
Visit photos
Check-in, check-out and visit photos can be opened only by the customer of the visit, its pet sitter, Staff and Admins.
Public application form
The pet sitter application form needs no account, so each file must be a JPG, PNG or PDF of at most 5 MB, and at most 3 applications an hour are accepted from one device or network (MSG49, MSG50).
Pet sitter earnings
A pet sitter's earnings can be seen only by that pet sitter and by Admins (UC-20).

2.4 Availability
Requirement
Description
Monthly uptime
PWC is available at least 99% of each month, that is at most about 7 hours down a month, measured by an uptime monitor. Reason: pet sitters check in and customers follow visits every day, Tet included.
Planned maintenance
Planned maintenance happens between 00:00 and 05:00, the quietest hours, lasts at most 1 hour and is announced to users one day before.

2.5 Reliability and Data Consistency
Requirement
Description
One pet sitter per visit
In a test where a pet sitter accepts and Staff assign the same visit at the same moment, and in one where two Staff members assign the same visit at the same moment, each repeated 50 times, every visit ends with exactly one pet sitter (GBR-13). This needs the visit to be locked while it is being given to someone.
Payment recorded once
When payOS sends the same result up to 5 times, the payment is recorded once: 0 duplicate payments (GBR-18).
Correct bills
For every booking in the test data, the bill worked out again from its visits, late cancellation fees and the amounts approved by an Admin for incidents and complaints equals the stored bill.
No overdue by mistake
No booking is marked overdue while a complaint or an incident about it is open or while payOS cannot be reached.
Bill cap
In the test data, no booking has a total amount approved to pay back that is higher than its bill: 0 cases (GBR-30).

V. Requirement Appendix
1. Business Rules
GBR-ID
Rule Statement
Applies To
Rationale
Enforced By
GBR-01
Every page and action needs the user to be signed in, except the public pages: home page, service list and details, service areas and policies, registration, the pet sitter application form and sign-in.
All pages and actions
Protects the personal data of customers and pet sitters.
System, on every request
GBR-02
A user can only open the pages and do the actions that the Permission Matrix (I.4.4) gives his or her role. “Restricted” rights are checked against the record itself, for example the customer's own booking or the pet sitter's assigned visit.
All pages, actions and APIs
A customer or pet sitter sees only his or her own data, even when typing a link by hand.
System, on every request
GBR-03
An Admin can do everything a Staff member can do.
Staff pages and actions
The manager can cover for the coordinator.
System, on every role check
GBR-04
A locked Customer, Staff or Sale Staff account cannot sign in, and its open sessions end at once. Pet sitter accounts are not locked: a suspended pet sitter can still sign in to see past visits and earnings but gets no new visits.
Sign-in and every request
Locking an account takes effect immediately.
System, at sign-in and on every request
GBR-05
An email address or a phone number can belong to only one account, whatever the role.
Registering a customer, sending a pet sitter application, creating Staff and pet sitter accounts, adding or changing the email or phone number
Sign-in codes must reach only one person.
System, when an email or phone number is saved
GBR-06
A new email or phone number is added, or replaces the old one, only after the user enters the code sent to it. Until then the account keeps what it had.
Adding or changing the email or phone number
A typing mistake cannot lock the user out.
System, when the code is checked
GBR-07
A one-time code has 6 digits and is valid for 5 minutes. A new code can be sent after 60 seconds and the previous code then stops working; at most 5 codes are sent to one email or phone number in an hour. After 5 wrong codes, that email or phone number is blocked for 15 minutes.
Verify OTP: registration, sign-in, adding or changing the email or phone number
Makes codes hard to guess and keeps the SMS cost down.
System, when codes are sent and checked
GBR-08
A session lasts 30 days for Customers and Pet Sitters and 12 hours for Staff, Sale Staff and Admins. After that, or after signing out, the user must sign in again.
All signed-in users
Staff and Admins see more personal data, so their sessions are shorter.
System, on every request
GBR-09
A booking must start at least 24 hours after it is made and can cover at most 30 days; it has one or two visit times a day, and visit 2 starts at or after the expected end of visit 1 (its start time plus the length of a visit).
Creating a booking
Leaves time to find a pet sitter and keeps the bill small enough to pay after the visits.
System, when a booking is saved
GBR-10
Only an address in a service area can be chosen for a booking or for a delivery. Other addresses can be saved but are marked Not served.
Creating a booking, placing a delivery order, saving an address
Pet sitters only work in the service areas Paw World serves, and the shipping fee depends on the service area.
System, when a booking, an order or an address is saved
GBR-11
A customer with an overdue bill cannot create a new booking until he or she pays. The visits already booked go on. The bookings of this customer are shown in Staff's Needs action list, and the overdue bills in Unpaid Bookings, so that Staff call the customer.
Creating a booking, monitoring visits, Unpaid Bookings
There is no deposit, so this is how Paw World limits unpaid bills.
System, when a booking is started
GBR-12
A visit is offered to one pet sitter at a time. Pet sitters who are outside their working hours or service areas, or are busy (travel time included), are left out; the rest can get the offer. The customer can ask only for a pet sitter who has cared for his or her pets before, never for any pet sitter by name. If that pet sitter can take the visit, he or she gets the first offer, whatever the rating or the number of visits that day. If not, or if he or she declines or does not answer in time, the other pet sitters who can take the visit are ranked by three things: whether they have cared for the customer's pets before, the rating from customers, and how few visits they have that day. This document does not fix how the three are weighed; the ranking algorithm sets it. The automatic offers never offer a visit again to a pet sitter who declined it or did not answer in time, also after the visit is sent back (a withdrawal or a change of time). Staff see the pet sitters in the same order when they assign a visit by hand. A pet sitter who declines an offer gives a reason. When several pet sitters have the same ranking score at the place being offered (the exact score, not rounded), the visit is offered to all of them at once, with no limit on their number; the first to accept gets the visit (GBR-13). The answer time is 2 hours, or 30 minutes if the visit starts within 24 hours (exactly 24 hours counts as within 24 hours), and it never ends after the visit starts.
Offering and accepting visits
Fills most visits without Staff and never gives a pet sitter two visits at once.
System (JOB-02)
GBR-13
If a pet sitter accepts an offer at the same moment as Staff assign the visit by hand, or two Staff members assign the same visit at the same moment, the first one saved keeps the visit. The later save is refused: that person is told the visit is taken and sees the visit as it is now.
Accepting an offer, assigning a pet sitter
A visit can have only one pet sitter.
System, when the visit is saved
GBR-14
A visit goes to Staff's Needs action list when nobody is left to offer it to, when it has no pet sitter 24 hours before it starts, or when its pet sitter has not checked in 30 minutes after the start. A visit still without a pet sitter 12 hours before it starts is cancelled by Staff without any charge.
Visit assignment
Staff step in only when the automatic offers fail, and the customer is told in time.
System (JOB-02) and Staff
GBR-15
Cancelling a visit 24 hours or more before it starts is free. A later cancellation adds 30% of that visit's price before any discount to the bill. A visit cancelled by Staff because no pet sitter is free, or because the pet sitter has not arrived 30 minutes after the start, costs nothing.
Cancelling a booking or a visit
Pet sitters lose work when visits are cancelled late; the customer pays nothing for Paw World's own failures.
System, when the cancellation is saved
GBR-16
Check-in is allowed from 30 minutes before the visit starts, only for the assigned pet sitter, with a photo. Check-out needs a photo and every required checklist item done or skipped with a reason. A visit not checked in 15 minutes after its start is shown in red to Staff.
Check-in, check-out
Gives the customer proof that the visit happened.
System, at check-in and check-out
GBR-17
When a booking ends (its last visit is finished or cancelled), the bill is the visits done, with the discount for many visits worked out again on the number of visits done (a visit that Staff cancelled at no charge still counts for the discount) and the customer's tier discount (GBR-42), plus late cancellation fees, minus any amount an Admin approved for an incident or a complaint. It is due 48 hours after it is final. While a complaint or an incident about the booking is open the bill waits; a bill of 0 closes the booking.
End of a booking, payment, complaints
The customer pays only for care received, after the complaint is settled.
System, when the booking ends
GBR-18
A payment is recorded only once, even if payOS sends the result again. A payment that fails before the due time can be made again. A booking is marked overdue only after payOS confirms that the bill is not paid. Nothing is collected automatically from an overdue customer: Staff follow the bill in Unpaid Bookings (UC-50) and Paw World collects the money outside PWC.
payOS webhook, overdue job
No double payment and no customer blocked by a late payment result.
System (payOS webhook, JOB-03)
GBR-19
A change request that Staff have not handled 6 hours before the old time or the new time, whichever comes first, expires, and the visits keep their old time. For a request that moves several visits, these are the earliest old time and the earliest new time of its visits. When a visit of a waiting request is cancelled, it leaves the request; the request stays waiting for its other visits, with its expiry worked out again from them, and is closed when no visit is left.
Approving booking changes
The pet sitter and the customer know for sure when the visit will happen.
System (JOB-02)
GBR-20
The price of a visit is the base price of the service plus the surcharges that apply (pet size, extra pets, travel). The total of a booking is the sum of its visits minus the discount for many visits in the price list (for example 5% from 7 visits and 10% from 20 visits) and the customer's tier discount (GBR-42), worked out again when the bill is made (GBR-17). A booking keeps the prices of the day it was made.
Creating a booking, the bill
The customer sees a fixed price before booking.
System, when the total is worked out
GBR-21
A pet sitter can withdraw from an accepted visit alone until 12 hours before it starts; the visit is then offered again with a handover note. A later withdrawal goes to Staff's Needs action list. A day off that touches a visit starting within 12 hours cannot be asked; the pet sitter withdraws from that visit instead.
Withdrawing from a visit, days off
Leaves time to find a replacement.
System, when the request is saved
GBR-22
A customer can ask to move a booking or a visit at least 12 hours before the old time, to a new time at least 24 hours away, and Staff approve or reject the request. Only the date and time change, so the price stays the same. Each visit can be moved only once, so that a visit cannot be moved far away and then cancelled for free to avoid the late cancellation fee (GBR-15). The new time must also follow the booking rules (GBR-09, GBR-33). PWC checks these rules when the customer sends the request; when Staff approve it, PWC checks again GBR-09 and GBR-33, which can change in the meantime. A visit that is already in a waiting request cannot be in another request.
Requesting a booking change
Leaves time for Staff to decide and to find a pet sitter.
System, when the request is sent
GBR-23
Care instructions can be changed until 2 hours before a visit; later changes apply from the next visit. When they change after a pet sitter is assigned, the pet sitter must confirm the new version before check-in.
Editing care instructions, check-in
The pet sitter always works from instructions he or she has read.
System, when instructions are saved and at check-in
GBR-24
A replacement pet sitter must confirm the handover at least 1 hour before the visit, otherwise the visit goes to Needs action. A visit not checked out 2 hours after its end goes to Needs action; Staff close it with a reason. If the visit has a check-in photo it counts as done; if not, it is cancelled at no charge and the pet sitter earns nothing.
Handover, check-out
Staff learn in time about visits that may go wrong.
System (JOB-02) and Staff
GBR-25
There is at most one open pet sitter application (Pending, Under review or Interview) for one phone number, one email and one ID card number. A rejected person can apply again 30 days after the rejection.
Sending a pet sitter application
The Admin reviews each person once at a time, and nobody needs an account to apply.
System, when the application is sent
GBR-26
A customer can complain about a finished visit within 7 days after it ends (for a visit closed by Staff, within 7 days after it was closed, from Booking Details), or about a whole booking within 7 days after the booking ends (its last visit is finished or cancelled); a booking whose visits were all cancelled can be complained about only when its bill is above 0 (late cancellation fees). There is one open complaint per visit and one open complaint about the whole booking. The customer can disagree once with the result of a complaint or an incident, within 3 days (72 hours) after receiving it; an Admin then makes the final decision. Without an answer in 72 hours the result is final, a bill that was waiting stops waiting and its 48 hours start from that moment (JOB-03); a booking already Overdue keeps its due time. Pet sitters have no such step in PWC. No deadline stops the handling of a case; cases open more than 3 days are shown apart on the Admin Dashboard.
Complaints, results of incidents
Feedback stays close to the visit, and every complaint has a final answer.
System, when a complaint is sent or the result is received
GBR-27
A customer registers with a full name and one email or phone number, confirmed with a code, and is signed in at once. The other one can be added later and is saved only after its code is entered. A user signs in only with an email or phone number of his or her own account. Accounts made from a form (Staff, approved pet sitters) get both values, and each one is confirmed the first time the user enters a code sent to it.
Registration, sign-in, Change Email or Phone Number
One confirmed contact is enough to prove the person; the second one is optional.
System, at registration and sign-in
GBR-28
A pet sitter account is created only when an Admin approves an application, from the phone number and email in it. Before that the person has no account. The new pet sitter gets no offers until he or she has service areas and either working hours (part-time) or shifts (full-time, GBR-45).
Approving a pet sitter application
No account is kept for people who are not approved.
System, when the application is approved
GBR-29
When the pet sitter has not checked in 15 minutes after the start, the visit is shown in red, Staff are told in the app (JOB-07) and call the pet sitter. At 30 minutes the visit goes to Needs action, and Staff send another pet sitter or cancel the visit at no charge. The price of a late visit is not lowered automatically; the customer can report an incident or complain. A check-in more than 5 minutes after the start counts as one late check-in in the pet sitter's count for the last 30 days; a visit with no check-in is not counted there.
Monitoring today's visits, cancelling a visit
The customer is never left waiting without anyone acting.
System (JOB-02, JOB-07) and Staff
GBR-30
Staff propose any amount to pay back for an incident or a complaint, with the reason and evidence; an Admin approves, lowers or rejects every amount. The total paid back for a booking is at most its bill; before the bill is made, at most the expected bill: the bill worked out as in GBR-17 on the visits not cancelled (done or still to come), with the discounts that apply and the late cancellation fees so far, before the amounts approved are taken off. A new amount, together with the amounts the Admin has already approved for the booking, cannot be more than this limit. Before payment the amount is taken off the bill, also when the booking is Overdue: it stays Overdue and its due time does not move; after payment it is recorded as a refund, which Paw World's accounting pays outside PWC. Staff cannot waive the price of a visit themselves: not charging a visit is an amount proposed by Staff and approved by an Admin. Larger damage is handled by Paw World outside PWC.
Resolving incidents, handling complaints, refunds
Every amount of money that leaves Paw World is checked by the manager.
System and Admin
GBR-31
A pet sitter has a contract that an Admin records: full-time (monthly pay) or part-time (pay for each visit). A part-time pet sitter earns the visit pay of the contract for each finished visit, or for each groomed pet at the shop; a visit closed by Staff with a check-in photo counts. A cancelled visit, or a visit closed without a check-in photo, earns nothing. The monthly pay of a full-time pet sitter does not depend on the number of visits. A tip paid through PWC is not part of the pay: it belongs to the pet sitters who did the work, shared equally among them. Late cancellation fees go to Paw World, and amounts paid back to customers are not taken off the earnings in PWC. Each finished visit or groomed pet also earns a difficulty bonus (GBR-43). PWC makes the statement of each pay period (GBR-44); Paw World pays it outside PWC.
View Sitter Earnings, Manage Pet Sitter Contracts
Pet sitters see what they have earned without PWC handling salaries.
System, when a visit is finished
GBR-32
When Staff assign a visit by hand, a pet sitter with an overlapping visit (travel time included) cannot be chosen. Outside the pet sitter's working hours or service areas the system shows a warning, and Staff assign only after confirming that they called the pet sitter and he or she agreed.
Assigning a pet sitter by hand
Staff can solve urgent cases without giving anyone two visits at once.
System and Staff
GBR-33
A customer who has not paid any bill yet can make a booking that covers at most 7 days and can have only one unpaid booking at a time. Two bookings of the same pet cannot overlap in time, for any customer.
Creating a booking
There is no deposit, so a new customer starts with a small exposure; one pet cannot be in two bookings at once.
System, when a booking is started
GBR-34
A customer must accept the responsibility policy (what Paw World is responsible for, what it is not, and what the customer must do) before confirming a booking. The policy is public on Service Areas and Policies. Money paid back in PWC is at most the bill (GBR-30).
Creating a booking, Service Areas and Policies
The customer knows the terms before the first visit, and the limit of what PWC pays back is clear.
System, when a booking is confirmed
GBR-35
Placing an order holds the quantity in stock, whether the order is online or at the counter. Only the quantity available (stock on hand minus the quantity held by unfinished orders) can be ordered. The stock on hand goes down when the goods leave the shop: handed to the carrier, picked up at the shop, or sold at the counter. Cancelling an order ends its hold. A returned parcel adds back only the quantity that can be sold again, and an order can be confirmed as returned only once.
Placing an order, Sell at Counter, Prepare and Ship Order, Hand Over Pick-up Order, Confirm Returned Order
One stock figure for every order, and no goods sold twice or counted twice.
System, when an order is placed, cancelled or its goods leave the shop
GBR-36
A delivery order is paid in advance through payOS or in cash on delivery; a pick-up order is paid in advance or at the shop on pick-up. A prepaid order not paid by the payment deadline, or a pick-up order not collected by the pick-up deadline, is cancelled automatically and its hold ends. The payment deadline counts from the time the order is placed; the pick-up deadline counts from the time Sale Staff mark the order ready. A customer who has refused goods more often than the refusal limit set by the Admin can only pay in advance. A pick-up order is handed over only when it is paid in full. A refusal counts against the customer only when he or she refused the parcel or did not collect a pick-up order by the deadline; a prepaid order not paid in time does not count.
Place Product Order, Update Delivery Status, Confirm Returned Order
Limits the goods held for customers who do not take them, without unfair blame when Paw World or the carrier failed.
System, when an order is placed, and JOB-04 when a deadline passes; Sale Staff
GBR-37
A prepaid order that is cancelled or returned is marked for a refund, which Paw World's accounting pays outside PWC. When Paw World made the mistake or the carrier could not deliver, the whole amount is refunded, including the shipping fee. When the customer refuses the goods without a reason, the price of the goods is refunded, and the shipping fee is kept only if the policy told the customer so before ordering.
Process Refund, Confirm Returned Order
The refund follows who caused the failure.
System and Sale Staff
GBR-38
At the counter the Sale Staff member looks up the product first and then creates the order, which holds the stock (GBR-35). A paid order (cash or VietQR) keeps the hold until the Sale Staff hand over the goods and complete the sale, which ends the hold and takes the goods off the stock on hand; a cancelled order only ends the hold. When the customer has not paid in full, the customer can pay again or change the way to pay before the order is cancelled.
Sell at Counter
The shelf and the system show the same stock, and a customer is not charged twice or left with a lost order.
System, when the order is created, paid or cancelled
GBR-39
Every delivery from a supplier gets a goods receipt, even when everything is rejected. The stock goes up only by the accepted quantity. Rejected goods are reported to the Admin with the quantity and the reason, and are returned to the supplier outside PWC. A make-up delivery is a new receipt. A product must be in the catalogue before its goods can be received.
Record Goods Receipt, Manage Products
The stock matches what was really received.
System, when a goods receipt is saved
GBR-40
A grooming appointment is saved only when a pet sitter with grooming skills is free on a shop shift in that slot, and it holds its place in the slot. Two appointments of the same pet cannot overlap in time. It has no deposit and is paid at the shop when the pet is handed back, in cash or by VietQR. The price shown when booking is the final price of each pet; if it changes before the customer confirms, the customer sees the new price first. It changes only when the pet is different from the customer's answers, only by a surcharge in the price list, and only if the customer agrees; if the customer does not agree, the pet is cancelled at no charge. A pet that is too dangerous to go on is stopped by the pet sitter, who notes the reason in the pet profile. A very risky pet can only be booked by calling the shop. Pets are handed back only when the amount is paid in full; if no pet is ready, the appointment is closed with nothing to pay. A customer who does not come is recorded as a no-show.
Book Grooming Appointment, Check In Pet at Shop, Hand Back Pet and Take Payment
The customer knows the price before coming, and the pet sitter and the pet stay safe.
System and Staff, at check-in and hand-back
GBR-41
During a dog walk, from check-in to check-out, the customer of the walk sees where the dog is. PWC does not keep the route or the past locations after check-out. Home visits and vet trips show no location.
View Dog Walk Location, Check In, Check Out
The customer is at ease during the walk, and no trace of a person's movements is stored.
System, from check-in to check-out
GBR-42
Each customer has a tier: Member, Silver or Gold. PWC sets it from what the customer paid in the last 12 months for services and products, counter sales included when the customer gave a phone number or an email. Each tier gives a discount on the price of services and products, applied automatically; at the counter only when the customer is found by phone number or email. A customer with an overdue bill, or who may only pay in advance, keeps the tier but gets no tier discount while blocked. There are no points, coupons or vouchers. Until the settings screen is built, the tier limits and discounts are fixed values in the system.
Creating a booking, Book Grooming Appointment, Place Product Order, Sell at Counter
Loyal customers get a simple, automatic benefit.
System (JOB-05 every night) and when a price is worked out
GBR-43
PWC sets the difficulty level of a pet from what the customer declares about it and the assessment criteria. This document does not fix the criteria and levels; the difficulty algorithm sets them. Each finished visit, and each groomed pet at the shop, earns the pet sitter who did it a bonus that depends on this level. At the shop the level is the one confirmed at check-in. A pet found harder during the work keeps its level for this booking; the pet sitter notes it in the pet profile, and the level goes up from the next booking. A visit that earns nothing (GBR-31) earns no bonus. Until the settings screen is built, the bonus amounts are fixed values in the system.
View Sitter Earnings, Review Pay Statement, Update Grooming Status, Check In Pet at Shop
Harder pets are paid more, so pet sitters do not avoid them.
System, when a visit or a pet is finished
GBR-44
At the end of each pay period (a week for part-time, a month for full-time) PWC makes a statement for each pet sitter: the contract pay or the visit pay, the difficulty bonuses and the tips paid through PWC. The pet sitter has 3 days after it is sent to accept it or to complain with a reason; with no complaint it is closed after 3 days. The Admin answers a complaint by correcting the statement with a reason or by explaining it; PWC keeps the history, tells the pet sitter and closes the statement, with no second review. The Admin pays outside PWC and marks the statement paid. A mistake found after payment is corrected in the next period.
Review Pay Statement, Close Pay Statement, View Sitter Earnings
Every pay period has one agreed amount, and pet sitters can question it in time.
System and Admin
GBR-45
A full-time pet sitter works the fixed shifts of the contract, which an Admin enters in the shift schedule; he or she does not set working hours and is offered visits only inside the shifts. Pet sitters who bathe and groom pets at the shop are full-time and work shop shifts. A part-time pet sitter sets weekly working hours. Every pet sitter chooses service areas, which an Admin can change. A change of hours, shifts or service areas that touches accepted visits flags those visits to Staff.
Manage Shift Schedule, Set Working Hours, Offering and accepting visits
Full-time pet sitters are used inside the hours they are paid for.
System, when hours, shifts or areas are saved
GBR-46
PWC tells users about bookings, offers, payments, pet sitter changes, incidents, product orders and shop appointments with in-app notifications, and tells the Admin about each line of a goods receipt with rejected goods (code, quantity rejected and reason). Email is used only for one-time codes, account messages, bills and payment reminders, and SMS only for one-time codes. A customer with only a phone number gets no email: he or she sees bills, due dates and order changes in the app.
View Notifications, all functions that change a booking, an offer, a payment, an order or an appointment, Record Goods Receipt
Users learn about changes in one place, and messages outside the app stay few.
System, when the change is saved
GBR-47
The customer and the pet sitter of a visit get one in-app reminder 2 hours before it starts. A customer gets one payment reminder 24 hours before the bill is due, in the app and by email when he or she has an email. Staff are told when a pet sitter has not checked in 15 minutes after the start (GBR-29).
Visits, payment, monitoring today's visits
Fewer missed visits and late payments.
System (JOB-01, JOB-03, JOB-07)
GBR-48
A customer can rate a finished visit once, within 7 days after it ends, and add a comment. Pet sitters do not rate customers. The rating shows on the pet sitter's profile and in the Sitter List.
Rate Visit, View Sitter Profile, View Sitter List
Customers' views are recorded while the visit is fresh.
System, when a rating is sent
GBR-49
A customer can report a faulty or wrong product of an order, with photos, within the time set by the Admin. Products are not taken back because the customer changed his or her mind, and are exchanged only for the same product. The Sale Staff member checks the claim and the Admin approves an exchange, approves a refund or rejects the claim. New goods for an exchange leave the stock on hand; a refund is paid by Paw World's accounting outside PWC. Faulty goods are not added back to the stock on hand, so no stock adjustment is made for them.
Report Faulty Product, Handle Faulty Product Claim
Customers are covered for faulty goods, and the stock stays right.
System, Sale Staff and Admin
GBR-50
Outside orders, goods receipts and the new goods of a faulty product exchange (GBR-49), only an Admin changes the stock on hand, with a stock adjustment: an increase or a decrease with a required reason (damaged, lost, internal use, wrong goods receipt). Goods receipts and adjustments are never edited; a mistake is corrected with a new adjustment. The stock on hand cannot go below zero. A decrease cannot be more than the quantity available (stock on hand minus the quantity held by unfinished orders), checked again when the adjustment is saved; an increase has no such limit. An adjustment does not release held goods or change any order. PWC has no stock count.
Adjust Stock, Record Goods Receipt
Every change of the stock has a reason and a record.
System, when an adjustment is saved

None for this version.
